<script>(()=>{const CITY_NAME='RP City';const w=CITY_NAME.split(' ');document.getElementById('introName').innerHTML=w.slice(0,-1).join(' ')+(w.length>1?' ':'')+'<em>'+w[w.length-1]+'</em>';
const el=document.getElementById('intro');let gone=0,started=0,stopped=0,ac=null,master=null,lp=null,loopT=0,introT=0;
const LEN=13.4;
const bye=()=>{if(gone)return;gone=1;clearTimeout(introT);el.classList.add('out');setTimeout(()=>el.remove(),900)};
function stopMusic(){if(stopped)return;stopped=1;clearInterval(loopT);try{if(master){const t=ac.currentTime;master.gain.cancelScheduledValues(t);master.gain.setValueAtTime(master.gain.value,t);master.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0,t+1.5)}setTimeout(()=>{try{ac&&ac.close()}catch(e){}},1800)}catch(e){}}
function note(f,t,d,type,vol,dest){const o=ac.createOscillator(),g=ac.createGain();o.type=type;o.frequency.value=f;g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol,t+0.02);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+d);o.connect(g);g.connect(dest);o.start(t);o.stop(t+d+0.05)}
function pad(f,t,d,dest){[-4,4].forEach(det=>{const o=ac.createOscillator(),g=ac.createGain();o.type='sine';o.frequency.value=f;o.detune.value=det;g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,t);g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.05,t+0.9);g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+d);o.connect(g);g.connect(dest);o.start(t);o.stop(t+d+0.05)})}
function theme(t0){
  // música neutra e suave (Dó maior, andamento lento, sem batida)
  const beat=0.8333,bar=beat*4;
  const chords=[[130.81,196,261.63,329.63],[110,164.81,220,261.63],[87.31,174.61,220,261.63],[98,196,246.94,293.66]];
  const mel=[[659.25,0,587.33,523.25],[523.25,0,440,0],[440,523.25,0,440],[493.88,0,587.33,0]];
  for(let b=0;b<4;b++){
    const t=t0+b*bar;
    chords[b].forEach(f=>pad(f,t,bar+0.8,lp));
    mel[b].forEach((f,i)=>{if(f)note(f,t+i*beat,beat*1.8,'sine',0.06,lp)});
  }
}
function startMusic(){
  if(started)return;
  const AC=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!AC)return;
  try{
    ac=new AC();master=ac.createGain();master.gain.value=0.95;
    lp=ac.createBiquadFilter();lp.type='lowpass';lp.frequency.value=1400;
    const dl=ac.createDelay();dl.delayTime.value=0.3;const fb=ac.createGain();fb.gain.value=0.3;dl.connect(fb);fb.connect(dl);
    lp.connect(master);lp.connect(dl);dl.connect(master);master.connect(ac.destination);
    started=1;
    const go=()=>{if(stopped)return;theme(ac.currentTime+0.1);loopT=setInterval(()=>{if(!stopped)theme(ac.currentTime+0.1)},LEN*1000)};
    if(ac.state==='running')go();else ac.resume().then(go).catch(()=>{});
    // a abertura dura enquanto a música toca; depois entra a tela do jogo, e a música continua até clicar em DIRIGIR
    introT=setTimeout(bye,LEN*1000);
  }catch(e){}
}
// tenta tocar ao abrir
startMusic();
// se o navegador bloqueou o som, o primeiro toque/tecla liga a música
const unlock=()=>{if(!started||(ac&&ac.state!=='running')){try{ac&&ac.resume()}catch(e){}}if(!started)startMusic();};
['pointerdown','touchstart','touchend','click','keydown'].forEach(ev=>addEventListener(ev,unlock,{passive:true}));
el.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(started)bye()});el.addEventListener('touchend',()=>{if(started)bye()});
addEventListener('keydown',()=>{if(started)bye()});

document.addEventListener('touchend',e=>{if(e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#go'))stopMusic()},true);
})();</script>

function makeFacade(style){
  const pat=[];for(let i=0;i<64;i++)pat.push(Math.random()<0.5);
  const cells=[];
  if(style===0){for(let y=0;y<4;y++)for(let x=0;x<4;x++)cells.push([x*32+6,y*32+5,20,21])}
  else if(style===1){for(let y=0;y<4;y++)for(let x=0;x<2;x++)cells.push([x*60+7,y*32+7,50,17])}
  else if(style===2){for(let y=0;y<3;y++)for(let x=0;x<6;x++)cells.push([x*21+5,y*42+5,11,32])}
  else if(style===3){for(let y=0;y<8;y++)for(let x=0;x<8;x++)cells.push([x*16+2,y*16+2,12,12])}
  else{for(let y=0;y<4;y++)for(let x=0;x<3;x++)cells.push([x*42+9,y*32+4,24,18])}
  const mk=lit=>{
    const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=256;const g=c.getContext('2d');g.scale(2,2);
    g.fillStyle=lit?'#000':(style===3?'#7f8da3':'#b4bccb');g.fillRect(0,0,128,128);
    if(!lit){
      if(style===2){g.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.10)';for(let y=0;y<16;y++)g.fillRect(0,y*8+7,128,1)}
      else{g.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.12)';for(let y=0;y<4;y++)g.fillRect(0,y*32+29,128,3)}
      if(style===4){g.fillStyle='#4a5160';for(let y=0;y<4;y++)g.fillRect(0,y*32+24,128,2)}
    }
    cells.forEach((cl,i)=>{
      const px=cl[0],py=cl[1],w=cl[2],h=cl[3],on=pat[i%64];
      if(lit){if(on){const gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,py,0,py+h);gr.addColorStop(0,'#ffe9b0');gr.addColorStop(1,'#ffb55a');g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(px,py,w,h)}}
      else{
        g.fillStyle='#5c6577';g.fillRect(px-1,py-1,w+2,h+2);
        const gr=g.createLinearGradient(px,py,px+w,py+h);
        if(on){gr.addColorStop(0,'#f3e2b0');gr.addColorStop(1,'#caa766')}else{gr.addColorStop(0,'#3a5878');gr.addColorStop(0.5,'#1b2a40');gr.addColorStop(1,'#101a2a')}
        g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(px,py,w,h);
        if(!on){g.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.18)';g.fillRect(px+2,py+2,Math.max(2,w*.25),h-4)}
      }
    });
    const t=new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);t.wrapS=t.wrapT=THREE.RepeatWrapping;t.anisotropy=ANISO;return t;
  };
  return {map:mk(false),em:mk(true)};
}

function buildingGeo(w,h,d){
  const g=new THREE.BoxGeometry(w,h,d);const uv=g.attributes.uv;
  for(let f=0;f<6;f++){
    let fu=1,fv=1;
    if(f<2){fu=d/24;fv=h/24}else if(f>3){fu=w/24;fv=h/24}
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const k=f*4+i;uv.setXY(k,uv.getX(k)*fu,uv.getY(k)*fv)}
  }
  return g;
}

const sandTex=(()=>{const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=128;const g=c.getContext('2d');g.fillStyle='rgb(196,172,128)';g.fillRect(0,0,128,128);for(let i=0;i<14;i++){g.fillStyle='rgba('+(150+Math.random()*50|0)+','+(125+Math.random()*40|0)+',90,.18)';g.beginPath();g.ellipse(Math.random()*128,Math.random()*128,10+Math.random()*30,5+Math.random()*14,Math.random()*3,0,7);g.fill()}
  for(let i=0;i<2600;i++){const v=120+Math.random()*100|0;g.fillStyle='rgba('+v+','+(v-14)+','+(v-48)+',.5)';g.fillRect(Math.random()*128,Math.random()*128,1+Math.random()*1.5,1+Math.random()*1.5)}
  for(let i=0;i<10;i++){g.strokeStyle='rgba(190,180,160,.25)';g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,i*13+Math.random()*8);g.bezierCurveTo(40,i*13-6,90,i*13+10,128,i*13+Math.random()*8);g.stroke()}

const sandMat=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0xab915e,map:sandTex,emissive:0x120e07});
const seaMat=new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
  uniforms:{time:{value:0},dayF:{value:1},chop:{value:1},cloud:{value:0},sunI:{value:1},fogN:{value:100},fogF:{value:1000},
    sunDir:{value:new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0)},sky:{value:new THREE.Color(0x6ab0ff)},fogC:{value:new THREE.Color(0x8ec5ff)},shore:{value:new THREE.Vector3()}},
  vertexShader:'varying vec3 vW;void main(){vec4 w=modelMatrix*vec4(position,1.);vW=w.xyz;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*viewMatrix*w;}',
  fragmentShader:[
  'uniform float time;uniform float dayF;uniform float chop;uniform float cloud;uniform float sunI;uniform float fogN;uniform float fogF;',
  'uniform vec3 sunDir;uniform vec3 sky;uniform vec3 fogC;uniform vec3 shore;varying vec3 vW;',
  'void main(){',
  ' vec2 p=vW.xz;vec2 g=vec2(0.);',
  ' vec2 d1=normalize(vec2(1.,.6));vec2 d2=normalize(vec2(-.5,1.));vec2 d3=normalize(vec2(.8,-.9));vec2 d4=normalize(vec2(-1.,-.3));',
  ' g+=d1*cos(dot(p,d1)*.08+time*.9)*.08;g+=d2*cos(dot(p,d2)*.17+time*1.3)*.085;g+=d3*cos(dot(p,d3)*.35+time*1.9)*.09;g+=d4*cos(dot(p,d4)*.9+time*2.8)*.07;',
  ' g*=chop;vec3 n=normalize(vec3(-g.x,1.,-g.y));',
  ' vec3 V=normalize(cameraPosition-vW);',
  ' float fres=.12+.88*pow(1.-max(dot(n,V),0.),3.);',
  ' float d=max(abs(vW.x-shore.x),abs(vW.z-shore.x))-shore.y;',
  ' vec3 shallow=vec3(.22,.78,.8);vec3 deep=vec3(.06,.36,.62);',
  ' vec3 base=mix(shallow,deep,smoothstep(0.,160.,d))*(.3+.9*dayF);',
  ' vec3 col=mix(base,sky,fres*.7);float cr=smoothstep(.1,.26,length(g));col+=vec3(.85,.95,1.)*cr*.22*dayF;col+=vec3(.05,.12,.14)*sin(dot(p,vec2(.6,.8))*.5+time*1.4)*dayF;',
  ' vec3 R=reflect(-normalize(sunDir),n);float spec=pow(max(dot(R,V),0.),90.)*sunI*(1.-cloud*.85);',
  ' col+=vec3(1.,.93,.78)*spec*1.0;',
  ' float fo=(1.-smoothstep(0.,12.,d))*(.5+.5*sin(d*.8-time*1.6+sin(p.x*.2+p.y*.17)*2.));',
  ' col=mix(col,vec3(.95,.98,1.)*(.4+.6*dayF),clamp(fo*.8,0.,1.));',
  ' col=mix(col,fogC,smoothstep(fogN,fogF,length(cameraPosition-vW)));',
  ' gl_FragColor=vec4(col,1.);',
  ' #include <tonemapping_fragment>',
  ' #include <encodings_fragment>',
  '}'].join('\n')
});
const citySpan=CITY_EDGE_MAX-CITY_EDGE_MIN,outerMin=CITY_EDGE_MIN-OCEAN_BUFFER,outerMax=CITY_EDGE_MAX+OCEAN_BUFFER;
function coastStrip(mat,x,z,w,d,y){const gm=new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w,d);if(mat===sandMat){const uv=gm.attributes.uv;for(let i=0;i<uv.count;i++)uv.setXY(i,uv.getX(i)*w/7,uv.getY(i)*d/7)}const m=new THREE.Mesh(gm,mat);m.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;m.position.set(x,y,z);m.receiveShadow=true;scene.add(m);return m}
// Praia de areia contínua nos quatro lados do perímetro urbano.
const sandOuterMin=CITY_EDGE_MIN-BEACH_WIDTH,sandOuterMax=CITY_EDGE_MAX+BEACH_WIDTH;
seaMat.uniforms.shore.value.set(cx,(sandOuterMax-sandOuterMin)/2,0);
coastStrip(sandMat,(CITY_EDGE_MIN+sandOuterMin)/2,cx,BEACH_WIDTH,citySpan+BEACH_WIDTH*2,-0.035);
coastStrip(sandMat,(CITY_EDGE_MAX+sandOuterMax)/2,cx,BEACH_WIDTH,citySpan+BEACH_WIDTH*2,-0.035);
coastStrip(sandMat,cx,(CITY_EDGE_MIN+sandOuterMin)/2,citySpan,BEACH_WIDTH,-0.035);
coastStrip(sandMat,cx,(CITY_EDGE_MAX+sandOuterMax)/2,citySpan,BEACH_WIDTH,-0.035);
// Água azul ocupa toda a faixa entre a praia e o limite externo do mapa.
const seaInnerMin=sandOuterMin,seaInnerMax=sandOuterMax;
coastStrip(seaMat,(outerMin+seaInnerMin)/2,cx,seaInnerMin-outerMin,outerMax-outerMin,-0.022);
coastStrip(seaMat,(outerMax+seaInnerMax)/2,cx,outerMax-seaInnerMax,outerMax-outerMin,-0.022);
coastStrip(seaMat,cx,(outerMin+seaInnerMin)/2,citySpan,seaInnerMin-outerMin,-0.022);
coastStrip(seaMat,cx,(outerMax+seaInnerMax)/2,citySpan,outerMax-seaInnerMax,-0.022);

function buildPlaza(bx,bz,i,j,center){
  let sd=(i*73856093^j*19349663)>>>0;const R=()=>{sd=(sd*1664525+1013904223)>>>0;return sd/4294967296};
  const sols=[],bens=[];PLZ.sol.set(i*NB+j,sols);PLZ.bench.set(i*NB+j,bens);
  const sol=(x,z,hx,hz)=>sols.push({x:bx+x,z:bz+z,hx,hz});
  const box=(w,h,d,m,x,y,z,ry)=>{const o=new THREE.Mesh(PLZ.boxG,m);o.scale.set(w,h,d);o.position.set(bx+x,y,bz+z);if(ry)o.rotation.y=ry;MERGE.push(o);return o};
  const cyl=(r,h,m,x,y,z)=>{const o=new THREE.Mesh(PLZ.cylG,m);o.scale.set(r,h,r);o.position.set(bx+x,y,bz+z);MERGE.push(o)};
  const palm=(x,z,h)=>{
    const t=new THREE.Mesh(PLZ.trunkG,PLZ.trunk);t.scale.set(1,h,1);t.position.set(bx+x,h/2+.1,bz+z);MERGE.push(t);sol(x,z,.5,.5);
    const n=7,a0=R()*6.28;
    for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const f=new THREE.Mesh(PLZ.frondG,PLZ.frond);f.rotation.order='YXZ';f.rotation.set(.45+R()*.25,a0+k*(Math.PI*2/n),0);f.position.set(bx+x,h+.1,bz+z);MERGE.push(f)}
  };
  const bench=(x,z,ry)=>{const vert=Math.abs(Math.sin(ry||0))>.5;sol(x,z,vert?.7:2.1,vert?2.1:.7);bens.push({x:bx+x,z:bz+z,h:ry||0});box(4,.18,1.1,PLZ.wood,x,.62,z,ry);box(4,.7,.14,PLZ.wood,x+Math.sin(ry||0)*-.5,.98,z+Math.cos(ry||0)*-.5,ry);box(.22,.55,1,PLZ.pole,x+Math.cos(ry||0)*-1.7,.35,z+Math.sin(ry||0)*1.7,ry);box(.22,.55,1,PLZ.pole,x+Math.cos(ry||0)*1.7,.35,z+Math.sin(ry||0)*-1.7,ry)};
  const lamp=(x,z)=>{sol(x,z,.3,.3);cyl(.12,6,PLZ.pole,x,3.1,z);box(.7,.35,.7,PLZ.lamp,x,6.3,z)};
  const arch=(x,z,ry,col)=>{ // pórtico amarelo vazado (pergolado), como na praça de referência
    const c=Math.cos(ry),s=Math.sin(ry),P=(lx,lz)=>[x+lx*c+lz*s,z-lx*s+lz*c];
    for(const sx of[-3.2,3.2]){const p=P(sx,0);sol(p[0],p[1],.35,.35);box(.5,4.2,.5,col,p[0],2.2,p[1],ry)}
    const m=P(0,0);box(7.4,.5,.6,col,m[0],4.4,m[1],ry);
    const r1=P(0,-1.3),r2=P(0,1.3);box(7.4,.25,.25,col,r1[0],4.6,r1[1],ry);box(7.4,.25,.25,col,r2[0],4.6,r2[1],ry);
  };
  // piso de pedra clara cobrindo o quarteirão
  box(HALF*2-1.5,.1,HALF*2-1.5,PLZ.pave,0,.1,0);
  // canteiros de grama nos cantos
  for(const sx of[-1,1])for(const sz of[-1,1]){box(9,.16,9,PLZ.grass,sx*21,.15,sz*21);box(9.4,.1,9.4,PLZ.stepD,sx*21,.12,sz*21)}
  // faixas de grama laterais
  box(2.2,.16,22,PLZ.grassD,-26,.15,0);box(2.2,.16,22,PLZ.grassD,26,.15,0);
  // palmeiras no perímetro e nos canteiros
  for(const [x,z] of[[-27,-27],[27,-27],[-27,27],[27,27],[-27,-9],[27,-9],[-27,9],[27,9],[-9,-27],[9,-27],[-9,27],[9,27]])palm(x+(R()-.5)*1.2,z+(R()-.5)*1.2,7+R()*3);
  for(const sx of[-1,1])for(const sz of[-1,1])palm(sx*21,sz*21,8+R()*3);
  // postes de luz
  for(const [x,z] of[[-15,-15],[15,-15],[-15,15],[15,15]])lamp(x,z);
  // bancos
  bench(-12,0,Math.PI/2);bench(12,0,-Math.PI/2);bench(0,-13,0);bench(0,13,Math.PI);
  { // LOJA VIP: barraca dourada com vendedor parado
    const vx=-10,vz=9;VENDORS.push({x:bx+vx,z:bz+vz});
    sol(vx,vz+2.6,2.3,.7);
    box(5.6,.14,4.8,PLZ.vGold,vx,.17,vz+1.4);
    box(4.2,1.1,1.1,PLZ.vGold,vx,.75,vz+2.6);box(4.4,.1,1.3,PLZ.dark,vx,1.35,vz+2.6);
    for(const sx of[-2.3,2.3])box(.2,3.8,.2,PLZ.pole,vx+sx,2,vz+3.3);
    box(5.6,.18,3.6,PLZ.red,vx,3.95,vz+1.8);
    box(4.2,.9,.12,PLZ.vSign,vx,4.65,vz+3.55);
    box(.28,1,.3,PLZ.dark,vx-.2,.65,vz);box(.28,1,.3,PLZ.dark,vx+.2,.65,vz);
    box(.9,1.1,.5,PLZ.blue,vx,1.7,vz);box(.26,1,.26,PLZ.blue,vx-.6,1.7,vz+.1);box(.26,1,.26,PLZ.blue,vx+.6,1.7,vz+.1);
    box(.5,.5,.5,PLZ.vSkin,vx,2.5,vz);cyl(.36,.4,PLZ.vGold,vx,2.95,vz);cyl(.55,.06,PLZ.vGold,vx,2.75,vz);
    box(.08,.08,.04,PLZ.dark,vx-.12,2.55,vz+.26);box(.08,.08,.04,PLZ.dark,vx+.12,2.55,vz+.26);
  }
  if(center){
    // PRAÇA DO CENTRO: escultura principal com torre escura, cubo vermelho e cubos rosa/azul + pórticos amarelos
    box(14,.35,14,PLZ.step,0,.25,0);box(11,.35,11,PLZ.stepD,0,.6,0); // degraus
    sol(-2,-1,2.2,2.2);sol(2.2,1.4,3.6,3.6);sol(-5,3.4,2.1,2.1);sol(5.2,-4,1.6,1.6);
    box(4.2,15,4.2,PLZ.dark,-2,8.1,-1);                               // torre escura
    box(7,7,7,PLZ.red,2.2,3.9,1.4);                                  // cubo vermelho
    box(4,4,4,PLZ.pink,-5,2.4,3.4);box(3,3,3,PLZ.blue,5.2,1.9,-4);box(2.4,2.4,2.4,PLZ.pink,1,9.4,-3);box(2,2,2,PLZ.white,-3,15.9,-1);
    arch(0,-19,0,PLZ.yellow);arch(0,19,0,PLZ.yellow);arch(-19,0,Math.PI/2,PLZ.yellow);arch(19,0,Math.PI/2,PLZ.yellow);
    box(1.4,.5,1.4,PLZ.red,-14,.4,-14);box(1.4,.5,1.4,PLZ.red,14,.4,14);
  }else{
    // praças comuns: escultura de cubos coloridos + um pórtico amarelo (variando por praça)
    const cols=[PLZ.red,PLZ.pink,PLZ.yellow,PLZ.blue,PLZ.dark];
    const a=cols[(i+j)%5],b=cols[(i*2+j+1)%5],c=cols[(i+j*2+2)%5];
    box(10,.3,10,PLZ.step,0,.25,0);
    sol(-1.2,-.8,2.3,2.3);sol(2.2,1.6,1.6,1.6);box(4.4,4.4+R()*2,4.4,a,-1.2,2.5,-.8);box(3,3,3,b,2.2,1.8,1.6);box(2.2,2.2,2.2,c,-2.2,5.6,-.8);
    arch(0,(R()<.5?-1:1)*18,0,PLZ.yellow);
    if(R()<.6)arch((R()<.5?-1:1)*18,0,Math.PI/2,(i+j)%2?PLZ.yellow:PLZ.pink);
  }
}

function buildMansion(bx,bz,i,j,idx,pr){
  const M=c=>new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:c}),wall=M(0xf4eee2),stone=M(0xb9b0a0),white=M(0xffffff),trim=M(0x7a4a36),wood=M(0x8a5a33),floorM=M(0xd8cfbf),dark=M(0x2b2b30),sofa=M(0x7a2f3a),sofa2=M(0x2f4a7a),

/* ---------- junta a geometria estática (prédios, calçadas, árvores) em poucas malhas por região ---------- */
function mergeStatic(meshes){
  const groups=new Map(),CH=CELL*6;
  for(const m of meshes){
    m.updateWorldMatrix(true,false);
    const g=m.geometry.clone();g.applyMatrix4(m.matrixWorld);
    const key=Math.floor(m.matrixWorld.elements[12]/CH)+','+Math.floor(m.matrixWorld.elements[14]/CH);
    let byMat=groups.get(key);if(!byMat)groups.set(key,byMat=new Map());
    let list=byMat.get(m.material);if(!list)byMat.set(m.material,list=[]);
    list.push(g);
  }
  groups.forEach(byMat=>byMat.forEach((gs,mat)=>{
    let nv=0,ni=0;for(const g of gs){nv+=g.attributes.position.count;ni+=g.index.count}
    const pos=new Float32Array(nv*3),nor=new Float32Array(nv*3),uv=new Float32Array(nv*2),idx=nv>65535?new Uint32Array(ni):new Uint16Array(ni);
    let vo=0,io=0;
    for(const g of gs){
      pos.set(g.attributes.position.array,vo*3);nor.set(g.attributes.normal.array,vo*3);uv.set(g.attributes.uv.array,vo*2);
      const ix=g.index.array;for(let k=0;k<ix.length;k++)idx[io+k]=ix[k]+vo;
      vo+=g.attributes.position.count;io+=ix.length;g.dispose();
    }
    const mg=new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    mg.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos,3));mg.setAttribute('normal',new THREE.BufferAttribute(nor,3));mg.setAttribute('uv',new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv,2));mg.setIndex(new THREE.BufferAttribute(idx,1));
    const mesh=new THREE.Mesh(mg,mat);mesh.receiveShadow=true;mesh.matrixAutoUpdate=false;scene.add(mesh);
  }));
}
mergeStatic(MERGE);MERGE.length=0;

{
  const dm=new THREE.Object3D(),n=NB*NB,curbM=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0xb8b2a6});

  const trunks=new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.2,.36,9,6),new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x8a6a4a}),n*6);

  const fronds=new THREE.InstancedMesh(fg,new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x2f8a3c}),n*36);
  let a=0,b=0,t=0,f=0;dm.rotation.order='YXZ';
  for(let i=0;i<NB;i++)for(let j=0;j<NB;j++){
    const bx=i*CELL,bz=j*CELL;
    for(const sg of[-1,1]){
      dm.rotation.set(0,0,0);dm.position.set(bx,.07,bz+sg*HALF);dm.updateMatrix();cx_.setMatrixAt(a++,dm.matrix);
      dm.position.set(bx+sg*HALF,.07,bz);dm.updateMatrix();cz_.setMatrixAt(b++,dm.matrix);

      for(let k=0;k<6;k++){dm.position.set(px,9,pz);dm.rotation.set(.5+Math.random()*.15,k*Math.PI/3+px,0);dm.updateMatrix();fronds.setMatrixAt(f++,dm.matrix)}
    }

/* ---------- beams (job / gps markers) ---------- */
function makeBeam(color){
  const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(3.2,3.2,150,16,1,true),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color,transparent:true,opacity:0.32,side:THREE.DoubleSide,depthWrite:false,fog:false}));
  m.position.y=75;m.visible=false;scene.add(m);return m;
}

  const t=new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);return t})();
{const geo=new THREE.PlaneGeometry(3.6,13);
 const zm=new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo,new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({map:zebraTex,transparent:true,depthWrite:false}),nInt*4);
 const m=new THREE.Matrix4(),qx=new THREE.Quaternion().setFromEuler(new THREE.Euler(-Math.PI/2,0,0)),
   qz=new THREE.Quaternion().setFromEuler(new THREE.Euler(-Math.PI/2,0,Math.PI/2));let n=0;
 const one=new THREE.Vector3(1,1,1);
 for(let a=KMIN;a<=KMAX;a++)for(let b=KMIN;b<=KMAX;b++)for(const s of[-1,1]){
   m.compose(new THREE.Vector3(rc(a)+s*10.2,0.07,rc(b)),qx,one);zm.setMatrixAt(n++,m);
   m.compose(new THREE.Vector3(rc(a),0.07,rc(b)+s*10.2),qz,one);zm.setMatrixAt(n++,m)}
 scene.add(zm)}

/* ---------- sky dome ---------- */
const skyMat=new THREE.ShaderMaterial({side:THREE.BackSide,depthWrite:false,fog:false,
  uniforms:{top:{value:new THREE.Color(0x3a78c8)},bot:{value:new THREE.Color(0x8ec5ff)},sunDir:{value:new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0)},night:{value:0},cloud:{value:0},time:{value:0}},
  vertexShader:'varying vec3 vP;void main(){vP=normalize(position);gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.);}',
  fragmentShader:[
  'uniform vec3 top;uniform vec3 bot;uniform vec3 sunDir;uniform float night;uniform float cloud;uniform float time;varying vec3 vP;',
  'float hash(vec3 p){p=fract(p*0.3183099+.1);p*=17.;return fract(p.x*p.y*p.z*(p.x+p.y+p.z));}',
  'float h2(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453);}',
  'float vn(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p);vec2 f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);return mix(mix(h2(i),h2(i+vec2(1.,0.)),f.x),mix(h2(i+vec2(0.,1.)),h2(i+vec2(1.,1.)),f.x),f.y);}',
  'float fbm(vec2 p){float a=.5;float s=0.;for(int i=0;i<4;i++){s+=a*vn(p);p*=2.03;a*=.5;}return s;}',
  'void main(){float h=clamp(vP.y,0.,1.);vec3 c=mix(bot,top,pow(h,0.55));',
  'float s=max(dot(vP,normalize(sunDir)),0.);float cv=1.-cloud*.8;',
  'c+=vec3(1.,.75,.45)*pow(s,18.)*0.55*(1.-night*0.85)*cv;c+=vec3(1.,.96,.85)*smoothstep(0.9987,0.9996,s)*(1.-night*0.6)*cv;',
  'float st=step(0.9972,hash(floor(vP*230.)))*night*smoothstep(0.08,0.35,vP.y)*(1.-cloud*.9);c+=vec3(st);',
  'vec2 cp=vP.xz/(vP.y+.18)*1.4+vec2(time*.012,time*.006);float n=fbm(cp);',
  'float cov=mix(.62,.28,cloud);float m=smoothstep(cov,cov+.22,n)*smoothstep(.02,.22,vP.y);',
  'vec3 cc=mix(vec3(1.,.97,.93),vec3(.42,.44,.5),cloud*.8)*(1.-night*.88);',
  'c=mix(c,cc,m*(.55+.4*cloud));',
  'gl_FragColor=vec4(c,1.);}'].join('\n')});
const skyMesh=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(420,24,16),skyMat);skyMesh.renderOrder=-10;skyMesh.frustumCulled=false;scene.add(skyMesh);

/* ---------- clima: chuva, nuvens, tempestade ---------- */
const RAIN_N=2200,rainGeo=new THREE.BufferGeometry();
{const pos=new Float32Array(RAIN_N*2*3),seed=new Float32Array(RAIN_N*2*4),e=new Float32Array(RAIN_N*2);
 for(let i=0;i<RAIN_N;i++){const sx=Math.random(),sy=Math.random(),sz=Math.random(),sw=Math.random();for(let k=0;k<2;k++){const o=i*2+k;seed[o*4]=sx;seed[o*4+1]=sy;seed[o*4+2]=sz;seed[o*4+3]=sw;e[o]=k}}
 rainGeo.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos,3));rainGeo.setAttribute('seed',new THREE.BufferAttribute(seed,4));rainGeo.setAttribute('e',new THREE.BufferAttribute(e,1))}
const rainMat=new THREE.ShaderMaterial({transparent:true,depthWrite:false,fog:false,
  uniforms:{t:{value:0},c:{value:new THREE.Vector3()},amt:{value:0},col:{value:new THREE.Color(0xbcd4ff)}},
  vertexShader:'uniform float t;uniform vec3 c;uniform float amt;attribute vec4 seed;attribute float e;varying float a;void main(){if(seed.w>amt){gl_Position=vec4(2.,2.,2.,1.);a=0.;return;}float x=mod(seed.x*60.-c.x,60.)-30.+c.x;float z=mod(seed.z*60.-c.z,60.)-30.+c.z;float y=mod(seed.y*40.-t*26.,40.)+c.y-14.;x+=e*.18;y+=e*1.1;a=.55-.35*e;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*viewMatrix*vec4(x,y,z,1.);}',
  fragmentShader:'uniform vec3 col;varying float a;void main(){gl_FragColor=vec4(col,a*.55);}'});
const rainMesh=new THREE.LineSegments(rainGeo,rainMat);rainMesh.frustumCulled=false;rainMesh.visible=false;rainMesh.renderOrder=5;scene.add(rainMesh);
const wx={mode:'clear',cloud:0,rain:0,flash:0,tc:0,tr:0,auto:true,next:90,thunder:0};
const WXT={clear:[0,0],cloud:[.6,0],rain:[.85,.7],storm:[1,1]};
const WXN={clear:'Tempo limpo',cloud:'Céu nublado',rain:'Começou a chover',storm:'Tempestade chegando'};
function setWeather(m,quiet){wx.mode=m;wx.tc=WXT[m][0];wx.tr=WXT[m][1];if(!quiet)toast(WXN[m],'#9fd0ff',2200)}
const wxIcon=()=>({clear:'☀',cloud:'☁',rain:'🌧',storm:'⛈'})[wx.mode];
const _gray=new THREE.Color(),_white=new THREE.Color(1,1,1);
function stepWeather(dt,time){
  if(wx.auto){wx.next-=dt;if(wx.next<=0){wx.next=rnd(100,240);const r=Math.random();setWeather(r<.4?'clear':r<.65?'cloud':r<.9?'rain':'storm')}}
  const k=Math.min(1,dt*.25);wx.cloud+=(wx.tc-wx.cloud)*k;wx.rain+=(wx.tr-wx.rain)*k;
  if(wx.mode==='storm'&&wx.rain>.6){wx.thunder-=dt;if(wx.thunder<=0){wx.thunder=rnd(5,16);wx.flash=1;setTimeout(playThunder,rnd(300,1800))}}
  wx.flash=Math.max(0,wx.flash-dt*3.5);
  rainMat.uniforms.t.value=time;rainMat.uniforms.c.value.copy(camera.position);
  rainMat.uniforms.amt.value=wx.rain*(QUALITY==='med'?.6:1);
  rainMesh.visible=wx.rain>.03&&!P.inHouse;
}

/* ---------- time of day ---------- */
const cDay=new THREE.Color(0x4fb0ff),cNight=new THREE.Color(0x070b18),cDusk=new THREE.Color(0xff7aa6),tmp=new THREE.Color();
function applyTime(t){                           // t in hours 0..24
  const s=Math.sin((t-6)/24*Math.PI*2);
  const d=clamp(s*1.5+0.35,0,1);
  const dusk=clamp(1-Math.abs(d-0.45)*3.2,0,1)*(d<0.95?1:0);
  tmp.copy(cNight).lerp(cDay,d).lerp(cDusk,dusk*0.55);
  {const l=(tmp.r*.3+tmp.g*.59+tmp.b*.11)*(1-wx.cloud*.35);tmp.lerp(_gray.setRGB(l,l,l),wx.cloud*.7);if(wx.flash>0)tmp.lerp(_white,wx.flash*.6)}
  scene.background.copy(tmp);scene.fog.color.copy(tmp);try{const cd=.22+.78*d;cloudMat.color.setRGB(cd,cd,cd*.97+.03)}catch(e){}
  hemi.intensity=(0.28+0.72*d)*(1-wx.cloud*.3)+wx.flash*1.5;sun.intensity=(0.08+1.05*d)*(1-wx.cloud*.65);
  sun.color.setHex(dusk>0.4?0xffb27a:0xfff2d6);
  const night=1-d;
  const nl=clamp(night+wx.cloud*.2*(1-night),0,1);
  facadeMats.forEach(m=>m.emissiveIntensity=nl*1.1);SIGN_MATS.forEach(m=>m.emissiveIntensity=nl*1.2);
  haloMat.opacity=clamp(nl*1.3-.2,0,.55);poolMat.opacity=clamp(nl*1.2-.15,0,.3);beamM.opacity=nl*.15;
  asphaltMat.color.setScalar(1-wx.rain*.35);
  bulbMat.color.setRGB(0.35+0.65*night,0.32+0.6*night,0.2+0.35*night);
  skyMat.uniforms.bot.value.copy(tmp);
  skyMat.uniforms.top.value.copy(tmp).multiplyScalar(0.55+0.1*(1-d)).lerp(cNight,0.15*(1-d));
  skyMat.uniforms.night.value=night;
  const ang=(t-6)/12*Math.PI;
  sunVec.set(Math.cos(ang)*0.8,Math.sin(ang),0.45).normalize();
  skyMat.uniforms.sunDir.value.copy(sunVec);
  if(sunVec.y<0.08){sunVec.negate();sunVec.y=Math.max(sunVec.y,0.5);sunVec.normalize()}   // moonlight from the opposite side
  {const U=seaMat.uniforms,tt=performance.now()/1000;
   U.time.value=tt;U.dayF.value=d;U.cloud.value=wx.cloud;U.chop.value=1+wx.rain*1.2;U.sunDir.value.copy(sunVec);U.sunI.value=sun.intensity;
   U.sky.value.copy(skyMat.uniforms.top.value).lerp(tmp,.5);U.fogC.value.copy(scene.fog.color);
   const fw=1-wx.cloud*.55;scene.fog.near=fogN0*fw;scene.fog.far=fogF0*fw;U.fogN.value=scene.fog.near;U.fogF.value=scene.fog.far;
   skyMat.uniforms.cloud.value=wx.cloud;skyMat.uniforms.time.value=tt}
  return night;
}
function placeSun(x,z){sun.position.set(x+sunVec.x*160,Math.max(Math.min(sunVec.y,0.5),0.22)*160,z+sunVec.z*160);sun.target.position.set(x,0,z);sun.target.updateMatrixWorld()}

/* ---------- vehicles ---------- */
const glassM=new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color:0x16202e,specular:0xaabbdd,shininess:90});
const tireM=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x101010});
const rimM=new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color:0xcfd5df,specular:0xffffff,shininess:80});
const trimM=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x14161a});
const headM=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xfff2b0});
const blobM=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x000000,transparent:true,opacity:0.28,depthWrite:false});
const cabGeo=(()=>{const g=new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.99,1.24,0.62,4);g.rotateY(Math.PI/4);return g})();
const bx_=(w,h,d,m)=>new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w,h,d),m);
const beamGeo=new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.7,.12,9,10,1,true),beamM=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xfff0c0,transparent:true,opacity:0,depthWrite:false,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,side:THREE.DoubleSide});
/* ---- reflexos realistas: ambiente procedural (céu, horizonte, chão e sol) ---- */
let CAR_ENV=null;
try{const pm=new THREE.PMREMGenerator(renderer),es=new THREE.Scene(),eg=new THREE.SphereGeometry(50,32,18),ps=eg.attributes.position,cl=[],c1=new THREE.Color(),c2=new THREE.Color();
  for(let i=0;i<ps.count;i++){const y=ps.getY(i)/50;
    if(y>.04){c1.setHex(0x3f7fd0);c2.setHex(0xe6f1ff);c1.lerp(c2,1-Math.min(1,y*1.7))}else if(y>-.04)c1.setHex(0xf0e8d8);else{c1.setHex(0x4b4741);c2.setHex(0x8a8478);c1.lerp(c2,Math.min(1,-y*2))}
    cl.push(c1.r,c1.g,c1.b)}
  eg.setAttribute('color',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(cl,3));
  es.add(new THREE.Mesh(eg,new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({vertexColors:true,side:THREE.BackSide})));
  const sb=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(7,12,8),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xfff4d8}));sb.position.set(28,30,16);es.add(sb);
  CAR_ENV=pm.fromScene(es,.02).texture;pm.dispose();glassM.envMap=CAR_ENV;glassM.reflectivity=.6;glassM.needsUpdate=true}catch(e){CAR_ENV=null}
function mkShell(pts,depth,mat){const sp=new THREE.Shape();sp.moveTo(pts[0][0],pts[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++)sp.lineTo(pts[i][0],pts[i][1]);
  const sg=new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(sp,{depth:depth,bevelEnabled:true,bevelSize:.11,bevelThickness:.13,bevelSegments:4});
  sg.rotateY(-Math.PI/2);sg.computeBoundingBox();sg.translate(-(sg.boundingBox.max.x+sg.boundingBox.min.x)/2,0,0);sg.computeVertexNormals();return new THREE.Mesh(sg,mat)}
const R34P=[[-2.08,.45],[2.08,.45],[2.14,.66],[2.0,.8],[1.4,.93],[.9,1.0],[.5,1.12],[.4,1.3],[.12,1.56],[-.3,1.62],[-1.0,1.6],[-1.3,1.4],[-1.5,1.16],[-2.05,1.12],[-2.14,.9]];
const SUVP=[[-2.3,.45],[2.12,.45],[2.18,.95],[2.02,1.2],[1.3,1.3],[1.22,1.7],[1.05,2.12],[-2.1,2.12],[-2.3,1.9]];
function makeCar(color,police,type){
  const g=new THREE.Group();
  const isR34=type==='r34';
  if(isR34&&(((color>>16&255)*.3+(color>>8&255)*.59+(color&255)*.11)/255<.2))color=0x0a2f9e; /* R34 nunca fica preto: azul do filme */
  let bodyM=CAR_ENV?new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({color:color,metalness:.75,roughness:.22,clearcoat:1,clearcoatRoughness:.03,envMap:CAR_ENV,envMapIntensity:1.7}):new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color:color,specular:isR34?0xbfd9f5:0xcccccc,shininess:isR34?140:110});
  if(isR34)bodyM=new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color:0x0a2f9e,specular:0xdbe8ff,shininess:170,emissive:0x01082a}); /* R34: azul vivo sem depender de reflexo (antes ficava preto) */
  const tailM=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xd01a1a});
  const SH=type!=='suv'&&type!=='r34';
  const body=bx_(2.0,0.62,4.3,bodyM);body.position.y=0.78;g.add(body);
  const low=bx_(2.04,0.22,4.36,isR34?bodyM:trimM);low.position.y=0.5;g.add(low);
  const hood=bx_(1.92,0.14,1.25,bodyM);hood.position.set(0,1.16,1.45);g.add(hood);
  const trunk=bx_(1.92,0.14,0.95,bodyM);trunk.position.set(0,1.16,-1.7);g.add(trunk);
  if(SH||type==='r34'||type==='suv'){body.visible=hood.visible=trunk.visible=false}
  const isSUV=type==='suv';
  if(!isSUV){const cab=new THREE.Mesh(cabGeo,glassM);cab.scale.set(1,1,1.26);cab.position.set(0,1.5,-0.2);g.add(cab);
  const roof=bx_(1.38,0.07,1.74,bodyM);roof.position.set(0,1.83,-0.2);g.add(roof);
  if(isR34){cab.visible=roof.visible=low.visible=false;
    /* ===== CARROCERIA NOVA DO R34: coupé quadrado, baixo e largo (tudo em caixas) ===== */
    const gl=new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color:0x020203,specular:0x8899aa,shininess:160,emissive:0x000000});
    const P_=(m,x,y,z,rx)=>{m.position.set(x,y,z);if(rx)m.rotation.x=rx;g.add(m);return m};
    P_(bx_(2.0,.52,4.3,bodyM),0,.61,0);                 // corpo inferior
    P_(bx_(1.96,.14,1.55,bodyM),0,.94,1.37);            // capô longo
    P_(bx_(2.0,.2,1.15,bodyM),0,.97,-1.57);             // porta-malas alto
    P_(bx_(1.56,.5,.8,gl),0,1.26,-.39);            // interior escuro da cabine
    P_(bx_(1.58,.8,.06,gl),0,1.265,.3,-.86);        // para-brisa inclinado
    P_(bx_(1.58,.7,.06,gl),0,1.295,-1.04,.86);      // vidro traseiro inclinado
    P_(bx_(1.62,.07,.95,bodyM),0,1.545,-.39);           // teto reto
    for(const sx of[-1,1]){
      P_(bx_(.08,.4,1.3,gl),sx*.79,1.27,-.4);        // vidros laterais
      P_(bx_(.09,.9,.08,bodyM),sx*.8,1.265,.3,-.86);    // coluna A
      P_(bx_(.1,.7,.1,bodyM),sx*.8,1.295,-1.04,.86);    // coluna C
      P_(bx_(.09,.42,.1,bodyM),sx*.8,1.27,-.55);        // coluna B
      P_(bx_(.07,.09,1.4,bodyM),sx*.8,1.04,-.4);        // soleira dos vidros
    }
  }
  if(SH){cab.visible=roof.visible=false;
    // carroceria arredondada (perfil lateral com bisel) + vidros inclinados, em vez de caixas
    const sp=new THREE.Shape();sp.moveTo(-2.1,.42);sp.lineTo(2.1,.42);sp.lineTo(2.12,.78);sp.quadraticCurveTo(2.08,.98,1.8,1.04);sp.lineTo(.62,1.16);sp.quadraticCurveTo(.42,1.22,.3,1.62);sp.quadraticCurveTo(.2,1.84,-.1,1.86);sp.lineTo(-.85,1.86);sp.quadraticCurveTo(-1.12,1.84,-1.3,1.5);sp.lineTo(-1.5,1.2);sp.quadraticCurveTo(-1.6,1.12,-1.9,1.1);sp.quadraticCurveTo(-2.14,1.06,-2.12,.8);sp.lineTo(-2.1,.42);
    const sg=new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(sp,{depth:1.7,bevelEnabled:true,bevelSize:.1,bevelThickness:.12,bevelSegments:4,curveSegments:10});
    sg.rotateY(-Math.PI/2);sg.computeBoundingBox();sg.translate(-(sg.boundingBox.max.x+sg.boundingBox.min.x)/2,0,0);
    const shell=new THREE.Mesh(sg,bodyM);g.add(shell);
    const ws=bx_(1.7,.5,.04,glassM);ws.position.set(0,1.42,.5);ws.rotation.x=-.5;g.add(ws);
    const rw=bx_(1.6,.46,.04,glassM);rw.position.set(0,1.5,-1.12);rw.rotation.x=.55;g.add(rw);
    for(const sx of[-1,1]){const sw=bx_(.03,.4,1.3,glassM);sw.position.set(sx*.99,1.5,-.4);g.add(sw)}
  }}
  if(isSUV){
    // SUV grande, preto e blindado: carroceria alta e quadrada, grade larga, faróis finos e rodas pretas
    const blk=new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color:0x08080a,specular:0x9aa4b4,shininess:140});
    const armM=new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color:0x15171b,specular:0x445060,shininess:70});
    const led=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xeaf4ff});
    const redL=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xff2020});
    body.scale.set(1.04,1.18,1.0);body.position.y=.88;
    const cabin=bx_(2.02,0.95,3.5,bodyM);cabin.position.set(0,1.62,-0.55);g.add(cabin);cabin.visible=false;g.add(mkShell(SUVP,1.78,bodyM));
    const roofS=bx_(2.04,0.1,3.55,blk);roofS.position.set(0,2.14,-0.55);g.add(roofS);
    const wsh=bx_(1.86,0.74,0.08,glassM);wsh.position.set(0,1.6,1.22);wsh.rotation.x=-0.5;g.add(wsh);
    const rws=bx_(1.86,0.62,0.06,glassM);rws.position.set(0,1.62,-2.33);g.add(rws);
    for(const sx of[-1,1]){
      const gl=bx_(0.04,0.5,3.2,glassM);gl.position.set(sx*1.02,1.78,-0.55);g.add(gl);
      for(const pz of[-0.2,-1.4]){const dl=bx_(0.045,0.9,0.05,blk);dl.position.set(sx*1.03,1.62,pz);g.add(dl)}
      const step=bx_(0.22,0.1,2.8,blk);step.position.set(sx*1.08,0.5,-0.1);g.add(step);
      const hd=bx_(0.06,0.06,.3,blk);hd.position.set(sx*1.06,1.3,-0.3);g.add(hd);
      const rl=bx_(0.07,0.5,0.06,led);rl.position.set(sx*.93,0.95,2.22);g.add(rl);          // faixa de LED vertical
      const hl=bx_(0.62,0.12,0.06,led);hl.position.set(sx*.68,1.28,2.2);g.add(hl);            // farol fino
      const tl=bx_(0.12,0.5,0.06,redL);tl.position.set(sx*.9,1.15,-2.34);g.add(tl);           // lanterna vertical
      const rr=bx_(0.05,0.05,3.2,blk);rr.position.set(sx*.8,2.22,-0.55);g.add(rr);            // rack do teto
    }
    const gr=bx_(1.5,0.62,0.12,trimM);gr.position.set(0,1.0,2.2);g.add(gr);                    // grade grande
    for(let k=0;k<6;k++){const bar=bx_(1.46,0.025,0.14,armM);bar.position.set(0,.76+k*.1,2.21);g.add(bar)}
    const emb=bx_(0.3,0.16,0.06,new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color:0xcfd5df,shininess:120}));emb.position.set(0,1.3,2.24);g.add(emb);
    const skid=bx_(1.3,0.1,0.3,blk);skid.position.set(0,0.38,2.28);g.add(skid);
    const lb=bx_(1.1,0.07,0.16,new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x2a6bff}));lb.position.set(0,1.28,1.55);lb.visible=false;g.add(lb);
  }
  const fb=bx_(2.06,0.2,0.2,isR34?bodyM:trimM);fb.position.set(0,0.55,2.2);g.add(fb);
  const rb=bx_(2.06,0.2,0.2,isR34?bodyM:trimM);rb.position.set(0,0.55,-2.2);g.add(rb);
  const gr=bx_(1.0,0.22,0.06,trimM);gr.position.set(0,0.88,2.17);g.add(gr);
  for(const sx of[-1.08,1.08]){const mr=bx_(0.18,0.14,0.3,bodyM);mr.position.set(sx,1.3,0.55);if(isR34)mr.position.set(sx*0.86,1.14,0.42);g.add(mr)}
  if(!police&&!isR34&&Math.random()<0.4){const sp=bx_(1.7,0.07,0.4,trimM);sp.position.set(0,1.32,-2.05);g.add(sp)}
  if(isR34){
    // Silhueta exclusiva do Skyline R34: para-choque baixo, saias, entradas de ar,
    // faróis redondos traseiros, quatro ponteiras e aerofólio alto.
    const silverM=new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color:0xffffff,specular:0xffffff,shininess:120});

  const before=levelFromXP(getXP()),xp=getXP()+Math.round(n),after=levelFromXP(xp);

function buildPed(L0){

  ['top','pants','shoes','hair'].forEach(k=>{const p=String(L[k]).split('.');L[k]=(FAM[k]&&FAM[k][p[0]])||p[0];V_[k]=p[1]||''});

  const body=ped.body;
  while(body.children.length){const c=body.children[0];body.remove(c);c.traverse(o=>{if(o.geometry)o.geometry.dispose();if(o.material)o.material.dispose()})}
  const sk=new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color:L.skin,specular:0x2a2018,shininess:22}),T=new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color:L.topC,specular:0x1c1c1c,shininess:10}),Pn=LM(L.pantsC),Sh=LM(L.shoesC),Hr=new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color:L.hairC,specular:0x666666,shininess:45}),dark=LM(0x15110f),white=LM(0xf4f4f4);

(function(){

function upgrade(root){const sk=new THREE.Color(LOOK.skin).getHex(),hr=new THREE.Color(LOOK.hairC).getHex();
  root.traverse(o=>{const m=o.material;if(!o.isMesh||!m||!m.isMeshLambertMaterial)return;let n=cache.get(m);
    if(!n){const h=m.color.getHex(),isS=h===sk,isH=h===hr;

      if(isS)n.emissive=m.color.clone().multiplyScalar(.035);cache.set(m,n)}
    o.material=n})}

  }

function openShop(){
  shopOpen=true;L2=Object.assign({},LOOK,{owned:LOOK.owned.slice()});shYaw=0;shCur='top';P.v=0;P.invisible=false;
  ped.g.visible=true;$('#app').classList.add('shopping');$('#shop').hidden=false;if(!phone.hidden)phone.hidden=true;shRender();
}
function closeShop(pay){
  if(pay){const t=shCost(L2);
    if(t>P.money){toast('Dinheiro insuficiente','#ff4d4d',1600);return}
    if(t>0)addMoney(-t,'Loja de roupas');
    Object.keys(CAT).forEach(k=>{if(CAT[k][1].length&&!L2.owned.includes(k+':'+L2[k]))L2.owned.push(k+':'+L2[k])});
    LOOK=Object.assign({},L2,{owned:L2.owned.slice()});saveLook();toast(t?'COMPRA CONCLUÍDA':'VISUAL SALVO','#72cc72',1800);
  }
  buildPed(LOOK);shopOpen=false;$('#shop').hidden=true;$('#app').classList.remove('shopping');setLabels();
}
$('#shop').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest('button');if(!b)return;
  if(b.dataset.c){shCur=b.dataset.c;shQ='';$('#shSearch').value=''}
  else if(b.dataset.i){L2[shCur]=b.dataset.i}
  else if(b.dataset.col){L2[CAT[shCur][2]]=b.dataset.col}
  else if(b.id==='shPay'){closeShop(true);return}
  else if(b.id==='shCancel'){closeShop(false);return}
  else if(b.id==='shL'){shYaw-=.6;return}
  else if(b.id==='shR'){shYaw+=.6;return}
  buildPed(L2);shRender();
});

function stepShop(dt,time){

  ped.legL.rotation.x=ped.legR.rotation.x=ped.armL.rotation.x=ped.armR.rotation.x=0;
}

let rollT=0,rollCD=0,rollDir=0,rolling=false,rollFace=0,rollSide=1,rollSideC=1;const ROLLD=.6;
function doRoll(){if(P.tankU||!P.onFoot||P.inHouse||P.sitting||P.flying||rollT>0||rollCD>0||shopOpen)return;
  const jx=joy.x+(inp.right-inp.left),jy=joy.y+(inp.gas?1:0)-(inp.brake?1:0);
  // Rolamento LATERAL: o boneco continua virado para a frente e rola para a direita/esquerda.
  if(Math.abs(jx)>.12)rollSide=jx>0?1:-1;
  const rx=-Math.cos(camYaw),rz=Math.sin(camYaw),dx=rx*rollSide,dz=rz*rollSide;
  rollDir=Math.atan2(dx,dz);rollFace=P.h;
  rollSideC=(dx*(-Math.cos(P.h))+dz*Math.sin(P.h))>=0?1:-1;
  rollT=ROLLD;rollCD=1.1}

function exitCar(){
  if(P.onFoot)return;
  if(Math.abs(P.v)>5){toast('Pare o carro primeiro','#ffb347',1400);return}
  parked.x=P.x;parked.z=P.z;parked.h=P.h;P.v=0;
  P.x+=Math.cos(P.h)*2.5;P.z+=-Math.sin(P.h)*2.5;
  inp.horn=0;camYaw=P.h;P.onFoot=true;ped.g.visible=true;me.tailM.color.setHex(0xff2020);setLabels();toast('A pé','#fff',900);
}
function enterCar(){
  if(!P.onFoot)return;
  // Use a posição real do veículo na cena: parked pode ficar desatualizado
  // depois de trocar de veículo, carregar um salvamento ou reposicionar o carro.
  const carX=(me&&me.g&&Number.isFinite(me.g.position.x))?me.g.position.x:parked.x;
  const carZ=(me&&me.g&&Number.isFinite(me.g.position.z))?me.g.position.z:parked.z;
  const carH=(me&&me.g&&Number.isFinite(me.g.rotation.y))?me.g.rotation.y:parked.h;
  const distance=Math.hypot(P.x-carX,P.z-carZ);
  // Raio maior para funcionar com o personagem junto à porta em telas touch.
  if(distance>10){toast('Chegue mais perto do carro','#ffb347',1400);return}
  parked.x=carX;parked.z=carZ;parked.h=carH;
  P.x=carX;P.z=carZ;P.h=carH;P.v=0;P.onFoot=false;
  ped.g.visible=false;setLabels();toast('No carro','#fff',900);
}

let _flyMax=0;function flyMax(){if(!_flyMax){for(const h of PROPERTY_HOMES)_flyMax=Math.max(_flyMax,h.roof||0);_flyMax=(_flyMax||31)+1}return _flyMax}
function roofSupport(x,z,h){let r=0;for(const b of homesNear(x,z)){if(Math.abs(x-b.x)<=11.5&&Math.abs(z-b.z)<=11.5&&h>=(b.roof||0)-.5){r=b.roof||0;break}}const c=window.CBSUP?window.CBSUP(x,z,h):0;return Math.max(r,c)}
/* ---------- colisões extras a pé: interior, postes, palmeiras, loja, presídio, carros ---------- */
function _pushBox(minX,maxX,minZ,maxZ){
  if(P.x<minX||P.x>maxX||P.z<minZ||P.z>maxZ)return;
  const o=[[P.x-minX,minX,0],[maxX-P.x,maxX,0],[P.z-minZ,minZ,1],[maxZ-P.z,maxZ,1]].sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0])[0];
  if(o[2])P.z=o[1];else P.x=o[1];
}
function _pushCircle(cx,cz,R){
  const dx=P.x-cx,dz=P.z-cz,d2=dx*dx+dz*dz;if(d2>=R*R)return;
  const d=Math.sqrt(d2)||.001,k=R/d;
  if(d<.001){P.x=cx+R;return}
  P.x=cx+dx*k;P.z=cz+dz*k;
}
// [x0,x1,z0,z1] em coordenadas locais do interior (paredes divisórias e móveis)
const HOME_SOLIDS=[
  [-10.5,-3.5,.8,1.2],[3.5,10.5,.8,1.2],[-.2,.2,-9,-2],      // paredes internas
  [-7.7,-2.3,4.85,6.15],[-7.7,-6.7,3.7,5.9],[-3.3,-2.3,3.7,5.9], // sofás
  [-6.6,-3.4,1.5,3.3],                                         // mesa de centro
  [3,9,-6.9,-4.7],[8.35,9.85,-7.85,-6.35],                      // bancada e geladeira
  [3.1,8.9,3.2,6.6],[1.97,3.23,2.2,3.2],[8.77,10.03,2.2,3.2],  // cama e criados-mudos
  [8,9.6,.65,1.75],[5.1,8.9,-2.4,-2.2],[5.05,5.25,-2.4,2.2],   // pia do banheiro e box de vidro
  [-10.15,-8.65,-6.8,-6.0],[-8.6,-7.9,-7.0,-6.2],              // mesa do escritório
  [-9.6,-8.4,6.6,7.8],[8.4,9.6,6.6,7.8]                        // vasos de planta
];
function collideInterior(){
  const r=.45,ox=HOME_INT.x,oz=HOME_INT.z;
  for(const s of HOME_SOLIDS)_pushBox(ox+s[0]-r,ox+s[1]+r,oz+s[2]-r,oz+s[3]+r);
}
function collideExtras(r){
  for(const m of MANS){if(Math.abs(P.x-m.x)>60||Math.abs(P.z-m.z)>60)continue;for(const q of m.s)_pushBox(q[0]-r,q[1]+r,q[2]-r,q[3]+r);if(m.door.a<.8){const b=m.door.box;_pushBox(b[0]-r,b[1]+r,b[2]-r,b[3]+r)}}
  const i0=Math.round(P.x/CELL),j0=Math.round(P.z/CELL);
  // palmeiras das calçadas
  for(let i=i0-1;i<=i0+1;i++)for(let j=j0-1;j<=j0+1;j++){
    if(i<0||j<0||i>=NB||j>=NB)continue;
    const bx=i*CELL,bz=j*CELL;
    for(const o of[-18,0,18]){_pushCircle(bx+o,bz-HALF+2.5,.4+r);_pushCircle(bx-HALF+2.5,bz+o,.4+r)}
  }
  // postes de luz
  {const a0=Math.round(P.x/CELL-.5),b0=Math.round(P.z/CELL-.5);
   for(let a=a0;a<=a0+1;a++)for(let b=b0;b<=b0+1;b++)for(const sx of[-1,1])for(const sz of[-1,1]){
     if((sx+sz)!==0&&((a+b)&1))continue;_pushCircle(rc(a)+sx*9.5,rc(b)+sz*9.5,.25+r)}}
  // placas dos postos
  for(const g of GAS)_pushCircle(rc(g[0])+11,rc(g[1])+11,.3+r);
  // loja de roupas
  _pushBox(STORE.x+4.5-r,STORE.x+13.5+r,STORE.z-3.5-r,STORE.z+3.5+r);
  // presídio: blocos internos e grade
  if(Math.abs(P.x-PRISON.x)<50&&Math.abs(P.z-PRISON.z)<50){
    for(const s of[[-36,-14,-8,8],[14,36,-8,8],[-7,7,-8.2,-7.8]])_pushBox(PRISON.x+s[0]-r,PRISON.x+s[1]+r,PRISON.z+s[2]-r,PRISON.z+s[3]+r);
  }
  // carros do trânsito (dois círculos por carro)
  for(const ai of cars){
    if(Math.abs(ai.x-P.x)>8||Math.abs(ai.z-P.z)>8)continue;
    const fx=Math.sin(ai.h),fz=Math.cos(ai.h);
    _pushCircle(ai.x+fx*1.4,ai.z+fz*1.4,1.15+r);_pushCircle(ai.x,ai.z,1.15+r);_pushCircle(ai.x-fx*1.4,ai.z-fz*1.4,1.15+r);
  }
}
function collidePedBuildings(oldX,oldZ){
  if(P.inHouse){collideInterior();return}
  if(P.flying&&P.flyY>2)return;
  const r=0.72;
  for(const h of homesNear(P.x,P.z)){
    if(P.flyY>=(h.roof||0)-.5)continue;
    const minX=h.x-11-r,maxX=h.x+11+r,minZ=h.z-11-r,maxZ=h.z+11+r;
    if(P.x<minX||P.x>maxX||P.z<minZ||P.z>maxZ)continue;
    // A porta fica no lado +Z, com abertura central de 3,2 m.
    const atFront=P.z>h.z+10.5 && Math.abs(P.x-h.x)<2.15;
    if(atFront)continue;
    const options=[
      {x:Math.abs(P.x-minX),v:minX,axis:'x'}, {x:Math.abs(maxX-P.x),v:maxX,axis:'x'},
      {x:Math.abs(P.z-minZ),v:minZ,axis:'z'}, {x:Math.abs(maxZ-P.z),v:maxZ,axis:'z'}
    ].sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x);
    const q=options[0];
    if(q.axis==='x')P.x=q.v;else P.z=q.v;
  }
  // Sólidos das praças (esculturas, palmeiras, postes, colunas e bancos).
  for(const h of plzSolids(P.x,P.z)){
    const minX=h.x-h.hx-r,maxX=h.x+h.hx+r,minZ=h.z-h.hz-r,maxZ=h.z+h.hz+r;
    if(P.x<minX||P.x>maxX||P.z<minZ||P.z>maxZ)continue;
    const options=[
      {x:Math.abs(P.x-minX),v:minX,axis:'x'},{x:Math.abs(maxX-P.x),v:maxX,axis:'x'},
      {x:Math.abs(P.z-minZ),v:minZ,axis:'z'},{x:Math.abs(maxZ-P.z),v:maxZ,axis:'z'}
    ].sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x);
    const q=options[0];
    if(q.axis==='x')P.x=q.v;else P.z=q.v;
  }
  if(P.flyY<3)collideExtras(r);
}
/* ---------- sentar nos bancos das praças ---------- */
function benchNear(){
  if(!P.onFoot||P.inHouse||P.sitting||(P.flying&&P.flyY>1))return null;
  const i=Math.round(P.x/CELL),j=Math.round(P.z/CELL);
  if(i<0||j<0||i>=NB||j>=NB)return null;
  const list=PLZ.bench.get(i*NB+j);if(!list)return null;
  let best=null,bd=3.4;
  for(const b of list){const d=Math.hypot(P.x-b.x,P.z-b.z);if(d<bd){bd=d;best=b}}
  if(!best)return null;
  const cd=(me&&me.g)?Math.hypot(P.x-me.g.position.x,P.z-me.g.position.z):99;
  return bd<=cd?best:null;
}
function sitDown(b){
  P.sitting=b;P.x=b.x;P.z=b.z;P.h=b.h;P.v=0;camYaw=b.h;
  setLabels();toast('Sentado. Toque em LEVANTAR ou mexa o analógico','#7dff9b',1800);
}
function standUp(){
  const b=P.sitting;if(!b)return;
  P.sitting=null;P.x=b.x+Math.sin(b.h)*2.3;P.z=b.z+Math.cos(b.h)*2.3;
  if(ped.legL){ped.legL.rotation.x=0;ped.legR.rotation.x=0;ped.armL.rotation.x=0;ped.armR.rotation.x=0}
  setLabels();
}
let _lastBenchNear=false;
function stepFoot(dt,time){
  P.jumpY=0;P.jumpV=0;
  const jx=joy.x+(inp.right-inp.left),jy=joy.y+(inp.gas?1:0)-(inp.brake?1:0);
  const mag=Math.min(1,Math.hypot(jx,jy));
  {const bn=!!P.sitting||!!benchNear();if(bn!==_lastBenchNear){_lastBenchNear=bn;setLabels()}}
  if(P.sitting){
    if(mag>0.5)standUp();
    else{
      P.v=0;ped.g.position.set(P.x,-0.09,P.z);ped.g.rotation.y=P.h;ped.g.visible=!P.invisible;
      if(ped.legL){ped.legL.rotation.x=-1.25;ped.legR.rotation.x=-1.25;ped.armL.rotation.x=-.45;ped.armR.rotation.x=-.45}
      return;
    }
  }
  rollCD=Math.max(0,rollCD-dt);let sp=0;
  if(rollT>0){rollT-=dt;const k=1-Math.max(0,rollT)/ROLLD;sp=13*(1-.4*k);P.h=rollFace;P.x+=Math.sin(rollDir)*sp*dt;P.z+=Math.cos(rollDir)*sp*dt}
  else if(P.planeD){
    const mx=P.turbo?250:110;P.pthr=clamp((P.pthr||0)+jy*.6*dt,0,1);P.pv=Math.max(0,(P.pv||0)+(P.pthr*mx-(P.pv||0))*Math.min(1,.28*dt)-(jy<-.1?14*dt:0));
    const sg=Math.min(1,P.pv/8),tr=jx*sg*(1.1-.55*Math.min(1,P.pv/80));P.h-=tr*.9*dt;sp=P.pv;
    P.x+=Math.sin(P.h)*sp*dt;P.z+=Math.cos(P.h)*sp*dt;
  }
  else if(mag>0.12){
    const fx=Math.sin(camYaw),fz=Math.cos(camYaw),rx=-Math.cos(camYaw),rz=Math.sin(camYaw);
    const mx=fx*jy+rx*jx,mz=fz*jy+rz*jx;
    P.h+=angDiff(P.h,Math.atan2(mx,mz))*Math.min(1,14*dt);
    sp=P.hero?(P.flyY>2?(mag>0.92?42:32*Math.max(0.4,mag)):(mag>0.92?30:22*Math.max(0.4,mag))):(P.flying?(inp.hand?30:(mag>0.92?24:18*Math.max(0.4,mag))):(inp.hand?11:(mag>0.92?8.5:6.2*Math.max(0.4,mag))));
    if(VBOOTS&&!P.flying&&!P.hero)sp*=1.8;if(P.suit)sp=(P.flyY>2?(inp.hand?600:280):60)*Math.max(.25,Math.min(1,mag/.9));if(P.heliD){sp=180.56*Math.min(1,Math.max(.4,mag/.9))}P.x+=Math.sin(P.h)*sp*dt;P.z+=Math.cos(P.h)*sp*dt;
  }
  P.v=sp;P.hv=sp;
  collidePedBuildings(P.x,P.z);
  if(!P.inHouse){P.x=clamp(P.x,LIM_MIN,LIM_MAX);P.z=clamp(P.z,LIM_MIN,LIM_MAX);/* A pé, pode atravessar a calçada até a porta dos prédios. */}else{P.x=clamp(P.x,HOME_INT.x-9,HOME_INT.x+9);P.z=clamp(P.z,HOME_INT.z-7,HOME_INT.z+7)}
  if(P.planeD){const k=clamp(((P.pv||0)-45)/20,0,1);let dy=(inp.up-inp.down)*k*26;if(P.flyY>.3&&k<1)dy-=(1-k)*18;P.flyY=clamp(P.flyY+dy*dt,roofSupport(P.x,P.z,P.flyY),Math.min(500,window.CBCEIL?window.CBCEIL(P.x,P.z,P.flyY):1e9))}
  else if(P.flying){
    // No voo, o eixo vertical do analógico também controla a altitude:
    // empurrar para cima sobe e puxar para baixo desce, enquanto o personagem avança.
    const stickLift=0;
    const buttonLift=inp.up-inp.down;
    const lift=clamp(stickLift+buttonLift,-1,1);
    P.flyY=clamp(P.flyY+lift*(P.suit?(inp.hand?200:80):18)*dt,roofSupport(P.x,P.z,P.flyY),Math.min(P.suit?400:(P.heliD?Math.max(flyMax(),60):flyMax()),window.CBCEIL?window.CBCEIL(P.x,P.z,P.flyY):1e9));
  }else P.flyY=Math.max(roofSupport(P.x,P.z,P.flyY),P.flyY-(22+Math.min(40,P.flyY*.35))*dt);
  $('#flyUp').classList.toggle('show',P.flying);$('#flyDown').classList.toggle('show',P.flying);
  const dx=P.x-parked.x,dz=P.z-parked.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
  if(d<2.4&&d>0.01){P.x=parked.x+dx/d*2.4;P.z=parked.z+dz/d*2.4}
  ped.g.position.set(P.x,walkSurfaceY(P.x,P.z)+P.flyY+P.jumpY,P.z);ped.g.rotation.y=P.h;ped.g.visible=!P.invisible;
  const sw=Math.sin(time*(sp>8?15:10))*Math.min(1,sp/3)*0.9;
  ped.legL.rotation.x=sw;ped.legR.rotation.x=-sw;ped.armL.rotation.x=-sw;ped.armR.rotation.x=sw;
  if(shootAim>0)shootAim-=dt;if(shootAim>0||(LOOK&&LOOK.foto&&gunOut&&HG()&&!P.sitting)){ped.armL.rotation.x=-1.15;ped.armR.rotation.x=-.5}if(P.suit)suitPose(dt);
  if(holsterT>0){holsterT=Math.max(0,holsterT-dt);const u=1-holsterT/.9;   // animação: giro da dourada e coldre
    ped.armL.rotation.x=u<.35?-1.15-(u/.35)*1.0:-2.15+((u-.35)/.65)*2.25;ped.armR.rotation.x=-.5*(1-u);
    if(ped.gun)ped.gun.rotation.z=u<.6?(u/.6)*Math.PI*4:0;
    if(u>=.7&&holsterDo){holsterDo=false;gunOut=false;try{syncWeaponUI()}catch(e){}toast('Arma guardada','#fff',900)}}
  if(drawT>0){drawT=Math.max(0,drawT-dt);const u=1-drawT/.6;   // animação: saque da dourada com giro
    ped.armL.rotation.x=.1-u*1.25-Math.sin(u*Math.PI)*.9;
    if(ped.gun)ped.gun.rotation.z=u<.8?(u/.8)*Math.PI*4:0}
  if(rollT>0){const a=(1-Math.max(0,rollT)/ROLLD)*Math.PI*2,bd=ped.body;const th=rollSideC*a;bd.rotation.set(0,0,th);bd.position.set(.9*Math.sin(th),.9*(1-Math.cos(th)),0);ped.legL.rotation.x=ped.legR.rotation.x=-.9;ped.armL.rotation.x=ped.armR.rotation.x=-.9;rolling=true}else if(rolling){ped.body.rotation.set(0,0,0);ped.body.position.set(0,0,0);rolling=false}
  P.v=0;   // keeps the traffic and mission logic reading "stopped"
}

// Pequena rampa invisível na borda dos quarteirões: o personagem sobe andando sem degrau brusco.
function walkSurfaceY(x,z){
  const i=Math.round(x/CELL),j=Math.round(z/CELL);
  if(i<0||j<0||i>=NB||j>=NB)return 0;
  const dx=Math.abs(x-i*CELL),dz=Math.abs(z-j*CELL);
  if(dx>HALF+2||dz>HALF+2)return 0;
  const t=clamp((Math.min(HALF-dx,HALF-dz)+2)/2,0,1);
  return 0.055*t*t*(3-2*t);
}

function wire(A,B,sag){for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const t0=i/5,t1=(i+1)/5;[t0,t1].forEach(t=>WP.push(A[0]+(B[0]-A[0])*t,A[1]+(B[1]-A[1])*t-sag*4*t*(1-t),A[2]+(B[2]-A[2])*t))}}
const ALLA=[GR[0],GR[1],GR[2],GR[3],FL[0],FL[1],FL[2],FL[3],RFS,PIL,RB,SH,DS,TKL,LD,GB,PO,FG,TR],RNG=[],INSTM=new Map();
FB.forEach(([cx,cz],bi)=>{const sol=[],tops=[];FS[bi]=sol;GP.push([cx,cz]);const L0=ALLA.map(a=>a.length),W0=WP.length;
  for(let a=0;a<6;a++)for(let b=0;b<6;b++){
    const ox=cx+((a>>1)-1)*19+((a&1)?3.9:-3.9),oz=cz+((b>>1)-1)*19+((b&1)?3.9:-3.9);
    if(rnd()<.07){TR.push([ox+rr(-1.5,1.5),oz+rr(-1.5,1.5)]);continue}
    const w=rr(6.3,7.7),d=rr(6.3,7.7),x=ox+(rnd()-.5)*(7.8-w),z=oz+(rnd()-.5)*(7.8-d),fl=pick([2,2,2,3,3,3,3,4,4,5]),kind=pick([0,0,0,1,1,2,2,3,3,3]),over=rnd()<.3;
    sol.push([x-w/2-.6,x+w/2+.6,z-d/2-.6,z+d/2+.6,fl*3+1]);
    let rx=x,rz=z,rw=w,rd=d,topY=.08;
    for(let f=0;f<fl;f++){const last=f===fl-1&&fl>2&&rnd()<.28,ex=over&&f>0&&!last?.5:0;
      rw=(last?.72:1)*w+ex;rd=(last?.82:1)*d+ex;rx=x+(last?rr(-.6,.6):0);rz=z+(last?rr(-.5,.5):0);
      if(last)RFS.push([x,z,w+.4,d+.4,topY]);
      (f?FL:GR)[kind].push([rx,.08+f*3+1.5,rz,rw,rd]);topY=.08+(f+1)*3}
    RFS.push([rx,rz,rw+.5,rd+.5,topY]);tops.push([rx+rr(-rw/3,rw/3),topY+.3,rz+rr(-rd/3,rd/3)]);
    if(rnd()<.45)for(const[sx,sz]of[[-1,-1],[1,-1],[-1,1],[1,1]])if(rnd()<.6){const px=rx+sx*rw*.42,pz=rz+sz*rd*.42;PIL.push([px,topY,pz]);if(rnd()<.8)RB.push([px,topY,pz])}
    const r=rnd();
    if(r<.3)SH.push([rx,rz,rw*.92,rd*.92,topY+.9,rnd()<.5?.1:-.1]);
    else{if(r<.8)TKL.push([rx+rr(-1.4,1.4),rz+rr(-1.4,1.4),topY]);if(rnd()<.5)DS.push([rx+rr(-2,2),rz+rr(-2,2),topY,rnd()*6.28])}
    if(rnd()<.28)for(let i=0;i<3;i++)LD.push([rx+(i-1)*1.3,topY+.7,rz+rd/2-.3]);
    if(rnd()<.14)FG.push([x+rr(-1,1),fl*3-1.2,z+d/2+.1]);
  }
  const pole=(px,pz)=>{PO.push([px,pz]);const near=tops.filter(t=>Math.hypot(t[0]-px,t[2]-pz)<20);for(let k=0;k<Math.min(5,near.length);k++)wire([px,7.4,pz],pick(near),.9)};
  [[-9.5,-9.5],[9.5,-9.5],[-9.5,9.5],[9.5,9.5],[-9.5,0],[9.5,0]].forEach(([u,v])=>pole(cx+u,cz+v));
  for(let i=0;i<14;i++){const A=pick(tops),B=pick(tops),dd=Math.hypot(A[0]-B[0],A[2]-B[2]);if(dd>4&&dd<19)wire(A,B,.7)}
  for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const al=rnd()<.5;GB.push([cx+(al?pick([-9.5,9.5])+rr(-1.1,1.1):rr(-26,26)),cz+(al?rr(-26,26):pick([-9.5,9.5])+rr(-1.1,1.1))])}
  RNG[bi]={a:ALLA.map((a,i)=>[L0[i],a.length]),w:[W0,WP.length]};
});

    u.qrr=k;u.qrrT=performance.now()+ILG_QRR_TIME;u.hx=cx;u.hz=cz;u.skip=false;
    const nx=cx+(Math.random()-.5)*50,nz=cz+(Math.random()-.5)*50;u.tx=nx;u.tz=nz;
    if(!u.dead){u.x=nx;u.z=nz;u.g.position.set(nx,.12,nz)}}   // teleporte direto para a favela do QRR
  alertB=performance.now()+ILG_QRR_TIME;if(withArmor)ilgArmor(k);return n}

function ilgArmor(k){
  if(U.filter(u=>u.armored&&u.fv===k&&!u.dead).length>=2)return;
  const [cx,cz]=FB[k];
  [[cx+40,-Math.PI/2],[cx-40,Math.PI/2]].forEach(([ax,ah])=>{
  const g=new THREE.Group(),st=mat(0x30343a),dk=mat(0x1b1d20),lt=mat(0x555b63),rd=mat(0x8a1c1c);
  bx(4.4,1.5,8.4,st,0,1.7,0,0,g);bx(4.0,.7,3,dk,0,2.7,2.6,0,g);bx(4.6,.4,8.6,lt,0,.9,0,0,g);
  [-1,1].forEach(q=>{for(let z=-3;z<=3;z+=3)bx(.7,1.5,1.5,M.mt,q*2.4,.8,z,0,g)});
  bx(2.6,.9,2.8,st,0,3.3,-.6,0,g);bx(.28,.28,3,M.mt,0,3.4,1.9,0,g);bx(.5,.5,.9,dk,0,3.4,.5,0,g);bx(4.5,.25,.3,rd,0,2.2,4.3,0,g);
  const sp=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:tex(256,64,(c,w,h)=>{c.fillStyle='rgba(120,15,15,.9)';c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);c.fillStyle='#fff';c.font='bold 30px Arial';c.textAlign='center';c.fillText('BLINDADO',w/2,44)}),transparent:true,fog:false}));
  sp.scale.set(7,1.75,1);sp.position.set(0,6.4,0);g.add(sp);
  g.position.set(ax,.12,cz);g.rotation.y=ah;AG.add(g);
  const u={g,x:ax,z:cz,h:ah,armor:1,hp:4500,mhp:4500,side:'B',cd:1,r:3.4,sp:1.2,hx:ax,hz:cz,tx:ax,tz:cz,i:U.length,armored:1,fv:k,until:performance.now()+ILG_QRR_TIME+30000};
  U.push(u)});
  if(isIlg())toast('🛡 2 blindados com metralhadora chegaram do lado da '+FNAME(k),'#ffb347',2800)}
function ilgBlow(u){
  try{boom(u.x,u.z,'B',0)}catch(e){}
  for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.6,10,8),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:j%2?0xffd24a:0xff5a1e,transparent:true,opacity:.9}));
    m.position.set(u.x+(Math.random()-.5)*5,1.5+j*.8,u.z+(Math.random()-.5)*5);AG.add(m);FX.push({m,l:.5+j*.06})}
  if(isIlg())toast('💥 O blindado da '+FNAME(u.fv)+' explodiu!','#ff5a4d',3200)}
function ilgArmorGone(u){const i=U.indexOf(u);if(i>=0)U.splice(i,1);AG.remove(u.g)}
setInterval(()=>{try{const now=performance.now();
  for(let i=U.length-1;i>=0;i--){const u=U[i];if(!u.armored)continue;
    if(u.dead||u.hp<=0){ilgBlow(u);ilgArmorGone(u);continue}
    if(!INV[u.fv].on){if(!u.off)u.off=now;else if(now-u.off>8000){ilgArmorGone(u);continue}}else u.off=0;
    if(now>u.until)ilgArmorGone(u)}
}catch(e){}},250);
function ilgQRR(k){
  if(!isIlg())return 'Só o Ilegal pode pedir QRR';
  if(performance.now()<PAC[k])return FNAME(k)+' pacificada: QRR desativado por '+Math.ceil((PAC[k]-performance.now())/1000)+'s';
  if(!INV[k].on)return FNAME(k)+' não está invadida';
  if(performance.now()<qrrCd)return 'QRR em espera: '+Math.ceil((qrrCd-performance.now())/1000)+'s';
  if(ILG.bal<ILG_QRR_COST)return 'Conta do ilegal sem saldo para o QRR';
  ILG.bal-=ILG_QRR_COST;ilgSave();qrrCd=performance.now()+ILG_QRR_CD;
  const n=ilgSend(k,true);
  return 'QRR enviado! '+n+' bandidos de todas as favelas vão defender '+FNAME(k)}
function ilgQrrEnd(u){u.qrr=null;u.hx=u.ohx;u.hz=u.ohz;u.tx=u.ohx;u.tz=u.ohz;u.sp=u.sp0;if(!u.dead){u.x=u.ohx;u.z=u.ohz;u.g.position.set(u.x,.12,u.z)}}
setInterval(()=>{try{
  const now=performance.now();
  FB.forEach((_,k)=>{const st=INV[k],a=armyIn(k);
    if(a&&!st.on&&!(now<PAC[k])){st.on=true;st.pac=false;
      if(ILG.auto!==false){const n=ilgSend(k,true);if(isIlg())toast('🚨 '+FNAME(k)+' invadida! QRR automático: '+n+' bandidos teleportados + 2 blindados','#ff5a4d',4200)}
      else if(isIlg())toast('🚨 '+FNAME(k)+' invadida pelo exército! Abra o painel Ilegal e peça QRR','#ff5a4d',4200)}
    else if(!a&&st.on){st.on=false;if(isIlg())toast('Exército saiu de '+FNAME(k),'#7dff9b',2200)}
    if(st.on&&!st.pac&&defAlive(k)===0){st.pac=true;ILG.paci++;ilgSave();PAC[k]=now+PAC_MS;
      for(const u of U){if(isBand(u)&&u.qrr===k)ilgQrrEnd(u);if(isBand(u)&&u.fv===k&&u.dead&&u.rt<PAC_MS/1000)u.rt=PAC_MS/1000}
      for(let q=U.length-1;q>=0;q--){const u=U[q];if(u.armored&&u.fv===k)ilgArmorGone(u)}
      alertB=0;try{FAVAPI.demolish(k);DEMO[k]=true}catch(e){}if(isIlg())toast('☠ '+FNAME(k)+' pacificada: favela DEMOLIDA! Reconstrução em 5 min','#ffb347',4200)}
    if(DEMO[k]&&now>=PAC[k]){DEMO[k]=false;try{FAVAPI.rebuild(k)}catch(e){}if(isIlg())toast('🏚 '+FNAME(k)+' pode ser reconstruída: bandidos voltaram','#7dff9b',3500)}
    if(st.on&&ILG.auto!==false&&!(now<PAC[k]))ilgSend(k,false);
    if(!st.on)st.pac=false;ilgLabel(k)});
  for(const u of U){if(isBand(u)&&u.qrr!=null&&(now>u.qrrT||!INV[u.qrr].on))ilgQrrEnd(u)}
}catch(e){}},1000);

function armyNear(x,z){if(Math.abs(x-BX)<115&&Math.abs(z-BZ)<115)return true;for(const o of U)if(o.side==='A'&&!o.dead&&(o.x-x)**2+(o.z-z)**2<2025)return true;return false}
function foe(u){if(u.side==='A'){const A0=window.__rotaArmy;if(A0&&A0.maxOn&&!A0.fire)return null}let best=null,bd=u.tank?140:(u.side==='A'?((window.__rotaArmy&&(window.__rotaArmy.mode==='attack'||window.__rotaArmy.mode==='favela'))?110:75):(performance.now()<alertB?150:95));bd*=bd;const now=performance.now();
  const chk=(x,z,o)=>{const d=(x-u.x)**2+(z-u.z)**2;if(d<bd){bd=d;best=o}};
  for(const o of U)if(!o.dead&&o.side!==u.side)chk(o.x,o.z,o);
  if(u.side==='B'){for(const c of cars)if(c.police)chk(c.x,c.z,{x:c.x,z:c.z});for(const h of (ARMY.xh||[]))if(!h.dead)chk(h.x,h.z,h);if((policeRole||now<angryB)&&P.onFoot)chk(P.x,P.z,{x:P.x,z:P.z,pl:1})}
  else{if(!armyImmune()&&(P.wanted>=3||now<angryA||armyTrespass()))chk(P.x,P.z,{x:P.x,z:P.z,pl:1});const A=window.__rotaArmy;if(A&&A.mode==='attack'&&A.tg&&A.tg.pl2)chk(A.tg.x,A.tg.z,{x:A.tg.x,z:A.tg.z});
    for(const c of cars)if(c.police&&(P.wanted>0||armyNear(c.x,c.z)))chk(c.x,c.z,{x:c.x,z:c.z,pc:c});
    if(!best&&A&&((A.maxOn&&A.fire)||(A.mode==='attack'&&!A.tid))){for(const p of PEDS)if(p.state!=='hit')chk(p.x,p.z,{x:p.x,z:p.z,npc:p})}}
  return best}
function fire(u,f){try{playShot(u.x,u.z)}catch(e){}const dx=f.x-u.x,dz=f.z-u.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1,sp=u.tank?55:75,sc=u.tank?0:(u.side==='B'?.012:.05)*d;
  const m=new THREE.Mesh(u.tank?new THREE.SphereGeometry(.45,8,6):new THREE.BoxGeometry(.08,.08,.6),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:u.tank?0xffa030:0xffee66}));AG.add(m);
  BULLETS.push({x:u.x,y:u.tank?3:1.5,z:u.z,vx:dx/d*sp+(Math.random()-.5)*sc,vz:dz/d*sp+(Math.random()-.5)*sc,life:d/sp+.3,side:u.side,shell:u.tank,tk:u.tank?1:0,m})}
function boom(x,z,side,pl){if(pl||side==='A'){for(const c of cars.slice())if(Math.hypot(c.x-x,c.z-z)<7&&(!c.police||pl||P.wanted>0||armyNear(c.x,c.z)))removeAI(c);for(const p of PEDS)if(p.state!=='hit'&&Math.hypot(p.x-x,p.z-z)<7){p.state='hit';p.t=0;p.vx=0;p.vz=0}}const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1,10,8),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xff7a24,transparent:true,opacity:.85}));m.position.set(x,1.5,z);AG.add(m);FX.push({m,l:.5});
  for(const o of U)if(!o.dead&&o.side!==side&&(!o.armored||window.__tk)&&Math.hypot(o.x-x,o.z-z)<8)o.hp-=150*(o.armor||1)*((o.armored&&window.__mk)||1)}

function nearTank(){let b=null,bd=100;for(const u of U)if(u.tank&&!u.dead){const d=(u.x-P.x)**2+(u.z-P.z)**2;if(d<bd){bd=d;b=u}}return b}
function exitTank(){const u=P.tankU;if(!u)return;u.drv=0;P.tankU=null;P.x=u.x+Math.cos(u.h)*5;P.z=u.z-Math.sin(u.h)*5;ped.g.visible=!P.invisible;setLabels();syncWeaponUI();toast('Saiu do tanque','#fff',900)}
tankToggle=function(){if(P.tankU){exitTank();return true}
  if(P.onFoot&&!P.inHouse&&!P.sitting&&!P.flying){const u=nearTank();if(u){u.drv=1;P.tankU=u;P.x=u.x;P.z=u.z;P.h=u.h;ped.g.visible=false;setLabels();syncWeaponUI();toast('TANQUE: mire com a câmera e toque ATIRAR','#7dff9b',2200);return true}}return false}

function stepMissiles(dt){

    if(tg&&!tg.dead){const rx=tg.x-b.x,rz=tg.z-b.z,d=Math.hypot(rx,rz)||1,sp=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vz),k=Math.min(1,3.2*dt);b.vx+=((rx/d)*sp-b.vx)*k;b.vz+=((rz/d)*sp-b.vz)*k;b.vy=-(b.y-.8)/Math.max(.25,d/sp)}
    b.m.rotation.y=Math.atan2(b.vx,b.vz);
    const p=new THREE.Mesh(smkG,new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xcfcfcf,transparent:true,opacity:.6,depthWrite:false}));p.position.set(b.x-b.vx*.02,b.y,b.z-b.vz*.02);AG.add(p);SMK.push({p,l:.9})}
  for(let i=SMK.length-1;i>=0;i--){const e=SMK[i];e.l-=dt;e.p.scale.setScalar(1+(.9-e.l)*3);e.p.material.opacity=Math.max(0,e.l*.6);e.p.position.y+=dt*.6;if(e.l<=0){AG.remove(e.p);e.p.material.dispose();SMK.splice(i,1)}}}

function _ubx(o,x0,x1,z0,z1,r){x0-=r;x1+=r;z0-=r;z1+=r;if(o.x<=x0||o.x>=x1||o.z<=z0||o.z>=z1)return;
  const a=[o.x-x0,x1-o.x,o.z-z0,z1-o.z],m=Math.min(a[0],a[1],a[2],a[3]);
  if(m===a[0])o.x=x0;else if(m===a[1])o.x=x1;else if(m===a[2])o.z=z0;else o.z=z1}
function _ucr(o,cx,cz,R){const dx=o.x-cx,dz=o.z-cz,d2=dx*dx+dz*dz;if(d2>=R*R)return;const d=Math.sqrt(d2);if(d<.001){o.x=cx+R;return}o.x=cx+dx/d*R;o.z=cz+dz/d*R}
function _uworld(o,r,side){
  if(Math.abs(o.x-OX)<290&&Math.abs(o.z-OZ)<540)for(const s of SOLIDS){if(s.gate&&side==='A')continue;_ubx(o,s[0],s[1],s[2],s[3],r)}
  for(let k=0;k<FB.length;k++)if(Math.abs(o.x-FB[k][0])<44&&Math.abs(o.z-FB[k][1])<44)for(const s of FS[k])_ubx(o,s[0],s[1],s[2],s[3],r);
  for(const h of homesNear(o.x,o.z))_ubx(o,h.x-11,h.x+11,h.z-11,h.z+11,r);
  for(const h of plzSolids(o.x,o.z))_ubx(o,h.x-h.hx,h.x+h.hx,h.z-h.hz,h.z+h.hz,r);
  const i0=Math.round(o.x/CELL),j0=Math.round(o.z/CELL);
  if(i0>=-1&&j0>=-1&&i0<=NB&&j0<=NB){
    for(let i=i0-1;i<=i0+1;i++)for(let j=j0-1;j<=j0+1;j++){if(i<0||j<0||i>=NB||j>=NB)continue;const bx=i*CELL,bz=j*CELL;
      for(const q of[-18,0,18]){_ucr(o,bx+q,bz-HALF+2.5,.4+r);_ucr(o,bx-HALF+2.5,bz+q,.4+r)}}
    const a0=Math.round(o.x/CELL-.5),b0=Math.round(o.z/CELL-.5);
    for(let a=a0;a<=a0+1;a++)for(let b=b0;b<=b0+1;b++)for(const sx of[-1,1])for(const sz of[-1,1]){if((sx+sz)!==0&&((a+b)&1))continue;_ucr(o,rc(a)+sx*9.5,rc(b)+sz*9.5,.25+r)}
    _ubx(o,STORE.x+4.5,STORE.x+13.5,STORE.z-3.5,STORE.z+3.5,r)}
}
function unitCollide(u){
  if(u.tank){
    const fx=Math.sin(u.h),fz=Math.cos(u.h),R=1.9;
    for(const t of[3.1,0,-3.1]){const o={x:u.x+fx*t,z:u.z+fz*t},ox=o.x,oz=o.z;_uworld(o,R,u.side);u.x+=o.x-ox;u.z+=o.z-oz}
    // o tanque é pesado: empurra os carros do trânsito para fora do casco
    for(const ai of cars){if(Math.abs(ai.x-u.x)>8||Math.abs(ai.z-u.z)>8)continue;
      for(const t of[3.1,0,-3.1]){const c={x:ai.x,z:ai.z};_ucr(c,u.x+fx*t,u.z+fz*t,R+1.3);ai.x=c.x;ai.z=c.z}}
  }else{
    _uworld(u,.35,u.side);
    for(const ai of cars){if(Math.abs(ai.x-u.x)>6||Math.abs(ai.z-u.z)>6)continue;const fx=Math.sin(ai.h),fz=Math.cos(ai.h);
      for(const t of[1.4,0,-1.4])_ucr(u,ai.x+fx*t,ai.z+fz*t,1.2+.35)}
  }
}

function bSmart(u,f,dt){
  // inteligência tática: alerta o bando, cerca o alvo em grupo, desvia lateralmente e recua para se curar
  alertB=performance.now()+8000;
  const dx=f.x-u.x,dz=f.z-u.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1,nx=dx/d,nz=dz/d;let vx=0,vz=0;
  if(u.hp<u.mhp*.35){vx=-nx*1.2;vz=-nz*1.2;u.hp=Math.min(u.mhp,u.hp+4*dt)}          // ferido: recua e se trata
  else{
    const want=16+(u.i%3)*6,a=Math.atan2(dz,dx)+((u.i%5)-2)*.55;                     // cada um assume um ângulo (flanco)
    const gx=f.x-Math.cos(a)*want,gz=f.z-Math.sin(a)*want,ex=gx-u.x,ez=gz-u.z,ed=Math.hypot(ex,ez)||1;
    if(ed>2){vx=ex/ed;vz=ez/ed}
    const sg=Math.sin(performance.now()/700+u.i*1.7)>0?1:-1;vx+=-nz*.8*sg;vz+=nx*.8*sg;  // zigue-zague para não ser acertado
  }
  const m=Math.hypot(vx,vz)||1,sp=u.sp*1.3*dt;u.x+=vx/m*sp;u.z+=vz/m*sp}

function stepUnit(u,dt){
  if(u.dead){u.rt-=dt;if(u.rt<=0&&u.side==='B'&&!u.armored&&u.fv!==undefined&&performance.now()<PAC[u.fv]){u.rt=(PAC[u.fv]-performance.now())/1000+.5;return}if(u.rt<=0){u.dead=0;u.hp=u.mhp;u.x=u.hx;u.z=u.hz;u.g.rotation.set(0,0,0);u.g.position.set(u.x,.12,u.z)}return}
  if(u.hp<=0){if(u.drv)exitTank();u.dead=1;u.rt=(u.side==='B'&&!u.armored)?180:18;if(u.tank)u.g.rotation.z=.2;else{u.g.rotation.x=-1.5;u.g.position.y=.6}return}
  u.cd-=dt;if(u.drv){u.x=P.x;u.z=P.z;unitCollide(u);P.x=u.x;P.z=u.z;u.h=P.h;u.tc+=angDiff(u.tc,camYaw)*Math.min(1,8*dt);u.g.position.set(u.x,.12,u.z);u.g.rotation.y=u.h;u.tu.rotation.y=u.tc-u.h;return}
  const f=foe(u);let mv=!f;
  if(f){const ang=Math.atan2(f.x-u.x,f.z-u.z);
    if(u.tank){u.tc+=angDiff(u.tc,ang)*Math.min(1,4*dt);if(u.cd<=0&&Math.abs(angDiff(u.tc,ang))<.1){fire(u,f);u.cd=3}}
    else{u.h+=angDiff(u.h,ang)*Math.min(1,10*dt);if(u.cd<=0&&Math.abs(angDiff(u.h,ang))<.2){fire(u,f);u.cd=u.armored?.07+Math.random()*.05:(u.side==='B'?.3:.6)+Math.random()*(u.side==='B'?.4:.7)}}}
  if(f&&u.side==='B'&&!u.tank&&!u.armored)bSmart(u,f,dt);
  else if(u.side==='B'&&!u.armored&&u.hp<u.mhp)u.hp=Math.min(u.mhp,u.hp+8*dt);
  if(mv&&!u.tank&&u.side==='A'&&ARMY.mode!=='patrol'){const sl=ARMY.slot(u),dx=sl[0]-u.x,dz=sl[1]-u.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
    if(d>.6){const a=Math.atan2(dx,dz);u.h+=angDiff(u.h,a)*Math.min(1,8*dt);const v=Math.min(u.sp*(d>40?4:d>10?1.7:1)*dt,d);u.x+=Math.sin(u.h)*v;u.z+=Math.cos(u.h)*v}
    else u.h+=angDiff(u.h,sl[2])*Math.min(1,6*dt);
    mv=false}
  if(mv){
    if(u.tank){u.tx=u.wp[u.wi][0];u.tz=u.wp[u.wi][1]}
    const dx=u.tx-u.x,dz=u.tz-u.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
    if(d<2.5){if(u.tank)u.wi=(u.wi+1)%u.wp.length;else if(u.side==='B'){u.tx=u.hx+(Math.random()-.5)*6;u.tz=u.hz+(Math.random()-.5)*6}else{u.tx=u.hx+(Math.random()-.5)*30;u.tz=u.hz+(Math.random()-.5)*30}}
    else{const a=Math.atan2(dx,dz);u.h+=angDiff(u.h,a)*Math.min(1,(u.tank?2:8)*dt);if(!u.tank||Math.abs(angDiff(u.h,a))<.5){u.x+=Math.sin(u.h)*u.sp*dt;u.z+=Math.cos(u.h)*u.sp*dt}}
    if(u.tank)u.tc+=angDiff(u.tc,u.h)*Math.min(1,3*dt)}
  unitCollide(u);u.g.position.x=u.x;u.g.position.z=u.z;u.g.rotation.y=u.h;if(u.tank)u.tu.rotation.y=u.tc-u.h}
function stepBullets(dt){pcd-=dt;const now=performance.now(),hostA=(P.wanted>=3||now<angryA)&&!armyImmune(),tres=armyTrespass();
  for(let i=BULLETS.length-1;i>=0;i--){const b=BULLETS[i];b.x+=b.vx*dt;b.z+=b.vz*dt;b.life-=dt;if(b.vy)b.y=Math.max(.3,b.y+b.vy*dt);b.m.position.set(b.x,b.y,b.z);
    let hit=false;
    if(b.side==='B'&&ARMY.xh&&ARMY.xh.length){for(const h of ARMY.xh){if(h.dead||Math.hypot(h.x-b.x,h.z-b.z)>4.5)continue;hit=true;
      h.hp=(Number.isFinite(h.hp)?h.hp:2500)-(b.shell?150:14);
      if(h.hp<=0){h.dead=1;try{boom(h.x,h.z,'B',0);boom(h.x+3,h.z+3,'B',0)}catch(e){}scene.remove(h.g);const ix=ARMY.xh.indexOf(h);if(ix>=0)ARMY.xh.splice(ix,1);try{toast('Helicóptero do exército abatido','#ff8a8a',2200)}catch(e){}}
      break}}
    if(!hit)for(const o of U){if(o.dead||o.side===b.side)continue;if(Math.hypot(o.x-b.x,o.z-b.z)<o.r+.4){hit=true;if(!b.shell&&!o.armored)o.hp-=(o.tank?6:22)*(o.armor||1);break}}
    if(!hit&&b.side==='A'&&!b.pl){
      for(const p of PEDS){if(p.state==='hit')continue;if(Math.hypot(p.x-b.x,p.z-b.z)<1.1){p.hp=0;{p.state='hit';p.t=0;p.vx=b.vx*.04;p.vz=b.vz*.04}hit=true;break}}
      if(!hit)for(const c of cars){
        if(c.police){if(!(P.wanted>0||armyNear(c.x,c.z)))continue;if(Math.hypot(c.x-b.x,c.z-b.z)<2.6){c.hp=(Number.isFinite(c.hp)?c.hp:100)-(b.shell?100:34);hit=true;if(c.hp<=0){removeAI(c);if(policeNPC)setTimeout(()=>{if(started&&policeNPC)spawnAI(true)},12000)}break}continue}
        if(Math.hypot(c.x-b.x,c.z-b.z)<2.4&&b.shell){removeAI(c);setTimeout(()=>{if(started)spawnAI(false)},7000);hit=true;break}}
    }
    if(!hit&&!b.pl&&!P.tankU&&(b.side==='B'||hostA||(tres&&b.side==='A'))&&Math.hypot(P.x-b.x,P.z-b.z)<(P.onFoot?1.4:2.6)){hit=true;if(pcd<=0&&!P.god&&!vidaInfOn()&&!(!P.onFoot&&P.vehicle==='suv')){P.hp=Math.max(0,P.hp-(b.shell?25:5)*((!P.onFoot&&P.vehicle==='suv')?0:1));pcd=.35;
      {const X=window.__rotaX;if(X)X.health=Math.max(0,(Number.isFinite(X.health)?X.health:100)-(b.shell?50:10)*((!P.onFoot&&P.vehicle==='suv')?0:1))}try{window.__hurt&&window.__hurt()}catch(e){}}}
    if(hit||b.life<=0){if(b.shell){window.__tk=!!b.tk;window.__mk=b.mk||1;try{boom(b.x,b.z,b.side,b.pl)}finally{window.__tk=false;window.__mk=1}}AG.remove(b.m);BULLETS.splice(i,1)}}
  for(let i=FX.length-1;i>=0;i--){const e=FX[i];e.l-=dt;e.m.scale.setScalar(1+(.5-e.l)*12);e.m.material.opacity=Math.max(0,e.l*1.7);if(e.l<=0){AG.remove(e.m);FX.splice(i,1)}}}

const PLANES=[];let PCUR=null,lastPN=false,plRoll=0,plPitch=0;
function makePlane(x,z,h){
  const g=new THREE.Group(),SM=THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;g.rotation.order='YXZ';
  const wm=new SM({color:0xf2f4f7,roughness:.32,metalness:.35}),dm=new SM({color:0x141c28,roughness:.15,metalness:.7}),rm=new SM({color:0xc8102e,roughness:.45}),gm=new SM({color:0x25272b,roughness:.8}),mm=new SM({color:0x9aa3ad,roughness:.3,metalness:.8});
  const part=(geo,m,px,py,pz,rx,ry,rz)=>{const o=new THREE.Mesh(geo,m);o.position.set(px,py,pz);o.rotation.set(rx||0,ry||0,rz||0);g.add(o);return o};
  const H=Math.PI/2;
  part(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.5,1.5,14,28),wm,0,0,0,H);                    // fuselagem
  part(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.18,1.5,5.5,28),wm,0,0,9.7,H);                 // nariz
  part(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.5,.38,9,28),wm,0,.15,-11.5,H);                // cauda
  part(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.3,20,14),dm,0,.95,7.4).scale.set(.95,.55,1.5);   // cabine
  part(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.54,1.54,.9,28),rm,0,0,2.2,H);                // faixa vermelha
  for(let i=0;i<9;i++)for(const sx of[-1,1])part(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.06,.42,.6),dm,sx*1.49,.55,-5.4+i*1.2);   // janelas
  for(const sx of[-1,1]){
    part(new THREE.BoxGeometry(11.5,.26,4.8),wm,sx*6.6,-.55,-1.8,0,-sx*.32,sx*.05);   // asas
    part(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.2,1.6,1.6),rm,sx*12.1,.2,-4.3,0,0,sx*.15);           // winglet
    part(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.78,.78,4.2,20),mm,sx*3.5,-1.35,-.2,H);           // motores
    part(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.62,.62,.25,20),dm,sx*3.5,-1.35,1.95,H);         // entrada de ar
    part(new THREE.BoxGeometry(4.6,.2,2),wm,sx*3,.9,-12.2,0,-sx*.3,0);               // estabilizador
    part(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.18,1.2,.18),gm,sx*2.4,-2,-1.8);                      // trem principal
    part(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.55,.55,.42,16),gm,sx*2.4,-2.4,-1.8,0,0,H);      // rodas
  }
  part(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.26,4.4,3.8),wm,0,3.2,-11.6,-.3,0,0);                  // deriva
  part(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.28,1.6,1.8),rm,0,4.9,-12.6,-.3,0,0);
  part(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.16,1.1,.16),gm,0,-2,6.2);part(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.4,.4,.3,14),gm,0,-2.4,6.2,0,0,H);   // trem do nariz
  const beacon=part(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.22,10,8),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xff2222}),0,1.58,-2);
  const sh=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(9,24),blobM);sh.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;scene.add(sh);
  scene.add(g);const o={g,beacon,sh,x,z,h,y:0};PLANES.push(o);g.position.set(x,2.45+.2,z);g.rotation.y=h;return o}

function exitPlane(){const o=PCUR;if(!o)return;if(P.flyY>1.5||(P.pv||0)>8){toast('Pouse e pare o avião para sair','#ffb347',1600);return}

  P.x+=Math.cos(P.h)*7;P.z-=Math.sin(P.h)*7;ped.g.visible=!P.invisible;setLabels();toast('Saiu do avião','#fff',900)}

function planeStep(dt){
  if(PCUR){ped.g.visible=false;$('#bExit').textContent='SAIR DO AVIÃO';const k=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,((P.pv||0)-45)/20));
    camYaw+=angDiff(camYaw,P.h)*Math.min(1,2.5*dt);
    plRoll+=(Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,joy.x))*.55*Math.min(1,(P.pv||0)/40)*(P.flyY>1?1:0)-plRoll)*Math.min(1,3*dt);
    plPitch+=((inp.up-inp.down)*.3*k-plPitch)*Math.min(1,3*dt);
    PCUR.x=P.x;PCUR.z=P.z;PCUR.h=P.h;PCUR.y=P.flyY}
  else{plRoll*=.9;plPitch*=.9;const n=P.onFoot&&!P.inHouse&&!P.sitting&&!P.flying&&!P.tankU&&!!nearPlane();if(n)$('#bExit').textContent='ENTRAR NO AVIÃO';else if(lastPN)setLabels();lastPN=n}
  for(const o of PLANES){o.g.position.set(o.x,o.y+2.65,o.z);o.g.rotation.set(o===PCUR?-plPitch:0,o.h,o===PCUR?plRoll:0);
    o.beacon.visible=Math.floor(performance.now()/500)%2===0;o.sh.position.set(o.x,.25,o.z);o.sh.scale.setScalar(Math.max(.4,1-o.y/160));o.sh.visible=o.y<200}}
function pushS(s){if(s[4]!==undefined&&P.flyY>s[4]+1)return;if(s[5]!==undefined&&P.flyY<s[5])return;if(P.x>s[0]&&P.x<s[1]&&P.z>s[2]&&P.z<s[3]){const a=[P.x-s[0],s[1]-P.x,P.z-s[2],s[3]-P.z],m=Math.min(...a);if(m===a[0])P.x=s[0];else if(m===a[1])P.x=s[1];else if(m===a[2])P.z=s[2];else P.z=s[3];if(!P.onFoot)P.v=0}}
function pushC(cx,cz,R){
  const dx=P.x-cx,dz=P.z-cz,d2=dx*dx+dz*dz;if(d2>=R*R)return;
  const d=Math.sqrt(d2);if(d<.001){P.x=cx+R;return}
  P.x=cx+dx/d*R;P.z=cz+dz/d*R;if(!P.onFoot)P.v*=.6}
/* colisão varrida: carro rápido (até 166 m/s) cruzava o muro de 2 m num único quadro */
function sweepSolids(){
  const px=P._px,pz=P._pz;if(px===undefined)return;
  const dx=P.x-px,dz=P.z-pz,L=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(L<1||L>45)return;
  const pad=P.onFoot?.4:1.5;
  for(const s of SOLIDS){
    if(s[4]!==undefined&&P.flyY>s[4]+1)continue;
    if(s[5]!==undefined&&P.flyY<s[5])continue;
    const x0=s[0]-pad,x1=s[1]+pad,z0=s[2]-pad,z1=s[3]+pad;
    if(px>x0&&px<x1&&pz>z0&&pz<z1)continue;
    let t0=0,t1=1,ok=true;
    if(Math.abs(dx)<1e-6){if(px<=x0||px>=x1)ok=false}else{const a=(x0-px)/dx,b=(x1-px)/dx;t0=Math.max(t0,Math.min(a,b));t1=Math.min(t1,Math.max(a,b))}
    if(ok){if(Math.abs(dz)<1e-6){if(pz<=z0||pz>=z1)ok=false}else{const a=(z0-pz)/dz,b=(z1-pz)/dz;t0=Math.max(t0,Math.min(a,b));t1=Math.min(t1,Math.max(a,b))}}
    if(!ok||t0>t1||t0<=0)continue;
    const t=Math.max(0,t0-.02);P.x=px+dx*t;P.z=pz+dz*t;
    if(!P.onFoot){P.v*=.1;if(P.vh!==undefined)P.vh=P.h}
    return;
  }
}
function mobileObstacles(){
  if(P.tankU||P.heliD||P.planeD||P.flyY>3||P.inHouse)return;
  const ex=P.onFoot?.55:2.2;
  for(const u of U){if(u.dead||u.drv||(u.side==='A'&&!u.tank&&!P.onFoot))continue;if(Math.abs(u.x-P.x)>12||Math.abs(u.z-P.z)>12)continue;pushC(u.x,u.z,(u.tank?3.9:.7)+ex)}
  for(const o of HELIS){if(o.drv||o.y>2||Math.abs(o.x-P.x)>16||Math.abs(o.z-P.z)>16)continue;const fx=Math.sin(o.h),fz=Math.cos(o.h);
    pushC(o.x,o.z,2.6+ex);pushC(o.x+fx*4.4,o.z+fz*4.4,2.1+ex);pushC(o.x-fx*5,o.z-fz*5,1.8+ex);pushC(o.x-fx*8.5,o.z-fz*8.5,1+ex)}
  for(const o of PLANES){if(o===PCUR||o.y>3||Math.abs(o.x-P.x)>28||Math.abs(o.z-P.z)>28)continue;const fx=Math.sin(o.h),fz=Math.cos(o.h),rx=fz,rz=-fx;
    for(let t=-11;t<=9;t+=3.2)pushC(o.x+fx*t,o.z+fz*t,2+ex);
    for(const sd of[-1,1])for(let w=4;w<=11;w+=3.5)pushC(o.x+rx*sd*w-fx*1.8,o.z+rz*sd*w-fz*1.8,1.5+ex)}
}
addonStep=function(dt,time){dt=Math.min(dt,.05);for(let k=0;k<FB.length;k++)if(Math.abs(P.x-FB[k][0])<40&&Math.abs(P.z-FB[k][1])<40)for(const s of FS[k])pushS(s);
  try{if(P.tankU)ped.g.visible=false;{const nt=!P.tankU&&P.onFoot&&!P.inHouse&&!!nearTank();if(P.tankU||nt){$('#bExit').textContent=P.tankU?'SAIR DO TANQUE':'ENTRAR NO TANQUE'}else if(lastTN)setLabels();lastTN=P.tankU?false:nt}}catch(e){}
  try{heliStep(dt)}catch(e){}try{planeStep(dt)}catch(e){}
  for(const u of U){if(u.skip)continue;try{stepUnit(u,dt)}catch(e){}}
  try{stepBullets(dt)}catch(e){}
  {const inMil=Math.abs(P.x-OX)<270&&Math.abs(P.z-OZ)<520,inCB=Math.abs(P.x-CBX)<262&&Math.abs(P.z-CBZ)<272;
    if(inMil&&!seenIsle){seenIsle=true;toast('ILHA MILITAR','#7dff9b',1600)}else if(!inMil)seenIsle=false;
    if(inCB&&!seenCB){seenCB=true;toast('CASA BRANCA','#ffffff',2200)}else if(!inCB)seenCB=false;
    if(inMil||inCB){try{sweepSolids()}catch(e){}for(const s of SOLIDS)pushS(s);for(const c of (SOLIDS.circ||[]))pushC(c[0],c[1],c[2])}}
  try{mobileObstacles()}catch(e){}
  P._px=P.x;P._pz=P.z};
