const DIRS=[[0,1],[1,0],[0,-1],[-1,0]];
const LANE=3.6;
const civColors=[0xd9d9d9,0x3b4a66,0x8a2b2b,0x2f6b4a,0xc9a227,0x555a66,0x1f1f24,0x6a3d8a];
const cars=[];
function chooseDir(ai,chase){
  const opts=[];
  for(const d of DIRS){
    const na=ai.a+d[0],nb=ai.b+d[1];
    if(na<KMIN||na>KMAX||nb<KMIN||nb>KMAX)continue;
    if(d[0]===-ai.dx&&d[1]===-ai.dz)continue;
    opts.push(d);
  }
  if(!opts.length)opts.push([-ai.dx,-ai.dz]);
  let d;
  if(chase){
    let best=1e9;
    for(const o of opts){const s=Math.hypot(rc(ai.a+o[0])-P.x,rc(ai.b+o[1])-P.z);if(s<best){best=s;d=o}}
  }else{
    const st=opts.find(o=>o[0]===ai.dx&&o[1]===ai.dz);
    d=(st&&Math.random()<0.55)?st:opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0];
  }
  ai.dx=d[0];ai.dz=d[1];ai.a+=d[0];ai.b+=d[1];
}
function spawnAI(police){
  let a,b,tries=0;
  do{a=KMIN+((Math.random()*(KMAX-KMIN+1))|0);b=KMIN+((Math.random()*(KMAX-KMIN+1))|0);tries++}
  while(Math.hypot(rc(a)-P.x,rc(b)-P.z)<(police?45:60)&&tries<30);
  const car=makeCar(police?0xf4f4f4:civColors[(Math.random()*civColors.length)|0],police);
  const ai={car,police,a,b,dx:0,dz:1,x:rc(a),z:rc(b),h:0,v:0,vmax:police?14:rnd(10,15),t:0};
  const d=DIRS[(Math.random()*4)|0];ai.dx=d[0];ai.dz=d[1];ai.a-=0;
  // first target: neighbour in a valid direction
  let tries2=0;
  while((ai.a+ai.dx<KMIN||ai.a+ai.dx>KMAX||ai.b+ai.dz<KMIN||ai.b+ai.dz>KMAX)&&tries2++<8){const q=DIRS[(Math.random()*4)|0];ai.dx=q[0];ai.dz=q[1]}
  ai.a+=ai.dx;ai.b+=ai.dz;
  ai.h=Math.atan2(ai.dx,ai.dz);
  ai.x+=-ai.dz*LANE;ai.z+=ai.dx*LANE;
  car.g.position.set(ai.x,0,ai.z);scene.add(car.g);cars.push(ai);return ai;
}
function removeAI(ai){scene.remove(ai.car.g);cars.splice(cars.indexOf(ai),1)}
for(let i=0;i<16;i++)spawnAI(false);
let policeNPC=false;try{policeNPC=localStorage.getItem('rota-police-npc')==='1'}catch(e){}
function setPoliceNPC(on){policeNPC=on;try{localStorage.setItem('rota-police-npc',on?'1':'0')}catch(e){}for(const ai of cars.slice())if(ai.police)removeAI(ai);if(on)for(let i=0;i<4;i++)spawnAI(true)}
if(policeNPC)for(let i=0;i<4;i++)spawnAI(true);

/* ---------- pedestres ---------- */
const PEDS=[],CS=[[-1,-1],[1,-1],[1,1],[-1,1]];
const pedBodyG=new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.28,.24,.9,8),pedHeadG=new THREE.SphereGeometry(.2,8,6),pedLegG=new THREE.BoxGeometry(.12,.6,.14);
const pedSkins=[0xf3d2b5,0xe8b98f,0xd29a6e,0xb07a50,0x8a5a3a],pedShirts=[0xff3d81,0x2de2e6,0xffb347,0x2a6bff,0x7dff9b,0x8a5cff,0xeeeeee,0xc8102e],pedPants=[0x26324a,0x1b1b1f,0x4a3b2a,0x55606e];
const pickOf=a=>a[(Math.random()*a.length)|0];
const pedArmUpG=new THREE.BoxGeometry(.13,.3,.15).translate(0,-.15,0),pedArmLoG=new THREE.BoxGeometry(.11,.28,.13).translate(0,-.44,0),pedHandG=new THREE.BoxGeometry(.1,.1,.11).translate(0,-.62,0);
const pedShoeG=new THREE.BoxGeometry(.14,.07,.24),pedShortG=new THREE.BoxGeometry(.15,.3,.17),pedCalfG=new THREE.BoxGeometry(.1,.3,.12),pedHairG=new THREE.SphereGeometry(.215,8,6,0,Math.PI*2,0,Math.PI*.55),pedBeltG=new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.255,.255,.07,8),pedBrimG=new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.32,.32,.025,10),pedCapG=new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.2,.21,.14,10);
const pedHair=[0x15110f,0x3a2414,0x6b4423,0xc9a24a,0x8a1f1f,0x777777],pedShoes=[0x111111,0xf2f2f2,0x2a6bff,0xc8102e,0x4a3b2a];

const cornerIdx=(sx,sz)=>CS.findIndex(c=>c[0]===sx&&c[1]===sz);
function pedTarget(p){p.tx=p.i*CELL+CS[p.c][0]*29;p.tz=p.j*CELL+CS[p.c][1]*29}
function placePed(p,anywhere){
  p.hp=100;
  const cell=(c)=>clamp(Math.round(c/CELL)+(anywhere?0:((Math.random()*9)|0)-4),0,NB-1);
  p.i=cell(anywhere?rnd(0,NB*CELL):P.x);p.j=cell(anywhere?rnd(0,NB*CELL):P.z);
  const c0=(Math.random()*4)|0;p.dir=Math.random()<.5?1:-1;
  p.x=p.i*CELL+CS[c0][0]*29;p.z=p.j*CELL+CS[c0][1]*29;p.c=(c0+p.dir+4)%4;pedTarget(p);
  p.state='walk';p.t=0;p.g.rotation.x=0;
}
function spawnPed(){
  const g=new THREE.Group(),L=c=>new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:c});
  const skinC=pickOf(pedSkins),shirtC=pickOf(pedShirts),pantsC=pickOf(pedPants);
  const skin=L(skinC),shirt=L(shirtC),pants=L(pantsC),dark=L(0x151515);
  const body=new THREE.Mesh(pedBodyG,shirt);body.position.y=1.15;g.add(body);
  const belt=new THREE.Mesh(pedBeltG,L(0x1b1b1f));belt.position.y=.72;g.add(belt);
  const head=new THREE.Mesh(pedHeadG,skin);head.position.y=1.82;g.add(head);
  // cabelo / boné / chapéu
  const r=Math.random();
  if(r<.62){const h=new THREE.Mesh(pedHairG,L(pickOf(pedHair)));h.position.y=1.84;g.add(h)}
  else if(r<.82){const cm=L(pickOf(pedShirts));const cap=new THREE.Mesh(pedCapG,cm);cap.position.y=1.98;g.add(cap);const br=new THREE.Mesh(pedBrimG,cm);br.scale.set(.7,1,.9);br.position.set(0,1.92,.12);g.add(br)}
  // braços: manga curta ou comprida, com mão
  const longSleeve=Math.random()<.5,arms=[];
  for(const sx of[-.37,.37]){
    const pv=new THREE.Group();pv.position.set(sx,1.55,0);
    pv.add(new THREE.Mesh(pedArmUpG,shirt));
    pv.add(new THREE.Mesh(pedArmLoG,longSleeve?shirt:skin));
    pv.add(new THREE.Mesh(pedHandG,skin));
    g.add(pv);arms.push(pv);
  }
  // pernas: calça ou bermuda, com tênis
  const shorts=Math.random()<.3,shoeM=L(pickOf(pedShoes)),legs=[];
  for(const sx of[-.13,.13]){
    const pv=new THREE.Group();pv.position.set(sx,.72,0);
    if(shorts){const t=new THREE.Mesh(pedShortG,pants);t.position.y=-.15;pv.add(t);const c=new THREE.Mesh(pedCalfG,skin);c.position.y=-.45;pv.add(c)}
    else{const l=new THREE.Mesh(pedLegG,pants);l.position.y=-.3;pv.add(l)}
    const sh=new THREE.Mesh(pedShoeG,shoeM);sh.position.set(0,-.63,.04);pv.add(sh);
    g.add(pv);legs.push(pv);
  }
  scene.add(g);
  const p={g,legs,arms,sp:rnd(1.2,2),ph:Math.random()*6,h:0,i:0,j:0,c:0,dir:1,x:0,z:0,tx:0,tz:0,state:'walk',t:0,vx:0,vz:0};
  placePed(p,Math.random()<.3);PEDS.push(p);return p;
}
function setPedCount(k){while(PEDS.length<k)spawnPed();while(PEDS.length>k){const p=PEDS.pop();scene.remove(p.g)}}
function pedNext(p){
  if(Math.random()<.3){
    const sx=CS[p.c][0],sz=CS[p.c][1];let ni=p.i,nj=p.j,nc;
    if(Math.random()<.5){ni+=sx;nc=cornerIdx(-sx,sz)}else{nj+=sz;nc=cornerIdx(sx,-sz)}
    if(ni>=0&&nj>=0&&ni<NB&&nj<NB){p.i=ni;p.j=nj;p.c=nc;pedTarget(p);return}
  }
  p.c=(p.c+p.dir+4)%4;pedTarget(p);
}
function stepPeds(dt,time){
  const driving=!P.onFoot&&!P.inHouse&&!shopOpen;
  for(const p of PEDS){
    if(p.state==='hit'){
      p.t+=dt;p.x+=p.vx*dt;p.z+=p.vz*dt;const k=Math.max(0,1-3*dt);p.vx*=k;p.vz*=k;
      p.g.rotation.x=-Math.min(1.45,p.t*6);p.g.position.set(p.x,.1,p.z);
      if(p.t>3.5)placePed(p,false);
      continue;
    }
    const dx=p.tx-p.x,dz=p.tz-p.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
    if(d<.5)pedNext(p);else{p.h=Math.atan2(dx,dz);p.x+=dx/d*p.sp*dt;p.z+=dz/d*p.sp*dt}
    if(Math.abs(p.x-P.x)>340||Math.abs(p.z-P.z)>340){placePed(p,false);continue}
    p.g.position.set(p.x,.06,p.z);p.g.rotation.y=p.h;
    const sw=Math.sin(time*7+p.ph)*.6;p.legs[0].rotation.x=sw;p.legs[1].rotation.x=-sw;if(p.arms){p.arms[0].rotation.x=-sw*.9;p.arms[1].rotation.x=sw*.9}
    if(driving&&Math.abs(P.v)>5){
      const ex=p.x-P.x,ez=p.z-P.z;
      if(ex*ex+ez*ez<5.3){
        const sp=Math.abs(P.v),hd=(P.vh!==undefined?P.vh:P.h),sg=P.v>=0?1:-1;
        p.state='hit';p.t=0;p.vx=Math.sin(hd)*sg*sp*.7;p.vz=Math.cos(hd)*sg*sp*.7;
        P.v*=.88;playCrash(18);
        if(!P.god){if(P.wanted<3){P.wanted++;renderStars()}evade=0;const f=Math.min(300,Math.max(0,P.money));if(f>0)addMoney(-f,'Multa por atropelamento')}
        toast('ATROPELAMENTO','#ff3d81',1400);
      }
    }
  }
}
setPedCount(28);
const shotEffects=[];
let _shotT=0;
function playShot(gx,gz){
  if(!AC||!noiseBuf)return;
  let vol=1;
  if(Number.isFinite(gx)&&Number.isFinite(gz)){const d=Math.hypot(gx-P.x,gz-P.z);vol=Math.max(0,1-d/150);vol*=vol;if(vol<.03)return;
    const n=performance.now();if(n-_shotT<70)return;_shotT=n}
  const t=AC.currentTime,sr=AC.createBufferSource(),f=AC.createBiquadFilter(),g=AC.createGain();
  sr.buffer=noiseBuf;f.type='highpass';f.frequency.value=650;
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(.5*vol,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,t+.17);
  sr.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(AC.destination);sr.start();sr.stop(t+.2);
}
function shootTraffic(){if(P.suit&&P.onFoot&&!P.inHouse){suitBlast();return}if(P.tankU||P.heliD){playerTankFire();return}
  if(!HG()){toast('Você está sem arma na mochila.','#ffb347',1500);return}
  if(!gunOut){toast('Arma guardada. Toque no slot para sacar.','#ffb347',1500);return}
  if(!(LOOK&&LOOK.foto)){toast('Você está sem fuzil.','#ffb347',1500);return}
  if(!P.onFoot||P.inHouse||P.sitting){toast('Saia do carro e fique em pé na rua para atirar.','#ffb347',1500);return}
  const now=performance.now();if(now<shootCool)return;if(!gunWear())return;shootCool=now+gunCd();shootAim=.45;
  // Atira para onde a câmera aponta; o boneco vira para essa direção.
  const fwd=new THREE.Vector3(Math.sin(camYaw),0,Math.cos(camYaw));P.h=camYaw;
  const origin=new THREE.Vector3(P.x,walkSurfaceY(P.x,P.z)+P.flyY+P.jumpY+1.0,P.z);
  let target=null,kind='',best=95;const hitPoint=origin.clone().addScaledVector(fwd,95);
  // Mira assistida: o alvo precisa estar perto da linha de tiro (tolerância cresce um pouco com a distância).
  const test=(x,z,y,rad,obj,k)=>{const dx=x-P.x,dz=z-P.z,along=dx*fwd.x+dz*fwd.z;if(along<1||along>95)return;const lat=Math.abs(dx*fwd.z-dz*fwd.x);if(lat<rad+along*.06&&along<best){best=along;target=obj;kind=k;hitPoint.set(x,y,z)}};
  for(const ai of cars)test(ai.x,ai.z,.8,2.8,ai,'car');
  for(const p of PEDS)if(p.state!=='hit')test(p.x,p.z,1.2,.9,p,'ped');
  for(const u of U)if(!u.dead&&u.side!=='A')test(u.x,u.z,1.2,u.tank?3:.9,u,'unit');
  {const _ai=window.__aiTarget&&window.__aiTarget();if(_ai)test(_ai.x,_ai.z,1.2,.9,_ai,'ai')}
  const muzzle=origin.clone().addScaledVector(fwd,1.05);muzzle.x+=-.39*Math.cos(P.h);muzzle.z+=.39*Math.sin(P.h);
  
  const flash=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.07,8,6),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xff7a30,transparent:true,opacity:.9,depthTest:false,fog:false,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending}));flash.renderOrder=999;flash.position.copy(muzzle);scene.add(flash);shotEffects.push({obj:flash,life:.08,kind:'flash',max:.08});
  playShot();
  if(gunKind==='gold'){goldBeam(fwd,muzzle)}
  else if(gunKind==='boom'){boomBlast(hitPoint.x,hitPoint.z)}
  else if(kind==='car'){
    const boom=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.35,10,8),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xff7a24,transparent:true,opacity:.9}));boom.position.set(target.x,.8,target.z);scene.add(boom);shotEffects.push({obj:boom,life:.55,kind:'boom'});
    removeAI(target);setTimeout(()=>{if(started)spawnAI(false)},7000);toast('VEÍCULO DESTRUÍDO','#ffb347',900);addWanted(1);
  }else if(kind==='ai'){window.__aiHit&&window.__aiHit()
  }else if(kind==='unit'){target.hp-=(target.tank?25:35)*(target.armor||1);if(target.side==='A')angryA=performance.now()+40000;else angryB=performance.now()+40000;toast(target.hp<=0?'ALVO ELIMINADO':'ALVO ATINGIDO','#ff4d4d',700)}else if(kind==='ped'){
    target.hp=(Number.isFinite(target.hp)?target.hp:100)-34;const pedDead=target.hp<=0;if(pedDead){target.state='hit';target.t=0;target.vx=fwd.x*3;target.vz=fwd.z*3}
    const puff=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.12,8,6),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xb01212,transparent:true,opacity:.85}));puff.position.set(target.x,1.2,target.z);scene.add(puff);shotEffects.push({obj:puff,life:.55,kind:'boom'});
    toast(pedDead?'NPC ELIMINADO':'NPC ATINGIDO','#ff4d4d',900);if(pedDead)addWanted(1);
  }
}

  VS.dur=VS.dur||{};if(VS.dur[k]===undefined)VS.dur[k]=GDUR[k];
  if(VS.dur[k]<=0){toast('Arma quebrada!','#ff4d4d',1800);return false}
  VS.dur[k]--;
  if(VS.dur[k]<=0){
    if(k!=='std'){VS.owned=VS.owned.filter(x=>x!==k);delete VS.dur[k];VS.gun='std';gunKind='std';try{buildPed(LOOK)}catch(e){}toast('💥 '+GUNS[k].n+' quebrou!','#ff4d4d',2200)}

  }else if(VS.dur[k]<=10||VS.dur[k]%25===0)toast('Vida útil: '+VS.dur[k]+' tiros','#ffb347',900);
  vsave();return true}
function goldRevolver(G){
  while(G.children.length)G.remove(G.children[0]);
  /* texturas: ouro gravado e madrepérola */
  const mk=(w,h,fn)=>{const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=w;c.height=h;fn(c.getContext('2d'),w,h);const t=new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);t.wrapS=t.wrapT=THREE.RepeatWrapping;return t};
  const goldTex=mk(256,128,(x,w,h)=>{
    const g=x.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,h);g.addColorStop(0,'#fff0a8');g.addColorStop(.35,'#e8b52c');g.addColorStop(.7,'#b98512');g.addColorStop(1,'#f3cd52');
    x.fillStyle=g;x.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
    x.strokeStyle='rgba(95,60,0,.75)';x.lineWidth=1.6;
    for(let i=0;i<9;i++){const cx=14+i*30,cy=h/2+(i%2?12:-12);
      x.beginPath();x.arc(cx,cy,13,0,Math.PI*1.6);x.stroke();
      x.beginPath();x.arc(cx+8,cy,6,Math.PI*.4,Math.PI*2);x.stroke();
      x.beginPath();x.moveTo(cx+10,cy-14);x.quadraticCurveTo(cx+24,cy-30,cx+30,cy-6);x.stroke()}
    x.strokeStyle='rgba(255,240,170,.55)';x.lineWidth=1;
    for(let i=0;i<9;i++){const cx=14+i*30,cy=h/2+(i%2?12:-12);x.beginPath();x.arc(cx-1,cy-1,13,0,Math.PI*1.6);x.stroke()}
    x.fillStyle='rgba(80,50,0,.8)';x.fillRect(0,0,w,4);x.fillRect(0,h-4,w,4)});
  goldTex.anisotropy=4;
  const pearlTex=mk(128,128,(x,w,h)=>{
    x.fillStyle='#f4f1ea';x.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
    for(let i=0;i<420;i++){const a=Math.random();x.fillStyle=a<.5?'rgba(120,150,160,.55)':a<.8?'rgba(150,135,110,.45)':'rgba(255,255,255,.8)';x.fillRect(Math.random()*w,Math.random()*h,1+Math.random()*2.2,1+Math.random()*2.2)}});
  const gold=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xffffff,map:goldTex,emissive:0x5a3c00,emissiveIntensity:.55,metalness:.92,roughness:.2});
  const goldP=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xf2c545,emissive:0x5a3c00,emissiveIntensity:.5,metalness:.95,roughness:.14});
  const dk=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xa5780f,emissive:0x2a1c00,metalness:.85,roughness:.38});
  const pearl=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xffffff,map:pearlTex,emissive:0x2a2a28,metalness:.15,roughness:.4});
  const black=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x100c06,roughness:.7});
  const R=new THREE.Group();
  const add=(geo,m,x,y,z)=>{const o=new THREE.Mesh(geo,m);o.position.set(x,y,z);R.add(o);return o};
  const cyl=(r,l,m,x,y,z,seg)=>{const o=add(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r,r,l,seg||24),m,x,y,z);o.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;return o};
  /* armação: perfil lateral extrudado com bordas chanfradas (z = frente, y = cima) */
  const fs=new THREE.Shape();
  fs.moveTo(-.15,.07);fs.lineTo(.07,.07);fs.lineTo(.09,.045);fs.lineTo(.09,-.035);fs.lineTo(.045,-.04);
  fs.lineTo(.02,-.045);fs.bezierCurveTo(.005,-.1,-.045,-.1,-.06,-.045);fs.lineTo(-.1,-.03);

}

function goldOutline(){
  if(!ped||!ped.g)return;
  const add=[];
  ped.g.traverse(o=>{
    if(!o.isMesh||o.userData.goldOut||o.userData.hasOut)return;
    for(let q=o;q;q=q.parent){if(q===ped.gun)return;if(q===ped.g)break}
    add.push(o)});
  for(const o of add){

    m.scale.set(o.scale.x*1.12,o.scale.y*1.12,o.scale.z*1.12);
    if(o.parent)o.parent.add(m)}
  const tag=goldTag();if(tag.parent!==ped.g)ped.g.add(tag);tag.visible=!ownerTagHidden();
}

/* voo deitado: o corpo gira na horizontal (pivô no quadril) quando a armadura voa em movimento, com os braços à frente */
function suitPose(dt){
  const tgt=(P.flyY>2&&(P.hv||0)>3)?1.35:0;
  ped.sp=(ped.sp||0)+(tgt-(ped.sp||0))*Math.min(1,6*dt);
  const a=ped.sp,k=a/1.35;
  ped.body.rotation.set(a,0,0);ped.body.position.set(0,.9-.9*Math.cos(a),-.9*Math.sin(a));
  ped.armL.rotation.x=ped.armL.rotation.x*(1-k)+Math.PI*k;ped.armR.rotation.x=ped.armR.rotation.x*(1-k)+Math.PI*k;
  ped.legL.rotation.x*=(1-k);ped.legR.rotation.x*=(1-k);
}

function suitOff(quiet){
  if(!P.suit)return;
  P.suit=false;P.hero=!!P._sh;P.flying=!!P._sf;P.vidaInf=!!P._vi;
  ped.sp=0;ped.body.rotation.set(0,0,0);ped.body.position.set(0,0,0);
  buildPed(LOOK);
  document.getElementById('flyUp').classList.toggle('show',!!P.flying);document.getElementById('flyDown').classList.toggle('show',!!P.flying);
  syncWeaponUI();if(!quiet)toast('Armadura desativada','#4df2ff',1500);
}
function suitToggle(){
  if(P.suit){suitOff();return 'Armadura desativada'}
  if(!P.onFoot)return 'Saia do carro para vestir a armadura';
  if(P.inHouse)return 'Saia de casa para vestir a armadura';
  if(jailTime>0)return 'Função bloqueada durante a prisão';
  if(P.tankU||P.heliD||P.planeD)return 'Saia do veículo para vestir a armadura';
  P._sh=!!P.hero;P._sf=!!P.flying;P._vi=!!P.vidaInf;
  P.suit=true;P.hero=true;P.flying=true;P.vidaInf=true;
  const X=window.__rotaX;if(X)X.health=100;
  buildPed(LOOK);
  document.getElementById('flyUp').classList.add('show');document.getElementById('flyDown').classList.add('show');
  syncWeaponUI();phone.hidden=true;
  toast('ARMADURA TECH ATIVADA','#4df2ff',2200);
  return 'ARMADURA ATIVADA: joystick = voar · SUBIR/DESCER · CORRER = supersônico · ATIRAR (ou G) = raios · MÍSSIL = salva de 4 teleguiados · invulnerável';
}

/* TROVÃO ALTÍSSIMO (sintetizado, sem arquivo de áudio) */
function goldThunder(){try{
  const AC=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!AC)return;
  const c=window.__goldAC||(window.__goldAC=new AC());if(c.state==='suspended')c.resume();
  const t0=c.currentTime+.01;
  const comp=c.createDynamicsCompressor();comp.threshold.value=-24;comp.knee.value=6;comp.ratio.value=14;comp.attack.value=.001;comp.release.value=.25;
  const master=c.createGain();master.gain.value=2.2;master.connect(comp);comp.connect(c.destination);
  const noise=(sec)=>{const b=c.createBuffer(1,Math.floor(c.sampleRate*sec),c.sampleRate),d=b.getChannelData(0);for(let i=0;i<d.length;i++)d[i]=Math.random()*2-1;const n=c.createBufferSource();n.buffer=b;return n};
  const dist=(k)=>{const w=c.createWaveShaper(),cv=new Float32Array(1024);for(let i=0;i<1024;i++){const x=i/512-1;cv[i]=Math.tanh(x*k)}w.curve=cv;return w};
  // 1) estalo seco (o "CRACK")
  {const n=noise(.5),hp=c.createBiquadFilter(),g=c.createGain(),w=dist(8);hp.type='highpass';hp.frequency.value=900;
   g.gain.setValueAtTime(2.5,t0);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,t0+.45);n.connect(hp);hp.connect(w);w.connect(g);g.connect(master);n.start(t0);n.stop(t0+.5)}
  // 2) explosão grave
  {const n=noise(3),lp=c.createBiquadFilter(),g=c.createGain(),w=dist(3);lp.type='lowpass';lp.frequency.setValueAtTime(1800,t0+.05);lp.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(70,t0+2.4);
   g.gain.setValueAtTime(0,t0);g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(3,t0+.05);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,t0+3);n.connect(lp);lp.connect(w);w.connect(g);g.connect(master);n.start(t0);n.stop(t0+3)}
  // 3) pancada de subgrave
  {const o=c.createOscillator(),g=c.createGain();o.type='sine';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(85,t0);o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(24,t0+1.6);
   g.gain.setValueAtTime(0,t0);g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(3.2,t0+.03);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,t0+2);o.connect(g);g.connect(master);o.start(t0);o.stop(t0+2.1)}
  // 4) trovão rolando
  {const n=noise(4.5),lp=c.createBiquadFilter(),g=c.createGain(),lfo=c.createOscillator(),lg=c.createGain();lp.type='lowpass';lp.frequency.value=220;
   lfo.frequency.value=9;lg.gain.value=.8;lfo.connect(lg);lg.connect(g.gain);
   g.gain.setValueAtTime(0,t0+.15);g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(1.6,t0+.6);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,t0+4.5);n.connect(lp);lp.connect(g);g.connect(master);n.start(t0+.15);n.stop(t0+4.6);lfo.start(t0);lfo.stop(t0+4.6)}
}catch(e){}}
/* IMPACTO: cratera, rachaduras no piso, pedaços voando, poeira e tremor de tela */
function goldImpact(x,z){try{
  const G=new THREE.Group();scene.add(G);const anim=[];
  const bm=(c,o,add)=>new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:o,depthWrite:false,fog:false,blending:add?THREE.AdditiveBlending:THREE.NormalBlending});
  const cm=bm(0x0b0806,.92),gm=bm(0xff7a14,.9,true),km=bm(0x070504,.95),ge=bm(0xff9a2a,.85,true);
  // cratera
  const cr=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(4.6,44),cm);cr.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;cr.position.set(x,.13,z);G.add(cr);
  const cg=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(3.2,4.8,44),gm);cg.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;cg.position.set(x,.14,z);G.add(cg);
  // rachaduras
  const bx=new THREE.BoxGeometry(1,.04,1),N=16;
  for(let k=0;k<N;k++){
    let a=k/N*Math.PI*2+(Math.random()-.5)*.35,px=x,pz=z,len=7+Math.random()*10,done=0;
    while(done<len){const st=1+Math.random()*1.6;a+=(Math.random()-.5)*.7;const nx=px+Math.cos(a)*st,nz=pz+Math.sin(a)*st,w=.34*(1-done/len)+.07;
      const mx=(px+nx)/2,mz=(pz+nz)/2,ang=Math.atan2(nz-pz,nx-px);
      const m=new THREE.Mesh(bx,km);m.scale.set(st*1.05,1,w);m.position.set(mx,.16,mz);m.rotation.y=-ang;G.add(m);
      const g2=new THREE.Mesh(bx,ge);g2.scale.set(st*1.05,1,w*2.4);g2.position.set(mx,.15,mz);g2.rotation.y=-ang;G.add(g2);
      px=nx;pz=nz;done+=st}
  }
  // pedaços de piso voando
  const deb=[],dg=new THREE.BoxGeometry(1,1,1);
  for(let k=0;k<70;k++){const sz=.15+Math.random()*.55,m=new THREE.Mesh(dg,new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:[0x2b2b2b,0x3d3d3d,0x555555,0x1a1a1a][k%4],fog:false}));
    m.scale.set(sz,sz*(.5+Math.random()),sz);m.position.set(x+(Math.random()-.5)*3,.4,z+(Math.random()-.5)*3);
    const a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2,sp=5+Math.random()*13;m.userData={vx:Math.cos(a)*sp,vy:9+Math.random()*16,vz:Math.sin(a)*sp,rx:Math.random()*9,rz:Math.random()*9};G.add(m);deb.push(m)}
  // poeira
  const dust=[];for(let k=0;k<10;k++){const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1,10,8),bm(0x8a8070,.4));m.position.set(x+(Math.random()-.5)*5,1,z+(Math.random()-.5)*5);m.userData.s=1.5+Math.random()*1.5;G.add(m);dust.push(m)}
  // anel de choque
  const sw=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(.9,1.4,48),bm(0xffffff,.8,true));sw.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;sw.position.set(x,.2,z);G.add(sw);
  // tremor de tela
  const cv=(typeof renderer!=='undefined'&&renderer.domElement)?renderer.domElement:null;
  const T0=performance.now();let last=T0;
  const iv=setInterval(()=>{
    const now=performance.now(),t=(now-T0)/1000,dt=Math.min(.05,(now-last)/1000);last=now;
    for(const m of deb){const u=m.userData;u.vy-=34*dt;m.position.x+=u.vx*dt;m.position.y=Math.max(.1,m.position.y+u.vy*dt);m.position.z+=u.vz*dt;m.rotation.x+=u.rx*dt;m.rotation.z+=u.rz*dt;if(m.position.y<=.1){u.vx*=.6;u.vz*=.6;u.vy=0}}
    for(const m of dust){const k=1+t*m.userData.s*1.6;m.scale.setScalar(k*1.4);m.position.y=1+t*1.8;m.material.opacity=Math.max(0,.4*(1-t/2.2))}
    sw.scale.setScalar(1+t*26);sw.material.opacity=Math.max(0,.8*(1-t/.6));
    gm.opacity=Math.max(0,.9*(1-t/5));ge.opacity=Math.max(0,.85*(1-t/4));
    if(cv){if(t<1.1){const f=(1-t/1.1)**2*26;cv.style.transform='translate('+((Math.random()-.5)*f).toFixed(1)+'px,'+((Math.random()-.5)*f).toFixed(1)+'px)'}else cv.style.transform=''}
    if(t>22)cm.opacity=km.opacity=Math.max(0,.92*(1-(t-22)/4));
    if(t>=26){clearInterval(iv);if(cv)cv.style.transform='';scene.remove(G);G.traverse(o=>{if(o.geometry&&o.geometry!==bx&&o.geometry!==dg)o.geometry.dispose()});bx.dispose();dg.dispose();[cm,gm,km,ge].forEach(m=>m.dispose());deb.forEach(m=>m.material.dispose());dust.forEach(m=>m.material.dispose());sw.material.dispose()}
  },33);
}catch(e){}}
function goldLightning(x,z){try{
  const G=new THREE.Group();scene.add(G);goldThunder();setTimeout(()=>goldImpact(x,z),300);
  const cyl=new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1,1,1,6,1,true),up=new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0);
  const mat=(c,o)=>new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:o,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,depthWrite:false,fog:false});
  const mCore=mat(0xffffff,1),mMid=mat(0xffe14a,.55),mOut=mat(0xffa51a,.18),mats=[[mCore,1],[mMid,.55],[mOut,.18]];
  const segs=[];
  function seg(a,b,r,delay){
    const d=b.clone().sub(a),L=d.length();if(L<.01)return;
    const q=new THREE.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(up,d.clone().normalize()),mid=a.clone().add(b).multiplyScalar(.5);
    for(const [m,k] of [[mCore,.35],[mMid,1],[mOut,2.4]]){
      const c=new THREE.Mesh(cyl,m);c.scale.set(r*k,L,r*k);c.position.copy(mid);c.quaternion.copy(q);c.visible=false;c.userData.d=delay;G.add(c);segs.push(c)}
  }
  function path(start,end,n,jit,r,delay0,branch){
    const pts=[start.clone()];
    for(let k=1;k<n;k++){const t=k/n,p=start.clone().lerp(end,t);p.x+=(Math.random()-.5)*jit*(1-t*.3);p.z+=(Math.random()-.5)*jit*(1-t*.3);pts.push(p)}
    pts.push(end.clone());
    for(let k=0;k<pts.length-1;k++){
      seg(pts[k],pts[k+1],r,delay0+k*(branch?.012:.014));
      if(!branch&&k>1&&k<pts.length-2&&Math.random()<.5){
        const o=pts[k],e=o.clone();e.x+=(Math.random()-.5)*34;e.z+=(Math.random()-.5)*34;e.y=Math.max(4,o.y-18-Math.random()*26);
        path(o,e,5,5,r*.45,delay0+k*.014,true)}
    }
  }
  path(new THREE.Vector3(x+(Math.random()-.5)*10,95,z+(Math.random()-.5)*10),new THREE.Vector3(x,1.9,z),16,9,.22,0,false);
  // clarão e onda no chão
  const fl=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1,16,12),mat(0xffffff,.95));fl.position.set(x,1.9,z);fl.visible=false;G.add(fl);
  const rg=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(.8,1.2,40),mat(0xffe14a,.9));rg.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;rg.position.set(x,.15,z);rg.visible=false;G.add(rg);
  const ov=document.createElement('div');ov.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;background:#fff;opacity:0;pointer-events:none;z-index:9999;transition:opacity .08s';document.body.appendChild(ov);
  toast('⚡ UM RAIO CAIU NA CABEÇA!','#fff27a',2200);
  const T0=performance.now(),DUR=1900,HIT=.3;let flashed=false;
  const iv=setInterval(()=>{
    const t=(performance.now()-T0)/1000;
    for(const c of segs)c.visible=t>=c.userData.d;
    if(t>=HIT){fl.visible=rg.visible=true;
      if(!flashed){flashed=true;ov.style.opacity='.75';setTimeout(()=>ov.style.opacity='0',110)}
      const u=Math.min(1,(t-HIT)/1.2);fl.scale.setScalar(1.2+u*3.2);fl.material.opacity=.95*(1-u);rg.scale.setScalar(1+u*14);rg.material.opacity=.9*(1-u)}
    // pisca e some
    const f=t<.7?1:Math.max(0,1-(t-.7)/1.2),fk=(t>.45&&t<.7)?(Math.floor(t*40)%2?.35:1):1;
    mats.forEach(([m,o])=>m.opacity=o*f*fk);
    if(t*1000>=DUR){clearInterval(iv);scene.remove(G);cyl.dispose();fl.geometry.dispose();rg.geometry.dispose();mats.forEach(([m])=>m.dispose());fl.material.dispose();rg.material.dispose();ov.remove()}
  },33);
}catch(e){}}
function goldErase(kind,tg){
  try{goldLightning(kind==='player'?tg.m.position.x:tg.x,kind==='player'?tg.m.position.z:tg.z)}catch(e){}
  const px=kind==='player'?tg.m.position.x:tg.x,pz=kind==='player'?tg.m.position.z:tg.z;
  const boom=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.5,12,10),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xffd24a,transparent:true,opacity:.95,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,depthWrite:false}));
  boom.position.set(px,1.2,pz);scene.add(boom);shotEffects.push({obj:boom,life:.55,kind:'boom'});
  if(kind==='ped'){const i=PEDS.indexOf(tg);if(i>=0)PEDS.splice(i,1);scene.remove(tg.g);setTimeout(()=>{if(started)spawnPed()},8000)}
  else if(kind==='car'){removeAI(tg);setTimeout(()=>{if(started)spawnAI(false)},7000)}
  else if(kind==='unit'){tg.hp=0;if(tg.side==='A')angryA=performance.now()+40000;else angryB=performance.now()+40000;toast('ALVO ELIMINADO','#ffd24a',700)}
  else if(kind==='player'){try{window.__goldHitPlayer(tg)}catch(e){console.error('goldHitPlayer',e)}}
}

function vStatus(it){
  if(it.t==='gun'){if(gunKind===it.id)return it.id==='gold'?'EQUIPADA':'EQUIPADA ('+durLeft(it.id)+')';if(it.id==='std'||VS.owned.includes(it.id))return it.id==='gold'?'EQUIPAR':'EQUIPAR ('+durLeft(it.id)+')';return fmtC(it.p)}
  if(it.t==='ammo')return fmtC(it.p)+' · você tem '+(VS.ammoGold||0);
  if(it.t==='perm'){if(VS.owned.includes(it.id))return TOG[it.id]?(TOG[it.id]()?'DESLIGAR':'LIGAR'):'ATIVA';return fmtC(it.p)}
  return fmtC(it.p)
}

// fogo dourado no personagem (partículas de chama que sobem do corpo)
let FIRE=null,fireT=0;
function updateGoldFire(on,g){
  if(!FIRE){
    const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=64;const x=c.getContext('2d');
    const gr=x.createRadialGradient(32,32,2,32,32,30);gr.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,255,230,1)');gr.addColorStop(.3,'rgba(255,214,70,.9)');gr.addColorStop(.65,'rgba(255,150,20,.45)');gr.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,100,0,0)');
    x.fillStyle=gr;x.fillRect(0,0,64,64);
    const tex=new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);FIRE=[];
    for(let i=0;i<70;i++){const sp=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:tex,transparent:true,depthWrite:false,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,fog:false}));sp.visible=false;sp.userData={life:0,max:1,vy:0,vx:0,vz:0,s:1};scene.add(sp);FIRE.push(sp)}
  }
  const now=performance.now(),dt=Math.min(.05,(now-fireT)/1000||.016);fireT=now;
  let spawn=on?3:0;
  for(const sp of FIRE){
    const u=sp.userData;
    if(u.life<=0){
      if(spawn>0){spawn--;const a=Math.random()*6.283,r=Math.random()*.55;
        sp.position.set(g.x+Math.cos(a)*r,g.y+.05+Math.random()*1.7,g.z+Math.sin(a)*r);
        u.life=u.max=.55+Math.random()*.5;u.vy=1.6+Math.random()*1.8;u.vx=(Math.random()-.5)*.5;u.vz=(Math.random()-.5)*.5;u.s=.7+Math.random()*.7;sp.visible=true}
      else sp.visible=false;
      continue}
    u.life-=dt;const k=Math.max(0,u.life/u.max);
    sp.position.x+=u.vx*dt;sp.position.y+=u.vy*dt;sp.position.z+=u.vz*dt;
    sp.scale.setScalar(u.s*(.35+k*.9));
    sp.material.opacity=Math.min(1,k*1.6);
    sp.material.color.setHSL(.09+k*.04,1,.5+k*.2);
    if(u.life<=0)sp.visible=false;
  }
}
// anel de luz dourada (sempre ligado, segue o boneco a cada quadro)
let ringA=null,ringB=null;
function updateGoldRing(){
  if(!ringA){
    const mat=()=>new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xffd24a,transparent:true,opacity:.9,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,depthWrite:false,fog:false});
    ringA=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.85,.06,8,40),mat());ringB=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.05,.03,8,40),mat());
    ringA.rotation.x=ringB.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;scene.add(ringA);scene.add(ringB)}
  const on=!!(ped&&ped.g&&ped.g.visible&&P.onFoot&&!P.inHouse&&!P.invisible);
  const pg=ped&&ped.g?ped.g.position:{x:0,y:0,z:0};
  ringA.visible=ringB.visible=on;updateGoldFire(false,pg);if(!on)return; // fogo removido; círculo centrado no boneco
  const t=performance.now()/1000,g=pg,y=g.y+.12;
  ringA.position.set(g.x,y,g.z);ringB.position.set(g.x,y+.05,g.z);
  ringA.scale.setScalar(1+Math.sin(t*4)*.06);ringB.scale.setScalar(1+Math.sin(t*4+1.5)*.08);
  ringB.material.opacity=.45+Math.sin(t*4)*.2;
}

function vBuy(it){
  const X=window.__rotaX||{};
  if(it.t==='gun'){
    if(!(it.id==='std'||VS.owned.includes(it.id))){if(!vPay(it))return;VS.owned.push(it.id);if(it.id==='gold'){try{admOk=true;syncAdm();toast('Você agora é ADM supervisionado (sem dinheiro e sem banir)','#ffd24a',2600)}catch(e){}}}
    if(it.id==='std'&&VS.dur)delete VS.dur.std;
    gunKind=it.id;VS.gun=it.id;vsave();buildPed(LOOK);vmsg(it.n+' equipada.');return}
  if(it.t==='ammo'){if(!vPay(it))return;VS.ammoGold=(VS.ammoGold||0)+1;vsave();try{bgRender()}catch(e){}vmsg('Munição Dourada comprada! Você tem '+VS.ammoGold+'.');return}
  if(it.t==='perm'){
    if(VS.owned.includes(it.id)){
      if(it.id==='rainbow'){VS.rb=!VS.rb;vsave();if(!VS.rb)me.bodyM.color.setHex(P.color);vmsg(VS.rb?'Arco-íris ligado.':'Arco-íris desligado.')}
      else vmsg('Já ativo.');return}
    if(!vPay(it))return;VS.owned.push(it.id);if(it.id==='boots')VBOOTS=true;if(it.id==='rainbow')VS.rb=true;vsave();vmsg(it.n+' adquirido!');return}
  if(it.id==='kit'){if(!vPay(it))return;bagAdd('kit');vmsg('Kit Médico guardado na mochila.');return}
  if(it.id==='paint')return vCar(it,0xd4af37,'Carro dourado!');
  if(it.id==='paintp')return vCar(it,0xff2d95,'Carro rosa choque!');
  if(it.id==='paintb')return vCar(it,0x111111,'Carro preto fosco!');
  if(it.id==='crown'){if(!vPay(it))return;bagAdd('crown');vmsg('Coroa guardada na mochila.');return}
  if(it.id==='chain'){if(!vPay(it))return;bagAdd('chain');vmsg('Corrente guardada na mochila.');return}
  if(it.id==='goldfit'){if(!vPay(it))return;bagAdd('goldfit');vmsg('Roupa dourada guardada na mochila.');return}
}

  const tx=String(m&&m.text||'');if(tx.slice(0,3)!=='#H1')return false;
  if(Number.isFinite(m.t)&&m.t<HM.t0)return true;
  let q;try{q=JSON.parse(tx.slice(3))}catch(e){return true}
  if(!q||typeof q!=='object'||q.to===undefined||String(q.to)!==String(PLAYER_ID))return true;
  const from=String(m.id);if(from===String(PLAYER_ID)||!/^\d{1,6}$/.test(from))return true;
  const key=from+'|'+q.n+'|'+q.t;if(HM.seen.has(key))return true;HM.seen.add(key);if(HM.seen.size>400)HM.seen.clear();
  const rsp=o=>{try{window.__hmSend(Object.assign({to:from,n:q.n},o))}catch(e){}};
  try{
  // ---- dono: recebe pedidos ----

    const fr=HS.friends.includes(from);

      if(!P.house){rsp({t:'vr',ok:0,why:'esse jogador não tem casa'});return true}

    if(!fr){rsp({t:'cr',a:q.t,ok:0,f:0,c:{}});return true}
    if(q.t==='snap'){rsp({t:'cr',a:'snap',ok:1,f:1,c:BG.chest});return true}

    if(q.t==='take'){const have=BG.chest[id]||0,give=Math.min(n,have);if(give>0){BG.chest[id]=have-give;if(BG.chest[id]<=0)delete BG.chest[id];bgSave();bgRender()}
      rsp({t:'cr',a:'take',ok:give>0?1:0,f:1,id,q:give,c:BG.chest});if(give>0)toast('ID '+from+' pegou '+give+'× '+BAGI[id].n+' do seu baú','#ffb347',2400);return true}
    if(q.t==='put'){BG.chest[id]=(BG.chest[id]||0)+n;bgSave();bgRender();rsp({t:'cr',a:'put',ok:1,f:1,id,q:n,c:BG.chest});toast('ID '+from+' guardou '+n+'× '+BAGI[id].n+' no seu baú','#7dff9b',2400);return true}
    return true}
  // ---- visitante: recebe respostas ----
  const pd=HM.pend.get(q.n);
  if(q.t==='vr'){if(!pd||pd.a!=='visit'||String(pd.owner)!==from)return true;HM.pend.delete(q.n);
    const mm=document.getElementById('casaMsg');
    if(q.ok){if(mm)mm.textContent='';enterVisit(from,!!q.f,q.c)}else{const w='Não deu para entrar: '+String(q.why||'recusado').slice(0,60);if(mm)mm.textContent=w;toast(w,'#ffb347',2600)}
    return true}
  if(q.t==='cr'){
    const mine=HV.on&&HV.owner===from;
    if(pd&&String(pd.owner)===from){HM.pend.delete(q.n);
      if(pd.a==='take'){if(q.ok&&Number(q.q)>0&&String(q.id)===pd.id)bagAdd(pd.id,Math.min(Math.floor(Number(q.q)),pd.q));else toast('Esse item não está mais no baú','#ffb347',1800)}
      else if(pd.a==='put'&&!q.ok){bagAdd(pd.id,pd.q);toast('O dono não aceitou: item devolvido','#ffb347',2000)}}
    if(mine){HV.chest=hmClean(q.c);HV.friend=!!q.f;bgRender()}
    return true}
  }catch(e){}
  return true};

let laserS=null;
function updateLaserSight(){
  const on=!!(LOOK&&LOOK.foto&&gunOut&&HG()&&P.onFoot&&!P.inHouse&&!P.sitting&&!shopOpen&&started);if(ped&&ped.gun)ped.gun.visible=gunOut&&HG();
  if(!laserS){
    const mk=(rt,rb,col,op)=>{const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(rt,rb,1,6,1,true),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:col,transparent:true,opacity:op,depthTest:false,fog:false,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,side:THREE.DoubleSide}));m.renderOrder=999;m.visible=false;scene.add(m);return m};
    const dot=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.05,8,6),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xff3a2a,transparent:true,opacity:.95,depthTest:false,fog:false,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending}));dot.renderOrder=999;dot.visible=false;scene.add(dot);
    laserS={glow:mk(.03,.03,0xff1a1a,.55),core:mk(.01,.01,0xffe8e0,1),dot};
  }
  const L=laserS;L.glow.visible=L.core.visible=L.dot.visible=false;if(!on)return; // laser de mira removido
  // arma sempre na horizontal, apontando para a frente do boneco; o laser sai da ponta do cano
  if(ped.gun&&ped.armL)ped.gun.rotation.x=-ped.armL.rotation.x;
  ped.g.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  const st=new THREE.Vector3(0,0,.72);if(ped.gun)ped.gun.localToWorld(st);else st.set(P.x,walkSurfaceY(P.x,P.z)+1,P.z);
  const fwd=new THREE.Vector3(Math.sin(P.h),0,Math.cos(P.h));
  const sy=st.y;
  let best=60;
  const test=(x,z,rad)=>{const dx=x-st.x,dz=z-st.z,al=dx*fwd.x+dz*fwd.z;if(al<1||al>best)return;const lat=Math.abs(dx*fwd.z-dz*fwd.x);if(lat<rad+al*.06)best=al};
  for(const ai of cars)test(ai.x,ai.z,2.8);
  for(const p of PEDS)if(p.state!=='hit')test(p.x,p.z,.9);
  const end=new THREE.Vector3(st.x+fwd.x*best,sy,st.z+fwd.z*best);
  const d=end.clone().sub(st),len=Math.max(.1,d.length());d.normalize();
  const q=new THREE.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0),d);
  const boost=shootAim>0?1.6:1;
  for(const m of [L.glow,L.core]){m.scale.set(boost,len,boost);m.position.copy(st).addScaledVector(d,len/2);m.quaternion.copy(q)}
  L.dot.position.copy(end);
}

addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.target&&e.target.tagName==='INPUT')return;if(e.key==='1')toggleGun()});
function updateShotEffects(dt){updateLaserSight();try{updateGoldRing()}catch(e){}for(let i=shotEffects.length-1;i>=0;i--){const e=shotEffects[i];if(e.kind==='ray'){e.t+=dt;e.life-=dt;const st=e.start.clone();
    const d=e.end.clone().sub(st),L=Math.max(.01,d.length());d.normalize();const k=Math.min(1,e.t/.04),len=L*k,qq=new THREE.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0),d),f=Math.max(0,e.life/e.max);
    for(const m of [e.obj,e.core]){m.scale.set(1,len,1);m.position.copy(st).addScaledVector(d,len/2);m.quaternion.copy(qq)}
    e.obj.material.opacity=.75*f;e.core.material.opacity=f;
    if(e.life<=0){for(const m of [e.obj,e.core]){scene.remove(m);m.geometry.dispose();m.material.dispose()}shotEffects.splice(i,1)}continue}
  e.life-=dt;if(e.kind==='boom'){const k=1+(0.55-e.life)*1.2;e.obj.scale.setScalar(k);e.obj.material.opacity=Math.max(0,e.life/.55)}else if(e.max){e.obj.material.opacity=Math.max(0,e.life/e.max)}if(e.life<=0){scene.remove(e.obj);e.obj.geometry.dispose();e.obj.material.dispose();shotEffects.splice(i,1)}}}

function stepAI(ai,dt,time){
  const along=ai.dx!==0?ai.x:ai.z;
  const tgt=ai.dx!==0?rc(ai.a):rc(ai.b);
  const dirc=ai.dx!==0?ai.dx:ai.dz;
  if((tgt-along)*dirc<=0.5)chooseDir(ai,ai.police&&P.wanted>0);
  // desired speed, slow for anything ahead
  let want=ai.police&&P.wanted>0?115+P.wanted*4:ai.vmax;
  const check=(ox,oz)=>{
    const rx=ox-ai.x,rz=oz-ai.z,fwd=rx*ai.dx+rz*ai.dz,lat=Math.abs(-rx*ai.dz+rz*ai.dx);
    if(fwd>0&&fwd<12&&lat<2.8)want=Math.min(want,Math.max(0,(fwd-5.5)*1.6));
  };
  if(!(ai.police&&P.wanted>0))check(P.x,P.z);
  if(P.onFoot)check(parked.x,parked.z);
  for(const o of cars)if(o!==ai)check(o.x,o.z);
  ai.v+=clamp(want-ai.v,-45*dt,(ai.police&&P.wanted>0?70:16)*dt);
  ai.x+=ai.dx*ai.v*dt;ai.z+=ai.dz*ai.v*dt;
  const k=Math.min(1,3*dt);
  if(ai.dx!==0)ai.z+=(rc(ai.b)+ai.dx*LANE-ai.z)*k;else ai.x+=(rc(ai.a)-ai.dz*LANE-ai.x)*k;
  ai.h+=angDiff(ai.h,Math.atan2(ai.dx,ai.dz))*Math.min(1,6*dt);
  const g=ai.car.g;g.position.set(ai.x,0,ai.z);g.rotation.y=ai.h;
  for(const w of ai.car.wheels)w.spin.rotation.x+=ai.v*dt/0.38;
  if(ai.car.bar){const on=Math.sin(time*14)>0&&P.wanted>0;ai.car.bar.r.visible=on;ai.car.bar.b.visible=!on&&P.wanted>0}
}

let camMode=0,camH=0,camYaw=0,camPitch=.12;
function cycleCam(){camMode=(camMode+1)%3;toast(['Câmera: perseguição','Câmera: distante','Câmera: capô'][camMode],'#fff',900)}
function updateCamera(dt){
  if(shopOpen){const a=P.h+shYaw,hf=["hair","chain","head","hat","glasses","ear","neck"].includes(shCur),d=hf?2.2:3.8,ty=hf?1.7:1.1;camera.position.set(P.x+Math.sin(a)*d,ty+.15,P.z+Math.cos(a)*d);camera.lookAt(P.x,ty,P.z);return}
  if(P.onFoot)camH=camYaw;else camH+=angDiff(camH,P.h)*Math.min(1,5*dt);
  const sp=Math.abs(P.v);
  // Dentro da casa, usar câmera em primeira pessoa para enxergar o interior.
  if(P.inHouse){
    const eyeY=1.62;
    camera.position.set(P.x,eyeY,P.z);
    camera.lookAt(P.x+Math.sin(camYaw)*12,eyeY-0.08+(.12-camPitch)*12,P.z+Math.cos(camYaw)*12);
  }else if(camMode===2&&!P.onFoot){
    camera.position.set(P.x+Math.sin(P.h)*0.9,1.6,P.z+Math.cos(P.h)*0.9);
    camera.lookAt(P.x+Math.sin(P.h)*30,1.3,P.z+Math.cos(P.h)*30);
  }else{
    // A pé a câmera fica bem mais perto do boneco; de carro mantém a distância original.
    const foot=P.onFoot;
    const back=P.planeD?(camMode===0?32:48):P.heliD?(camMode===0?21:32):P.tankU?(camMode===0?16:24):foot?(camMode===0?3.4:6.5):(camMode===0?9.5:17),up=P.tankU?(camMode===0?7:10):foot?(camMode===0?1.8:3.4):(camMode===0?4.4:8.5);
    if(foot){
      const cp=Math.cos(camPitch),sn=Math.sin(camPitch),py=(P.planeD?5:P.heliD?3.4:P.tankU?3:1.4)+P.flyY;
      camera.position.set(P.x-Math.sin(camH)*back*cp,Math.max(.35,py+back*sn),P.z-Math.cos(camH)*back*cp);
      camera.lookAt(P.x+Math.sin(camH)*2,py,P.z+Math.cos(camH)*2);
    }else{
    camera.position.set(P.x-Math.sin(camH)*back,up+P.flyY,P.z-Math.cos(camH)*back);
    camera.lookAt(P.x+Math.sin(camH)*(foot?2:5),(foot?1.4:1.6)+P.flyY,P.z+Math.cos(camH)*(foot?2:5));
    }
  }
  const fov=(P.fovBase||62)+(P.suit?Math.min(30,sp*.1):sp*0.45);
  if(Math.abs(camera.fov-fov)>0.2){camera.fov=fov;camera.updateProjectionMatrix()}
}

/* ---------- audio ---------- */
let AC=null,eng,engG,hornO,hornG,sirO,sirG,noiseBuf,scrG,rainG;
let curGear=1,rpm=.2,shiftT=0;
function updateGear(dt){
  shiftT=Math.max(0,shiftT-dt);
  if(P.onFoot||P.inHouse){rpm=.2;curGear=1;return}
  const ref=Math.max(VEHICLES[P.vehicle].max*(P.tuned?1.35:1),40),r=clamp(Math.abs(P.v)/ref,0,1),gf=r*6,g=clamp(1+Math.floor(gf),1,6);
  if(g!==curGear){if(inp.gas&&g>curGear)shiftT=.14;curGear=g}
  const within=r>=1?1:gf-Math.floor(gf);
  rpm+=(.22+.78*within+(inp.gas?.04:0)-rpm)*Math.min(1,10*dt);
}
function playCrash(power){
  if(!AC||!noiseBuf)return;
  const t=AC.currentTime,s=AC.createBufferSource(),f=AC.createBiquadFilter(),g=AC.createGain();
  s.buffer=noiseBuf;f.type='lowpass';f.frequency.value=900;
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(clamp(power/40,.08,.5),t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,t+.35);
  s.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(AC.destination);s.start();s.stop(t+.4);
}
function playThunder(){
  if(!AC||!noiseBuf)return;
  const t=AC.currentTime,s=AC.createBufferSource(),f=AC.createBiquadFilter(),g=AC.createGain();
  s.buffer=noiseBuf;s.loop=true;f.type='lowpass';f.frequency.value=260;
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(.4,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,t+2.2);
  s.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(AC.destination);s.start();s.stop(t+2.3);
}
function initAudio(){
  try{
    AC=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const lp=AC.createBiquadFilter();lp.type='lowpass';lp.frequency.value=700;lp.connect(AC.destination);
    eng=AC.createOscillator();eng.type='sawtooth';engG=AC.createGain();engG.gain.value=0.05;eng.connect(engG);engG.connect(lp);eng.start();
    // ---- Som do Nissan Skyline R34 (RB26 6 cilindros em linha + turbo), sintetizado ----
    {const lp2=AC.createBiquadFilter();lp2.type='lowpass';lp2.frequency.value=2600;lp2.Q.value=1.2;
     r34G=AC.createGain();r34G.gain.value=0;lp2.connect(r34G);r34G.connect(AC.destination);
     r34A=AC.createOscillator();r34A.type='sawtooth';r34A.connect(lp2);r34A.start();
     r34B=AC.createOscillator();r34B.type='square';const bg=AC.createGain();bg.gain.value=.5;r34B.connect(bg);bg.connect(lp2);r34B.start();
     r34T=AC.createOscillator();r34T.type='sine';r34TG=AC.createGain();r34TG.gain.value=0;r34T.connect(r34TG);r34TG.connect(AC.destination);r34T.start()}
    hornG=AC.createGain();hornG.gain.value=0;const hlp=AC.createBiquadFilter();hlp.type='lowpass';hlp.frequency.value=1600;hlp.connect(hornG);hornG.connect(AC.destination);
    hornO=AC.createOscillator();hornO.type='square';hornO.frequency.value=350;hornO.connect(hlp);hornO.start();
    const hornO2=AC.createOscillator();hornO2.type='square';hornO2.frequency.value=440;hornO2.connect(hlp);hornO2.start();
    sirO=AC.createOscillator();sirO.type='triangle';sirG=AC.createGain();sirG.gain.value=0;sirO.connect(sirG);sirG.connect(AC.destination);sirO.start();
    noiseBuf=AC.createBuffer(1,AC.sampleRate,AC.sampleRate);{const nd=noiseBuf.getChannelData(0);for(let i=0;i<nd.length;i++)nd[i]=Math.random()*2-1}
    scrG=AC.createGain();scrG.gain.value=0;const scrF=AC.createBiquadFilter();scrF.type='bandpass';scrF.frequency.value=1800;scrF.Q.value=4;
    const scrS=AC.createBufferSource();scrS.buffer=noiseBuf;scrS.loop=true;scrS.connect(scrF);scrF.connect(scrG);scrG.connect(AC.destination);scrS.start();
    {const rs=AC.createBufferSource();rs.buffer=noiseBuf;rs.loop=true;const hp=AC.createBiquadFilter();hp.type='highpass';hp.frequency.value=1500;rainG=AC.createGain();rainG.gain.value=0;rs.connect(hp);hp.connect(rainG);rainG.connect(AC.destination);rs.start()}
  }catch(e){AC=null}
}
let r34G,r34A,r34B,r34T,r34TG,_r34Gas=0,_r34Shift=0,_r34Pop=0,_r34Fire=0,_r34Last=0,_r34Lim=0;
function r34Burst(freq,vol,dur,type){if(type!=='highpass')_r34Fire=Math.min(1.6,Math.max(_r34Fire,vol*4.5));const t=AC.currentTime,s=AC.createBufferSource(),f=AC.createBiquadFilter(),g=AC.createGain();
  s.buffer=noiseBuf;f.type=type||'lowpass';f.frequency.value=freq;g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,t+dur);
  s.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(AC.destination);s.start(t);s.stop(t+dur+.05)}
function r34Pop(vol,big){if(!AC||!noiseBuf)return;_r34Fire=Math.min(1.6,Math.max(_r34Fire,vol*2.2));const t=AC.currentTime,dur=big?.16:.09;
  const s=AC.createBufferSource(),f=AC.createBiquadFilter(),g=AC.createGain();
  s.buffer=noiseBuf;s.playbackRate.value=.8+Math.random()*.6;f.type='bandpass';f.frequency.value=900+Math.random()*1800;f.Q.value=.7;
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(Math.min(1,vol*2.4),t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,t+dur);
  s.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(AC.destination);s.start(t,Math.random()*.5);s.stop(t+dur+.05);
  const o=AC.createOscillator(),og=AC.createGain();o.type='triangle';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(190+Math.random()*90,t);o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(45,t+dur);
  og.gain.setValueAtTime(Math.min(1,vol*1.6),t);og.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,t+dur);o.connect(og);og.connect(AC.destination);o.start(t);o.stop(t+dur+.05)}
function updateAudio(time,nearPolice){
  if(!AC)return;
  const t=AC.currentTime;
  if(r34G){
    const on=P.vehicle==='r34'&&!P.onFoot&&!P.inHouse&&P.fuel>0&&P.hp>0&&started;
    const base=(38+rpm*90)*(shiftT>0?.82:1)+inp.gas*8;
    r34A.frequency.setTargetAtTime(base*1.5,t,.04);r34B.frequency.setTargetAtTime(base*.75,t,.04);
    r34G.gain.setTargetAtTime(on?.10+inp.gas*.10:0,t,.08);
    r34T.frequency.setTargetAtTime(1400+rpm*2600,t,.1);
    r34TG.gain.setTargetAtTime(on?Math.max(0,inp.gas-.35)*.012*Math.min(1,rpm*1.4):0,t,.15);
    if(on){
      if(_r34Gas>.6&&inp.gas<.25&&rpm>.3){r34Burst(4200,.07,.4,'highpass');r34Burst(900,.1,.2)} // blow-off
      if(shiftT>0&&_r34Shift<=0){r34Pop(.6,true);if(Math.random()<.7)setTimeout(()=>{if(AC)r34Pop(.4,false)},90+Math.random()*80)} // estouros no câmbio
      if(inp.gas<.2&&rpm>.45&&time>_r34Pop){_r34Pop=time+.12+Math.random()*.25;if(Math.random()<.55)r34Pop(.35,Math.random()<.3)} // pipocos na desaceleração
    }
    if(on&&inp.gas>.5&&rpm>.9&&time>_r34Lim){ /* LIMITADOR: metralhadora de pipocos + fogo */
      _r34Lim=time+.04+Math.random()*.06;
      if(Math.random()<.92){r34Pop(.4+Math.random()*.2,Math.random()<.3);
        if(Math.random()<.5)setTimeout(()=>{if(AC)r34Pop(.3,false)},20+Math.random()*30)}
    }
    _r34Gas=inp.gas;_r34Shift=shiftT;
    // fogo saindo do escapamento
    const dtf=Math.min(.1,Math.max(0,time-_r34Last));_r34Last=time;
    _r34Fire=Math.max(0,_r34Fire-dtf*5);
    const fls=me&&me.g&&me.g.userData&&me.g.userData.flames;
    if(fls)for(const fl of fls){
      const k=on?_r34Fire:0;
      fl.visible=k>.02;
      if(fl.visible){const fk=k*(.75+Math.random()*.5);fl.scale.set(1+Math.random()*.3,fk*1.6*fl.userData.ln,1+Math.random()*.3);fl.material.opacity=Math.min(1,k*1.3)}
    }
  }
  eng.frequency.setTargetAtTime((38+rpm*90)*(shiftT>0?.82:1)+inp.gas*8,t,0.05);
  engG.gain.setTargetAtTime(P.fuel>0&&P.hp>0&&!P.onFoot&&P.vehicle!=='r34'?(0.14+inp.gas*0.09)*(shiftT>0?.45:1):0,t,0.1);
  if(rainG)rainG.gain.setTargetAtTime(wx.rain*(P.inHouse?.012:.045),t,.4);
  if(scrG)scrG.gain.setTargetAtTime((!P.onFoot&&Math.abs(P.v)>10&&((P.slip||0)>.22||(inp.hand&&Math.abs(P.v)>12)))?clamp(.08+(P.slip||0)*.2,.08,.25):0,t,.05);
  hornG.gain.setTargetAtTime(inp.horn&&!P.onFoot&&!P.inHouse&&!P.sitting?0.22:0,t,0.015);
  sirO.frequency.setTargetAtTime(Math.sin(time*5)>0?880:660,t,0.02);
  sirG.gain.setTargetAtTime(P.wanted>0?clamp(0.07*(1-nearPolice/160),0,0.07):0,t,0.1);
}

/* ---------- HUD ---------- */
const el={clock:$('#clock'),money:$('#money'),stars:$('#stars'),kmh:$('#kmh'),fuel:$('#fuel i'),hp:$('#hp i'),job:$('#job'),toast:$('#toast'),district:$('#district'),pt:$('#pt')};
let toastTimer=0;
const TOAST_SO_IMPORTANTES=true; // mude para false para voltar a mostrar todas as mensagens
const TOAST_OK=/^(Carro reparado|Carro consertado|Carro já está inteiro|Saldo insuficiente)/; // o aviso [DECRETADO] da Dourada usa outro sistema (__decreeBanner) e continua aparecendo
function toast(txt,color,ms){if(TOAST_SO_IMPORTANTES&&!TOAST_OK.test(String(txt)))return;el.toast.textContent=txt;el.toast.style.color=color||'#fff';el.toast.classList.add('on');clearTimeout(toastTimer);toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>el.toast.classList.remove('on'),ms||1800)}
const fmt=n=>'$ '+Math.round(n).toLocaleString('pt-BR');
const DISTRICTS=[['Praia Norte','Mirante','Alto da Serra'],['Vila Esperança','Centro','Distrito Financeiro'],['Porto','Bairro Operário','Zona Industrial']];
let lastDistrict='',districtT=0;
function districtAt(x,z){
  const span=CELL*NB/3;
  const c=clamp(Math.floor((x+HALF)/span),0,2),r=clamp(Math.floor((z+HALF)/span),0,2);
  return DISTRICTS[r][c];
}
function renderStars(){let s='';for(let i=0;i<5;i++)s+=i<P.wanted?'<b>★</b>':'★';el.stars.innerHTML=s}

/* minimap */
const mctx=$('#map').getContext('2d');const MS=0.9,MR=116;
function drawMap(){
  const g=mctx,W=232,cxm=W/2,cym=W/2;
  g.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);g.fillStyle='#1b212c';g.fillRect(0,0,W,W);
  const fx=Math.sin(P.h),fz=Math.cos(P.h),rx=-Math.cos(P.h),rz=Math.sin(P.h);
  const a=rx*MS,b=-fx*MS,c=rz*MS,d=-fz*MS,e=cxm-(a*P.x+c*P.z),f=cym-(b*P.x+d*P.z);
  g.setTransform(a,b,c,d,e,f);
  g.fillStyle='#3a4359';
  {const ci=Math.round(P.x/CELL),cj=Math.round(P.z/CELL);for(let i=Math.max(0,ci-3);i<=Math.min(NB-1,ci+3);i++)for(let j=Math.max(0,cj-3);j<=Math.min(NB-1,cj+3);j++){const bx=i*CELL,bz=j*CELL;if(Math.abs(bx-P.x)<170&&Math.abs(bz-P.z)<170)g.fillRect(bx-HALF,bz-HALF,HALF*2,HALF*2)}}
  g.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
  const toMap=(x,z,clampEdge)=>{
    const dx=x-P.x,dz=z-P.z;let sx=(dx*rx+dz*rz)*MS,sy=-(dx*fx+dz*fz)*MS;
    const dist=Math.hypot(sx,sy),lim=MR-10;
    if(dist>lim){if(!clampEdge)return null;sx*=lim/dist;sy*=lim/dist}
    return [cxm+sx,cym+sy];
  };
  const dot=(x,z,col,r,edge)=>{const p=toMap(x,z,edge);if(!p)return;g.fillStyle=col;g.beginPath();g.arc(p[0],p[1],r,0,7);g.fill()};
  GAS.forEach(([ga,gb])=>dot(rc(ga),rc(gb),'#7dff9b',5,false));dot(STORE.x,STORE.z,'#6fc3ff',5,false);
  if(P.onFoot)dot(parked.x,parked.z,'#ff3d81',5,true);
  if(gps)dot(gps.x,gps.z,'#ffb347',6,true);
  if(window.__rotaMapDots)try{window.__rotaMapDots().forEach(q=>dot(q[0],q[1],q[2],q[3],true))}catch(e){}
  if(job){const t=job.stage===0?job.pick:job.drop;dot(rc(t.a),rc(t.b),'#2de2e6',7,true)}
  for(const ai of cars)if(ai.police&&P.wanted>0)dot(ai.x,ai.z,Math.sin(performance.now()/120)>0?'#ff3030':'#3a7bff',5,true);
  g.fillStyle='#ff3d81';g.beginPath();g.moveTo(cxm,cym-9);g.lineTo(cxm+6.5,cym+7);g.lineTo(cxm,cym+3);g.lineTo(cxm-6.5,cym+7);g.closePath();g.fill();
}

/* ---------- jobs, gps, phone ---------- */
let job=null,gps=null;
const randInt=()=>KMIN+((Math.random()*(KMAX-KMIN+1))|0);
function randomPoint(minDist,fromX,fromZ){let a,b,n=0;do{a=randInt();b=randInt();n++}while(Math.hypot(rc(a)-fromX,rc(b)-fromZ)<minDist&&n<40);return{a,b}}
const CARGO=[
  {n:'Encomenda comum',pay:1,time:1,fragile:0,xp:30},
  {n:'Carga frágil',pay:1.5,time:1.15,fragile:1,xp:50},
  {n:'Entrega urgente',pay:1.8,time:.7,fragile:0,xp:60},
  {n:'Carga pesada',pay:1.4,time:1.1,fragile:0,xp:45}];
function startJob(){
  const pick=randomPoint(90,P.x,P.z),drop=randomPoint(260,rc(pick.a),rc(pick.b));
  const dist=Math.hypot(rc(drop.a)-rc(pick.a),rc(drop.b)-rc(pick.b));
  const toPick=Math.hypot(rc(pick.a)-P.x,rc(pick.b)-P.z);
  const cargo=CARGO[(Math.random()*CARGO.length)|0];
  job={stage:0,pick,drop,cargo,crashes:0,pay:Math.round((120+dist*0.75)*cargo.pay*(1+levelFromXP(getXP())*0.03)),t:Math.round((toPick/9+14)*cargo.time)};
  toast('Entrega aceita: '+cargo.n,'#2de2e6');syncPhone();
}
function cancelJob(fine){
  job=null;beamJob.visible=false;el.job.hidden=true;
  if(fine){addMoney(-50,'Entrega cancelada');toast('Entrega cancelada −$50','#ff3d81')}
  syncPhone();
}
function stepJob(dt){
  if(!job){beamJob.visible=false;return}
  const tg=job.stage===0?job.pick:job.drop,tx=rc(tg.a),tz=rc(tg.b);
  beamJob.visible=true;beamJob.position.x=tx;beamJob.position.z=tz;
  beamJob.material.color.setHex(job.stage===0?0x2de2e6:0xff3d81);
  job.t-=dt;
  const dist=Math.hypot(tx-P.x,tz-P.z);
  if(dist<9&&Math.abs(P.v)<14){
    if(job.stage===0){
      job.stage=1;job.t=Math.round((Math.hypot(rc(job.drop.a)-tx,rc(job.drop.b)-tz)/9+20)*job.cargo.time);
      toast('Encomenda coletada','#2de2e6');
    }else{
      const bonus=Math.max(0,Math.round(job.t*3));
      let base=job.pay;if(job.cargo.fragile)base=Math.round(base*Math.max(.4,1-job.crashes*.2));
      const clean=job.crashes===0?Math.round(job.pay*.25):0,total=base+bonus+clean;
      addMoney(total,'Entrega concluída ('+job.cargo.n+')');
      addXP(job.cargo.xp+(clean?15:0)+Math.min(40,Math.round(job.t)));
      toast('Entrega concluída +'+fmt(total)+(clean?' · sem batidas':''),'#7dff9b',2600);
      job=null;beamJob.visible=false;el.job.hidden=true;syncPhone();return;
    }
  }
  if(job.t<=0){addMoney(-50,'Entrega atrasada');toast('Tempo esgotado −$50','#ff3d81',2200);job=null;beamJob.visible=false;el.job.hidden=true;syncPhone();return}
  el.job.hidden=false;
  const m=Math.floor(Math.max(0,job.t)/60),s=Math.floor(Math.max(0,job.t)%60);
  el.job.style.borderLeftColor=job.stage===0?'#2de2e6':'#ff3d81';
  el.job.innerHTML=(job.stage===0?'Retirar: ':'Entregar: ')+job.cargo.n+'<small>'+(job.crashes?job.crashes+' batida(s) · ':'')+Math.round(dist)+' m · '+m+':'+String(s).padStart(2,'0')+' · '+fmt(job.pay)+'</small>';
}
function stepGps(){
  if(!gps){beamGps.visible=false;return}
  beamGps.visible=true;beamGps.position.x=gps.x;beamGps.position.z=gps.z;
  if(Math.hypot(gps.x-P.x,gps.z-P.z)<9){gps=null;beamGps.visible=false;toast('Você chegou','#ffb347')}
}
const phone=$('#phone');
function togglePhone(){phone.hidden=!phone.hidden;if(!phone.hidden)showView('home');syncPhone();if(typeof syncWeaponUI==='function')syncWeaponUI()}
function showView(n){document.querySelectorAll('#phone .view').forEach(v=>v.hidden=v.id!=='v-'+n);if(typeof syncWeaponUI==='function')syncWeaponUI()}
phone.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>e.stopPropagation());
phone.querySelectorAll('[data-app]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{const n=b.dataset.app;if(n==='close'){phone.hidden=true}else{showView(n);syncPhone();if(n==='identity')syncIdentity();if(n==='support')syncSupportStatus();if(n==='market')syncTuneMarket()}}));
function syncTuneMarket(){
  const installed=P.tuned&&P.nitroInstalled;
  $('#tuneStatus').textContent=installed?'KIT ESPORTIVO E NITRO INSTALADOS':'Modificações não instaladas';
  $('#buyTune').textContent=installed?'KIT JÁ INSTALADO':'INSTALAR KIT ESPORTIVO + NITRO · GRÁTIS';
  $('#buyTune').disabled=installed;
  const remove=$('#removeTune');if(remove)remove.disabled=!installed;
}

  if(!P.tuned&&!P.nitroInstalled){toast('Você não tem kit instalado','#fff',1200);return}
  P.tuned=false;P.nitroInstalled=false;inp.nitro=0;persist();syncTuneMarket();
  toast('Kit esportivo e nitro removidos do veículo.','#ffb347',1800);
});

  if(P.tuned&&P.nitroInstalled){toast('Kit já instalado','#fff',1200);return}
  P.tuned=true;P.nitroInstalled=true;persist();syncTuneMarket();
  toast('Kit instalado! Segure NITRO para usar a potência extra.','#7dff9b',2200);
});

});

// Identidade persistente deste personagem neste navegador.
const identityName=$('#identityName'),identityId=$('#identityId'),identityPhone=$('#identityPhone'),identityProfession=$('#identityProfession');
function getIdentity(){
  let name=PLAYER_ID==='1'?'Matyas':'Jogador',phoneNumber='',profession=PLAYER_ID==='1'?'ADM':'Cidadão',level=1;
  try{name=localStorage.getItem('rota-player-name')||name;phoneNumber=localStorage.getItem('rota-player-phone')||'';profession=localStorage.getItem('rota-player-role')||(PLAYER_ID==='1'?'ADM':'Cidadão');level=levelFromXP(getXP())}catch(e){}
  if(PLAYER_ID==='1'){name='Matyas';profession='ADM'}
  return {name,phoneNumber,profession,level};
}
function syncIdentity(){
  const d=getIdentity();identityName.value=d.name;identityId.value=PLAYER_ID;identityPhone.value=d.phoneNumber;
  const known=[...identityProfession.options].some(o=>o.value===d.profession||o.textContent===d.profession);
  identityProfession.value=known?d.profession:'Cidadão';if(PLAYER_ID==='1')identityProfession.value='ADM';
  $('#identityLevel').textContent=String(d.level);
  $('#identityCard').innerHTML='<b>ROTA RP · IDENTIDADE</b><br>Nome: '+escapeIdentity(d.name)+'<br>ID: '+escapeIdentity(PLAYER_ID)+'<br>Telefone: '+escapeIdentity(d.phoneNumber||'Não cadastrado')+'<br>Profissão: '+escapeIdentity(PLAYER_ID==='1'?'ADM':d.profession)+'<br>Nível: '+d.level+' (XP '+getXP()+')';
}
function escapeIdentity(v){return String(v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]))}
// Chamados locais de suporte: guarda a posição do jogador e permite ao ADM aceitar e teleportar.
function readSupportCalls(){try{const a=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('rota-adm-support-calls')||'[]');return Array.isArray(a)?a:[]}catch(e){return []}}
function saveSupportCalls(a){try{localStorage.setItem('rota-adm-support-calls',JSON.stringify(a.slice(-30)))}catch(e){}}
$('#supportSend').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  const reason=$('#supportReason').value.trim();if(reason.length<5){$('#supportStatus').textContent='Explique o problema com pelo menos 5 caracteres.';return}
  const calls=readSupportCalls(),identity=getIdentity();
  if(calls.some(c=>String(c.id)===String(PLAYER_ID)&&c.status==='pendente')){$('#supportStatus').textContent='Você já tem um chamado aguardando atendimento.';return}
  calls.push({callId:'call-'+Date.now()+'-'+Math.floor(Math.random()*10000),id:PLAYER_ID,name:identity.name,reason,x:P.x,z:P.z,status:'pendente',priority:($('#supportPriority')||{}).value||'normal',createdAt:Date.now()});saveSupportCalls(calls);
  $('#supportReason').value='';$('#supportStatus').textContent='Chamado enviado. Aguarde um ADM.';toast('Chamado enviado à administração','#2de2e6',1600);renderSupportQueue();
});

function enterPrison(seconds){
  if(!P.onFoot)exitCar();
  P.inHouse=false;homeInterior.visible=false;homeExterior.visible=true;P.flying=false;P.invisible=false;P.flyY=0;ped.g.visible=true;
  jailTime=Math.max(1,Math.floor(seconds));P.x=PRISON.x;P.z=PRISON.z+20;P.h=Math.PI;P.v=0;camYaw=P.h;camH=P.h;ped.g.position.set(P.x,0,P.z);ped.g.rotation.y=P.h;
  $('#jailStatus').hidden=false;phone.hidden=true;setLabels();toast('ENCAMINHADO AO PRESÍDIO','#ffb347',2200);
}
function releasePrison(){jailTime=0;P.x=PRISON.x+75;P.z=PRISON.z+70;P.v=0;P.h=0;camYaw=0;camH=0;ped.g.position.set(P.x,0,P.z);$('#jailStatus').hidden=true;toast('PENA CUMPRIDA · LIBERADO','#7dff9b',2600)}
function stepPrison(dt){
  if(jailTime<=0)return;
  jailTime=Math.max(0,jailTime-dt);
  const d=Math.hypot(P.x-PRISON.x,P.z-PRISON.z);
  if(d>PRISON.r){P.x=PRISON.x;P.z=PRISON.z+20;P.v=0;ped.g.position.set(P.x,0,P.z);toast('FUGA DETECTADA · RETORNO AO PRESÍDIO','#ffb347',1800)}
  const mm=Math.floor(jailTime/60),ss=Math.floor(jailTime%60);
  $('#jailStatus').textContent='PRESÍDIO · TEMPO RESTANTE '+mm+':'+String(ss).padStart(2,'0');
  if(jailTime<=0)releasePrison();
}

/* ---------- physics ---------- */
function collideBlocks(o,r){
  let hit=0;
  // Só os prédios reais bloqueiam o carro; ruas, calçadas e lotes vazios ficam livres.
  for(const h of homesNear(o.x,o.z)){
    const minX=h.x-11.5-r,maxX=h.x+11.5+r,minZ=h.z-11.5-r,maxZ=h.z+11.5+r;
    if(o.x<minX||o.x>maxX||o.z<minZ||o.z>maxZ)continue;
    const options=[
      {d:Math.abs(o.x-minX),v:minX,axis:'x'},
      {d:Math.abs(maxX-o.x),v:maxX,axis:'x'},
      {d:Math.abs(o.z-minZ),v:minZ,axis:'z'},
      {d:Math.abs(maxZ-o.z),v:maxZ,axis:'z'}
    ].sort((a,b)=>a.d-b.d);
    const q=options[0];
    if(q.axis==='x')o.x=q.v;else o.z=q.v;
    const into=-(Math.sin(o.h)* (q.axis==='x'?(q.v===minX?-1:1):0) + Math.cos(o.h)*(q.axis==='z'?(q.v===minZ?-1:1):0))*o.v;
    if(into>hit)hit=into;
  }
  // Esculturas, palmeiras, postes, colunas e bancos das praças também são sólidos.
  for(const h of plzSolids(o.x,o.z)){
    const minX=h.x-h.hx-r,maxX=h.x+h.hx+r,minZ=h.z-h.hz-r,maxZ=h.z+h.hz+r;
    if(o.x<minX||o.x>maxX||o.z<minZ||o.z>maxZ)continue;
    const options=[
      {d:Math.abs(o.x-minX),v:minX,axis:'x'},
      {d:Math.abs(maxX-o.x),v:maxX,axis:'x'},
      {d:Math.abs(o.z-minZ),v:minZ,axis:'z'},
      {d:Math.abs(maxZ-o.z),v:maxZ,axis:'z'}
    ].sort((a,b)=>a.d-b.d);
    const q=options[0];
    if(q.axis==='x')o.x=q.v;else o.z=q.v;
    const into=-(Math.sin(o.h)*(q.axis==='x'?(q.v===minX?-1:1):0)+Math.cos(o.h)*(q.axis==='z'?(q.v===minZ?-1:1):0))*o.v;
    if(into>hit)hit=into;
  }
  return hit;
}
const _PLZ_NONE=[];
function plzSolids(x,z){const i=Math.round(x/CELL),j=Math.round(z/CELL);if(i<0||j<0||i>=NB||j>=NB)return _PLZ_NONE;return PLZ.sol.get(i*NB+j)||_PLZ_NONE}
const _homeList=[];
function homesNear(x,z){const i=Math.round(x/CELL),j=Math.round(z/CELL);_homeList.length=0;if(i>=0&&j>=0&&i<NB&&j<NB){const h=HOME_GRID.get(i*NB+j);if(h&&!h.mansion)_homeList.push(h)}return _homeList}
let hitCool=0,evade=0,bust=0,policeCool=0,refuelAcc=0,prevCrash=0,damageCooldown=0;
function hurt(into){
  if(P.god||damageCooldown>0)return;
  if(into>16){P.hp=Math.max(0,P.hp-Math.min(1.5,(into-16)*0.07)*(P.vehicle==='suv'?0:1));damageCooldown=0.8;prevCrash=0.4;playCrash(into);if(job)job.crashes++}
}
/* ===== MODO DRIFT do Nissan R34: desliza mais e paga R$ 10 a cada 1 cm de drift ===== */
const DRIFT_PAY_PER_M=0.1,DRIFT={on:false,run:0,earn:0,idle:0,acc:0,save:0};
{const css=document.createElement('style');css.textContent='#bDrift{left:max(14px,env(safe-area-inset-left,0px));bottom:calc(150px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px));width:96px;height:46px;border-radius:14px;font-size:14px;display:none}#bDrift.on{background:rgba(53,130,255,.65);border-color:#7db4ff}#driftHud{position:fixed;z-index:4;top:calc(64px + env(safe-area-inset-top,0px));left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);display:none;text-align:center;font:700 22px Oswald,sans-serif;letter-spacing:.06em;color:#7db4ff;-webkit-text-stroke:1px #000;paint-order:stroke fill;text-shadow:2px 2px 0 #000;pointer-events:none}#driftHud small{display:block;font-size:14px;color:#fff}';document.head.appendChild(css);
 const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='ctl';b.id='bDrift';b.type='button';b.textContent='DRIFT: OFF';document.body.appendChild(b);
 const hud=document.createElement('div');hud.id='driftHud';document.body.appendChild(hud);
 const tg=e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();DRIFT.on=!DRIFT.on;b.classList.toggle('on',DRIFT.on);b.textContent='DRIFT: '+(DRIFT.on?'ON':'OFF');try{toast(DRIFT.on?'Modo DRIFT ligado! R$ 1 a cada 10 metros derrapando':'Modo DRIFT desligado','#7db4ff',1600)}catch(_){}};
 b.addEventListener('pointerdown',tg);b.addEventListener('click',e=>e.preventDefault());
 setInterval(()=>{const ok=P.vehicle==='r34'&&!P.onFoot&&!P.inHouse&&(typeof started==='undefined'||started);b.style.display=ok?'flex':'none';if(!ok&&DRIFT.on){DRIFT.on=false;b.classList.remove('on');b.textContent='DRIFT: OFF'}},300)}
function driftTick(dt,sp,slip,drifting,alive){
  const hud=document.getElementById('driftHud');
  if(drifting&&alive&&P.v>8&&slip>.2){
    const m=sp*dt,gain=m*DRIFT_PAY_PER_M;DRIFT.run+=m;DRIFT.acc+=gain;DRIFT.earn+=gain;DRIFT.idle=0;
    const whole=Math.floor(DRIFT.acc);if(whole>0){DRIFT.acc-=whole;P.money+=whole}
    DRIFT.save+=dt;if(DRIFT.save>2){DRIFT.save=0;persist()}
  }else if(DRIFT.run>0){DRIFT.idle+=dt;if(DRIFT.idle>1.6){DRIFT.run=0;DRIFT.earn=0;DRIFT.idle=0;persist()}}
  if(hud){if(DRIFT.run>0){hud.style.display='block';hud.innerHTML='DRIFT '+DRIFT.run.toFixed(1)+' m<small>+R$ '+Math.round(DRIFT.earn).toLocaleString('pt-BR')+'</small>'}else hud.style.display='none'}
}
function stepPlayer(dt,time){
  damageCooldown=Math.max(0,damageCooldown-dt);
  const alive=P.hp>0&&P.fuel>0;
  let acc=0;
  if(alive){if(inp.gas)acc+=(P.hp<30?14:VEHICLES[P.vehicle].acc)*(P.turbo?3.5:(P.tuned?1.45:1))*(P.nitroInstalled&&inp.nitro?1.8:1);if(inp.brake)acc-=P.v>1?42:13}
  P.v+=acc*dt;
  P.v-=P.v*0.32*dt;
  if(!inp.gas&&!inp.brake||!alive)P.v-=Math.sign(P.v)*Math.min(Math.abs(P.v),5*dt);
  if(inp.hand){P.v-=Math.sign(P.v)*Math.min(Math.abs(P.v),(Math.abs(P.v)>12?34:110)*dt);}
  const maxSpeed=P.turbo?277.78:((P.tuned&&P.nitroInstalled&&inp.nitro)?138.89:(P.tuned?Math.max(VEHICLES[P.vehicle].max,125):(P.hp<30?26:VEHICLES[P.vehicle].max)));
  P.v=clamp(P.v,-10,maxSpeed);
  const s=inp.right-inp.left;
  const isR34=P.vehicle==='r34';
  const drifting=isR34&&DRIFT.on;
  P.steer+=(s-P.steer)*Math.min(1,(isR34?22:9)*dt);
  const sp=Math.abs(P.v);
  const turn=P.steer*((isR34?4.2:2.1)/(1+sp*(isR34?0.025:0.05)))*(inp.hand?1.7:1)*(drifting?1.35:1);
  P.h-=turn*dt*Math.sign(P.v)*Math.min(1,sp/3.5);
  // Derrapagem: a direção do movimento acompanha o carro com atraso (maior com o freio de mão).
  if(P.vh===undefined||sp<2.5)P.vh=P.h;else P.vh+=angDiff(P.vh,P.h)*Math.min(1,(inp.hand?1.1:drifting?(sp>25?1.4:3):(sp>25?5.5:14))*(1-wx.rain*.4)*dt);
  const slip=Math.abs(angDiff(P.vh,P.h));P.slip=slip*Math.min(1,sp/20);
  P.v-=P.v*Math.min(1,slip*(drifting?.15:.8))*dt;driftTick(dt,sp,slip,drifting,alive);
  P.x+=Math.sin(P.vh)*P.v*dt;P.z+=Math.cos(P.vh)*P.v*dt;
  P.x=clamp(P.x,LIM_MIN,LIM_MAX);P.z=clamp(P.z,LIM_MIN,LIM_MAX);
  const into=collideBlocks(P,P.vehicle==='suv'?2.8:1.9);
  if(into>0.4){P.v*=1-0.55*clamp(into/(sp+0.1),0,1);hurt(into)}
  P.fuel=Math.max(0,P.fuel-(0.01+sp*0.00325)*dt); // consumo reduzido pela metade para o tanque durar mais
  // traffic collisions
  hitCool=Math.max(0,hitCool-dt);
  for(const ai of cars){
    let dx=P.x-ai.x,dz=P.z-ai.z;const d=Math.hypot(dx,dz),R=3.4;
    if(d<R&&d>0.01){
      dx/=d;dz/=d;const push=R-d;
      P.x+=dx*push*0.7;P.z+=dz*push*0.7;ai.x-=dx*push*0.3;ai.z-=dz*push*0.3;
      const rel=Math.abs(P.v-ai.v*0.4);
      if(rel>4){
        hurt(rel*0.8);P.v*=0.6;ai.v*=0.3;
        if(!ai.police&&rel>8&&hitCool<=0&&P.wanted<3){P.wanted++;hitCool=4;renderStars();toast('Procurado','#ffd23f',1400)}
      }
    }
  }
  // fuel stations
  let atGas=false;
  for(const [ga,gb] of GAS){
    if(Math.hypot(rc(ga)-P.x,rc(gb)-P.z)<7&&Math.abs(P.v)<6){atGas=true}
  }
  if(atGas&&P.fuel<100&&P.money>0){
    const add=Math.min(100-P.fuel,18*dt);P.fuel+=add;refuelAcc+=add*0.6;
    if(refuelAcc>=1){const c=Math.floor(refuelAcc);refuelAcc-=c;P.money=Math.max(0,P.money-c);persist()}
  }
  // mesh
  const g=me.g;g.position.set(P.x,0,P.z);g.rotation.y=P.h;
  g.rotation.z=-P.steer*clamp(sp/40,0,1)*0.04;
  for(const w of me.wheels){w.spin.rotation.x+=P.v*dt/0.38;if(w.front)w.outer.rotation.y=-P.steer*0.45}
  me.tailM.color.setHex(inp.brake||inp.hand?0xff2020:0x7a1010);
  prevCrash=Math.max(0,prevCrash-dt);
}

/* ---------- police logic ---------- */
let _polT=0;
function ensurePolice(dt){_polT-=dt;const pol=cars.filter(a=>a.police);
  if(P.wanted>0){if(_polT<=0&&pol.length<Math.min(9,3+P.wanted*2)){_polT=1;const a=spawnAI(true);if(!policeNPC)a.temp=true}}
  else if(!policeNPC){for(const a of pol)if(a.temp)removeAI(a)}}
function addWanted(n,max){if(seizeImmune(PLAYER_ID))return;if(P.wanted>=(max||3))return;P.wanted=Math.min(max||3,P.wanted+n);evade=0;renderStars();toast('Procurado','#ffd23f',1200)}
function stepWanted(dt){
  if(seizeImmune(PLAYER_ID)&&P.wanted>0){P.wanted=0;evade=0;bust=0;renderStars()}
  policeCool=0;ensurePolice(dt);
  let near=999;
  for(const ai of cars)if(ai.police){const d=Math.hypot(ai.x-P.x,ai.z-P.z);if(d<near)near=d}
  if(P.wanted>0&&!seizeImmune(PLAYER_ID)){
    if(near<40||cityCanAccess())evade=0;else evade+=dt;
    if(evade>=14){
      evade=0;P.wanted=Math.max(0,P.wanted-1);renderStars();
      if(P.wanted===0)toast('Nível de procurado encerrado','#7dff9b',1800);
    }
    // Polícia NPC encostada no jogador, quase parada, faz a abordagem.
    if(near<22&&Math.abs(P.v)<112&&jailTime<=0&&!P.god&&!(P.flying&&P.flyY>2)){
      bust+=dt;
      if(bust>=0.8){
        bust=0;P.v=0;
        const fine=Math.min(Math.max(0,P.money),200*P.wanted);if(fine>0)addMoney(-fine,'Multa de prisão');
        const sec=60+P.wanted*30;P.wanted=0;renderStars();enterPrison(sec);try{seizeMine(String(PLAYER_ID),'prisão pela polícia')}catch(e){}
      }
    }else bust=Math.max(0,bust-dt*2);
  }else{evade=0;bust=0}
  return near;
}

/* ---------- main loop ---------- */
let perfT=0,perfN=0;
let started=false,clock=9,last=performance.now(),hudT=0,mapT=0,frame=0;
function tick(now){
  requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  const raw=now-last;const dt=Math.min(0.033,raw/1000);last=now;const time=now/1000;
  let nightF;
  if(!started){
    nightF=applyTime(clock);placeSun(SPAWN.x,SPAWN.z);
    const a=time*0.12;camera.position.set(SPAWN.x+Math.sin(a)*26,9,SPAWN.z+Math.cos(a)*26);camera.lookAt(SPAWN.x,2,SPAWN.z);skyMesh.position.copy(camera.position);
    me.g.position.set(SPAWN.x,0,SPAWN.z);
    renderer.render(scene,camera);return;
  }
  if(!P.freezeTime)clock=(clock+dt*(P.timeScale||1)*((clock>=6&&clock<19)?(24/3000):(24/600)))%24;
  perfT+=raw;perfN++;
  if(perfN>=150){const avg=perfT/perfN;perfT=0;perfN=0;
    if(avg>40&&QUALITY!=='med'){const q=QUALITY==='4k'?'high':'med';setQuality(q);toast('Qualidade ajustada: '+(q==='high'?'Alta':'Média'),'#ffb347',2200);syncQ()}}
  nightF=applyTime(clock);
  spot.intensity=nightF*3.2*(1+wx.rain*.3);
  if(P.god){P.hp=100;P.fuel=100}
  stepPrison(dt);jailTaskCooldown=Math.max(0,jailTaskCooldown-dt);
  if(policeRole&&jailTime<=0&&!policeSuspended(PLAYER_ID)){policePayTimer+=dt;if(policePayTimer>=600){policePayTimer-=600;const sal=policeSalary(PLAYER_ID);addMoney(sal,'Salário policial');if(!phone.hidden)syncPhone();toast('Salário policial +R$ '+sal.toLocaleString('pt-BR'),'#7dff9b',2200)}}
  if(shopOpen)stepShop(dt,time);else if(P.onFoot)stepFoot(dt,time);else stepPlayer(dt,time);
  placeSun(P.x,P.z);try{addonStep(dt,time)}catch(e){}
  for(const ai of cars.slice())stepAI(ai,dt,time);updateShotEffects(dt);
  const near=stepWanted(dt);
  stepJob(dt);stepGps();stepPeds(dt,time);updateGear(dt);stepWeather(dt,time);
  if(P.house){rentTimer+=dt;if(rentTimer>=120){rentTimer-=120;addMoney(250,'Renda do imóvel');if(!phone.hidden)syncPhone();toast('Renda do imóvel +$250','#7dff9b',1600)}}
  if((frame%180)===0)persist();
  // beam pulse
  const pulse=0.28+0.08*Math.sin(time*3);beamJob.material.opacity=pulse;beamGps.material.opacity=pulse;
  updateCamera(dt);skyMesh.position.copy(camera.position);
  // broken states
  if(P.hp<=0&&!P._warnedHp){P._warnedHp=1;toast('Carro quebrado. Repare no celular','#ff3d81',3000)}
  if(P.hp>0)P._warnedHp=0;
  if(P.fuel<=0&&!P._warnedFuel){P._warnedFuel=1;toast('Sem combustível. Vá a um posto','#ffb347',3000)}
  if(P.fuel>0)P._warnedFuel=0;
  // HUD
  hudT+=dt;
  if(hudT>0.1){
    hudT=0;
    const hh=Math.floor(clock),mm=Math.floor((clock%1)*60);
    const ts=String(hh).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(mm).padStart(2,'0');
    el.clock.textContent=ts+' '+wxIcon();el.pt.textContent=ts;
    el.money.textContent=fmt(P.money);{const n=nearStore();if(n!==lastNear){lastNear=n;setLabels()}}{const hs=houseNearby();if(hs!==lastHS){lastHS=hs;setLabels()}}
    el.kmh.textContent=Math.round(Math.abs((P.heliD||P.planeD)?(P.hv||0):P.v)*3.6);{const sm=document.querySelector('#speed small');if(sm)sm.textContent=P.onFoot?'KM/H':'KM/H · '+curGear+'ª'}
    el.fuel.style.transform='scaleX('+clamp(P.fuel/100,0,1)+')';
    el.hp.style.transform='scaleX('+clamp(P.hp/100,0,1)+')';
    el.hp.style.background=P.hp<30?'#ff4d4d':'#58e07a';
    const dn=districtAt(P.x,P.z);
    if(dn!==lastDistrict){lastDistrict=dn;districtT=4;el.district.textContent=dn}
    if(districtT>0){districtT-=0.1;el.district.style.opacity=districtT>0?1:0}
    if(!phone.hidden)$('#bankBal').textContent=fmt(P.money);
  }
  if((frame++&1)===0)drawMap();
  updateAudio(time,near);
  renderer.render(scene,camera);
}
/* ---------- joystick + camera drag (on foot) ---------- */
const toUI=(dx,dy)=>innerHeight>innerWidth?[dy,-dx]:[dx,dy];   // the page is rotated in portrait
const joyEl=$('#joy'),knob=$('#knob'),joy={x:0,y:0,id:null};
function joyMove(e){
  const r=joyEl.getBoundingClientRect();
  let [ux,uy]=toUI(e.clientX-(r.left+r.width/2),e.clientY-(r.top+r.height/2));
  const m=Math.hypot(ux,uy),R=46;if(m>R){ux*=R/m;uy*=R/m}
  knob.style.transform='translate('+ux+'px,'+uy+'px)';joy.x=ux/R;joy.y=-uy/R;
}
function joyEnd(e){if(e.pointerId!==joy.id)return;joy.id=null;joy.x=joy.y=0;knob.style.transform='none'}
joyEl.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{e.preventDefault();joy.id=e.pointerId;try{joyEl.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId)}catch(_){}joyMove(e)});
joyEl.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(e.pointerId===joy.id)joyMove(e)});
joyEl.addEventListener('pointerup',joyEnd);joyEl.addEventListener('pointercancel',joyEnd);
let drag=null;
canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(P.onFoot){drag={id:e.pointerId,x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};try{canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId)}catch(_){}}});
canvas.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
  if(!drag||e.pointerId!==drag.id)return;
  const por=innerHeight>innerWidth,d=por?e.clientY-drag.y:e.clientX-drag.x,dv=por?e.clientX-drag.x:e.clientY-drag.y;drag.x=e.clientX;drag.y=e.clientY;
  // trava o eixo: o primeiro movimento do dedo decide se é só para o lado ou só para cima/baixo
  drag.ad=(drag.ad||0)+d;drag.av=(drag.av||0)+dv;
  if(!drag.ax){if(Math.abs(drag.ad)<6&&Math.abs(drag.av)<6)return;drag.ax=Math.abs(drag.ad)>=Math.abs(drag.av)?'h':'v';camYaw-=(drag.ax==='h'?drag.ad:0)*0.006;camPitch=Math.max(-.35,Math.min(1.15,camPitch+(drag.ax==='v'?drag.av:0)*0.005));return}
  if(drag.ax==='h')camYaw-=d*0.006;else camPitch=Math.max(-.35,Math.min(1.15,camPitch+dv*0.005));
});
const dragEnd=e=>{if(drag&&e.pointerId===drag.id)drag=null};
canvas.addEventListener('pointerup',dragEnd);canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel',dragEnd);

/* ---------- ADM commands ---------- */
const TP={centro:[rc(3),rc(3)],posto:[rc(GAS[0][0]),rc(GAS[0][1])],garagem:[SPAWN.x,SPAWN.z],porto:[rc(6),rc(6)],praia:[rc(-1),rc(-1)],serra:[rc(7),rc(0)],loja:[STORE.x-3,STORE.z]};
function teleport(x,z){
  P.sitting=null;P.x=x;P.z=z;P.v=0;
  if(P.onFoot){parked.x=x-2.5;parked.z=z;parked.h=P.h;me.g.position.set(parked.x,0,parked.z);me.g.rotation.y=parked.h}
  camH=P.h;camYaw=P.h;
}

// Animação da dourada no slot de arma (toque no slot ou tecla 1)
const _animOK=()=>!!(P.onFoot&&!P.inHouse&&!P.sitting&&!P.flying&&jailTime<=0);
{const _tg=toggleGun;
 toggleGun=function(){
   if(gunKind==='gold'&&gunOut&&_animOK()&&LOOK&&LOOK.foto){if(holsterT<=0&&drawT<=0){holsterT=.9;holsterDo=true}return}
   const was=gunOut,r=_tg.apply(this,arguments);
   if(gunKind==='gold'&&!was&&gunOut&&_animOK())drawT=.6;return r}}
function runCmd(raw){
  if(jailTime>0&&['voar','jetpack','tp','invisivel'].includes(raw.trim().replace(/^\//,'').toLowerCase().split(/\s+/)[0]))return 'Função bloqueada durante a prisão';
  const parts=raw.trim().replace(/^\//,'').toLowerCase().split(/\s+/),c=parts[0],a=parts.slice(1),n=parseFloat(a[0]);
  if(admLimited()){admAudit('Comando: /'+raw.trim().replace(/^\//,'').slice(0,100));if(['dinheiro','ban','banir','desbanir','unban'].includes(c))return 'Sem permissão: ADM da dourada não pode usar dinheiro ou banir'}
  switch(c){
    case 'ajuda':return 'Comandos: dinheiro N, reparar, abastecer, full, deus, turbo, voar, jetpack, invisivel, procurado 0-5, policia, policianpc on|off, pedestres N, clima limpo|nublado|chuva|tempestade|auto, transito N, hora 0-23, congelar, tp local ou tp X Z, posicao, roupas, pele RRGGBB, resetvisual, anuncio texto, nevoa, fov, exposicao, saturacao, vinheta, filtro, hud, minimapa, setdinheiro, combustivel, vida, camera, tempo, trazercarro, irparacarro, pararcarro, boost, qualidade, salvar, spawn, cor RRGGBB, reset';
    case 'dinheiro':{if(isNaN(n))return 'Uso: /dinheiro 5000';addMoney(Math.round(n),'ADM');syncPhone();return 'Saldo: '+fmt(P.money)}
    case 'reparar':P.hp=100;return 'Carro reparado';
    case 'abastecer':P.fuel=100;return 'Tanque cheio';
    case 'deus':
      P.god=!P.god;
      if(P.god){P.hp=100;P.fuel=100;P.wanted=0;renderStars()}
      return 'Modo Deus '+(P.god?'ATIVADO — veículo protegido e combustível infinito':'desativado');
    case 'turbo':if(!admOk)return 'Apenas o ADM pode ativar o turbo';P.turbo=!P.turbo;return P.turbo?'TURBO ADM ATIVADO — carro até 1.000 km/h · helicóptero até 650 km/h':'Turbo ADM desativado';
    case 'armadura':
      if(!admOk)return 'Apenas o ADM pode usar a armadura tech';
      return suitToggle();
    case 'heroi':
      if(!admOk)return 'Apenas o ADM pode usar o modo super-herói';
      if(!P.onFoot)return 'Saia do carro para ativar o super-herói';
      if(P.inHouse)return 'Saia de casa para ativar o super-herói';
      P.hero=!P.hero;P.flying=P.hero;
      $('#flyUp').classList.toggle('show',P.flying);$('#flyDown').classList.toggle('show',P.flying);
      return P.hero?'SUPER-HERÓI ATIVADO: voo e supervelocidade! Use SUBIR/DESCER.':'Super-herói desativado';
    case 'voar':
    case 'jetpack':
      if(!P.onFoot)return 'Saia do carro para voar';
      if(P.inHouse)return 'Saia de casa para ativar o voo';
      P.flying=!P.flying;
      $('#flyUp').classList.toggle('show',P.flying);$('#flyDown').classList.toggle('show',P.flying);
      return (c==='jetpack'?'Jetpack ':'Voo GTA ')+(P.flying?'ativado — mova pelo joystick e use SUBIR/DESCER':'desativado');
    case 'invisivel':P.invisible=!P.invisible;ped.g.visible=P.onFoot&&!P.invisible;return 'Invisibilidade '+(P.invisible?'ativada':'desativada');
    case 'procurado':{
      if(isNaN(n))return 'Uso: /procurado 0 a 5';
      P.wanted=clamp(Math.round(n),0,5);renderStars();
      
      return 'Procurado: '+P.wanted}
    case 'policianpc':{const on=a[0]!=='off';setPoliceNPC(on);return 'Polícia NPC '+(on?'ligada (4 viaturas)':'desligada')}
    case 'clima':{
      if(a[0]==='auto'){wx.auto=true;wx.next=5;return 'Clima automático'}
      const m={limpo:'clear',sol:'clear',nublado:'cloud',chuva:'rain',tempestade:'storm'}[a[0]];
      if(!m)return 'Uso: /clima limpo, nublado, chuva, tempestade ou auto';
      wx.auto=false;setWeather(m);return 'Clima: '+a[0]+' (automático desligado)'}
    case 'pedestres':{const k=isNaN(n)?28:clamp(Math.round(n),0,80);setPedCount(k);return k+' pedestres'}
    case 'policia':return 'A polícia é formada por jogadores. Abra o celular > Polícia e faça o recrutamento para entrar na equipe.'
    case 'transito':{
      const k=isNaN(n)?8:Math.round(n);
      if(k<=0){for(const ai of cars.slice())if(!ai.police)removeAI(ai);return 'Trânsito removido'}
      const add=Math.max(0,Math.min(k,40-cars.filter(x=>!x.police).length));
      for(let i=0;i<add;i++)spawnAI(false);return add+' carros adicionados'}
    case 'hora':{if(isNaN(n)||n<0||n>=24)return 'Uso: /hora 0 a 23';clock=n;return 'Hora: '+n+'h'}
    case 'tp':{if(a.length>=2&&!isNaN(parseFloat(a[0]))&&!isNaN(parseFloat(a[1]))){teleport(parseFloat(a[0]),parseFloat(a[1]));return 'Teleportado: '+a[0]+', '+a[1]}const t=TP[a[0]];if(!t)return 'Locais: '+Object.keys(TP).join(', ');teleport(t[0],t[1]);return 'Teleportado: '+a[0]}

}

}
